`README.md`:

```md
# JSA Client

Single-player and **open LAN** toolkit for Minecraft Java (**1.6.4 through 26.3**).

Movement QoL, visuals, and brother-friendly pranks. **Not** an anti-cheat bypass client.

Meteor modules and addon jars are not bundled here. They require a separate [Meteor Client](https://meteorclient.com/) install; JSA only lists and toggles them in ClickGUI when Meteor is present.

## Modules

| Path | Role |
|------|------|
| `core` | Version-agnostic engine + all modules + era matrix |
| `fabric` | Fabric mod adapter (pinned version, default 1.20.1) |
| `loader` | Reflective multi-version adapter for any MC jar |

See **[VERSIONS.md](VERSIONS.md)** for era wiring, launch commands, and capability honesty.

## Quick start

```bash
# Capability matrix (no Minecraft needed)
./gradlew :loader:jar
java -cp loader/build/libs/jsa-loader-1.0.0.jar dev.jsa.client.loader.LoaderMain --matrix

# Unit smoke tests
./gradlew :core:test

# Fabric mod (1.20.1 by default)
./gradlew :fabric:build
```

Launch a specific vanilla/Prism version with the loader:

```bash
java -Djsa.mc.version=1.12.2 -cp "jsa-loader-1.0.0.jar;<mc jars>" dev.jsa.client.loader.LoaderMain
```

## Pranks

Category **Pranks** (`module.troll`) — 19 LAN-safe joke modules. Details in prior notes / ClickGUI.

```

`VERSIONS.md`:

```md
# Minecraft version support (1.6.4 – 26.3)

JSA is an **SP / open-LAN** toolkit. One reflective **loader** covers the whole
window. The **fabric** module is an optional polished adapter pinned to one
version (default **1.20.1**).

Supported range (inclusive): **`1.6.4` … `26.3`**.

| Path | Role |
|------|------|
| `core` | Version-agnostic modules + era/capability matrix |
| `loader` | Reflective adapter for **any** MC client jar on the classpath |
| `fabric` | Fabric mod for a single pinned version |

No anti-cheat bypass is included.

---

## Eras actually wired

| Era | Versions | Adapter wiring | Notes |
|-----|----------|----------------|-------|
| Legacy | 1.6.4 | MCP `Minecraft` / `EntityClientPlayerMP` | No offhand/elytra/cooldown |
| 1.7.x | 1.7.0–1.7.10 | MCP client + resource packs | Same capability tier as late 1.6 for most modules |
| 1.8.x | 1.8.0–1.8.9 | `EntityPlayerSP`, creative flight flag | Pre-combat-update |
| Combat | 1.9–1.12.2 | EnumHand / offhand / elytra | AutoTotem needs 1.11+ |
| Flattening | 1.13.x | String IDs, swim | XRay presets use names |
| Yarn client | 1.14–1.15.2 | `MinecraftClient` | |
| Nether | 1.16.x | | |
| Caves Pt1 | 1.17.x | `world.level.*` packages, Java 16+ | |
| Tall world | 1.18.x | height -64..320, Java 17+ | |
| Wild | 1.19.x | | |
| Trails | 1.20.x | Fabric default pin 1.20.1 | |
| Trials | 1.21–1.25.x | | |
| Classic 1.26+ | 1.26–1.99 | Same modern reflective path | |
| Calendar | 26.0–26.3 | Post-`1.` numbering | Treated newer than any `1.x` |

**What is shared (not one jar per patch):** `ReflectivePlatformAdapter` +
`McClassIndex` era maps + `VersionCapabilities`. Modules degrade via
`ModuleGates` when a feature does not exist (e.g. ElytraFly on 1.8).

**What still needs a hand-written deep bind (optional polish):**

- Full live packet send/receive for every obfuscation mapping set (loader uses
  best-effort reflection; Fabric mock/real wiring is the polished path).
- Render ESP/Tracers GL sink (adapter must install `RenderEvent` sink).
- Per-version mixin sets if you want Fabric jars for *every* release — today
  only one Fabric pin is built; use **loader** for other versions.

---

## How to launch a given version

### A) Loader (any version in range)

1. Build:

```bash
./gradlew :loader:jar
```

Output: `loader/build/libs/jsa-loader-1.0.0.jar`

2. Put **that version’s** Minecraft client jar + libraries on the classpath
   (Prism/MultiMC instance `libraries` + `minecraft.jar`, or official launcher
   natives/libs).

3. Optionally force the version string (recommended when `version.json` is missing):

```bash
# cmd.exe
java -Djsa.mc.version=1.12.2 ^
  -cp "jsa-loader-1.0.0.jar;path\to\minecraft.jar;path\to\libraries\*" ^
  dev.jsa.client.loader.LoaderMain

# PowerShell (quote -D props)
java "-Djsa.mc.version=1.12.2" -cp "jsa-loader-1.0.0.jar;..." dev.jsa.client.loader.LoaderMain
```

Examples:

| Target | Property |
|--------|----------|
| 1.6.4 | `-Djsa.mc.version=1.6.4` |
| 1.8.9 | `-Djsa.mc.version=1.8.9` |
| 1.16.5 | `-Djsa.mc.version=1.16.5` |
| 1.20.1 | `-Djsa.mc.version=1.20.1` |
| 1.21.3 | `-Djsa.mc.version=1.21.3` |
| 26.3 | `-Djsa.mc.version=26.3` |

Print the capability matrix without Minecraft:

```bash
java -cp jsa-loader-1.0.0.jar dev.jsa.client.loader.LoaderMain --matrix
```

### B) Fabric (pinned)

Default pin is **1.20.1**. To retarget:

1. Edit `gradle.properties` (`minecraft_version`, yarn, fabric-api).
2. `./gradlew :fabric:build`
3. Drop the mod jar into that version’s `mods/` folder.

---

## Capability honesty

Modules consult `Versions.caps()`:

- **ElytraFly** → requires 1.9+
- **AutoTotem** → requires 1.11+ (and offhand)
- **Hold** (attack cooldown) → no-op before 1.9
- Older clients still get Fly/Speed/ESP/Pranks that only need basic position APIs

Run unit smoke tests:

```bash
./gradlew :core:test
```

`core/build.gradle`:

```gradle
// JSA Client `core` — version-agnostic modular engine for SP / open LAN.
// No Minecraft deps: modules talk through Platform abstractions only.
plugins {
    id 'java-library'
}

tasks.withType(JavaCompile).configureEach {
    options.encoding = 'UTF-8'
}

java {
    toolchain {
        languageVersion = JavaLanguageVersion.of(17)
    }
    withSourcesJar()
}

repositories {
    mavenCentral()
}

dependencies {
    // Config (de)serialization. `api` so the fabric adapter can reference the
    // config types without redeclaring the dependency.
    api 'com.google.code.gson:gson:2.10.1'

    testImplementation 'org.junit.jupiter:junit-jupiter:5.10.2'
    testRuntimeOnly 'org.junit.platform:junit-platform-launcher'
}

tasks.named('test', Test) {
    useJUnitPlatform()
}

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/JsaClientLoader.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core;

import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ModuleFactory;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Platform;
import dev.jsa.client.core.version.VersionProbe;
import dev.jsa.client.core.version.Versions;

/**
 * Adapter hook. Set {@link MC} first, then call {@link #boot()} once.
 * Boots the single-player / open-LAN module bundle via {@link ModuleFactory}.
 * Works for Fabric (one version) and the reflective multi-version loader.
 */
public final class JsaClientLoader {
    private JsaClientLoader() {}

    /** @return the platform the adapter installed (must be non-null). */
    public static Platform boot() {
        Platform p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Adapter must call MC.set(...) before JsaClientLoader.boot()");
        }
        // Prefer adapter-reported version; fall back to classpath probe.
        String raw = p.getMinecraftVersion();
        if (raw == null || raw.isBlank() || "unknown".equalsIgnoreCase(raw)) {
            Versions.install(VersionProbe.probe(p.getAdapterId()));
        } else {
            Versions.install(VersionProbe.parse(raw, p.getAdapterId()));
        }
        ModuleFactory.initialize();
        return p;
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/MC.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core;

import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Platform;

/**
 * Central handle for the active {@link Platform}. The adapter calls
 * {@link #set(Platform)} once at init; every module reads the world through
 * {@link #get()}.
 */
public final class MC {
    private static Platform platform;

    private MC() {}

    /** @return the active platform adapter, or null before init. */
    public static Platform get() { return platform; }

    /** Adapter calls this exactly once at startup. */
    public static void set(Platform p) { platform = p; }

    /** @return true when a platform is installed and a world is loaded. */
    public static boolean ready() {
        return platform != null && platform.inGame();
    }
}

public final class AttackEvent extends Event {

    public AttackEvent(Entity target) { this.target = target; }
    public Entity getTarget() { return target; }
}

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/event/Event.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.event;

/**
 * Base event. Modules mostly receive direct dispatch from the ModuleManager
 * (tick/render/motion), but packet events carry payload and travel through both
 * the manager and the {@link EventBus}.
 */
public class Event {
    private boolean cancelled;

    public boolean isCancelled() { return cancelled; }
    public void cancel() { cancelled = true; }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/event/EventBus.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.event;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

/**
 * Tiny synchronous pub/sub bus for non-module listeners (GUI, HUD, commands).
 * Module dispatch goes through ModuleManager instead.
 */
public final class EventBus {
    private static final EventBus INSTANCE = new EventBus();

    private final Map<Class<?>, CopyOnWriteArrayList<Consumer<?>>> listeners = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public static EventBus get() { return INSTANCE; }

    private EventBus() {}

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    public <T extends Event> void subscribe(Class<T> type, Consumer<T> handler) {
        listeners.computeIfAbsent(type, k -> new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>()).add((Consumer<?>) handler);
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    public <T extends Event> void unsubscribe(Class<T> type, Consumer<T> handler) {
        CopyOnWriteArrayList<Consumer<?>> list = listeners.get(type);
        if (list != null) list.remove((Consumer<?>) handler);
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    public <T extends Event> void post(T event) {
        CopyOnWriteArrayList<Consumer<?>> list = listeners.get(event.getClass());
        if (list != null) {
            for (Consumer<?> c : list) ((Consumer<T>) c).accept(event);
        }
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/event/MotionEvent.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.event;

/**
 * Fired once per tick after the client computed motion, right before the
 * position packet goes out. Movement modules mutate motion/position here.
 */
public final class MotionEvent extends Event {
    private static final MotionEvent INSTANCE = new MotionEvent();

    public static MotionEvent get() { return INSTANCE; }
    private MotionEvent() {}
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/event/PacketEvent.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.event;

import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Packet;

/**
 * Fired for every packet send (client->server) or receive (server->client).
 * Cancelling a send prevents the vanilla send; cancelling a receive hides the
 * packet from the client (used by Velocity, NoRender, troll sound-steal, ...).
 */
public final class PacketEvent extends Event {
    private final Packet packet;

    public PacketEvent(Packet packet) {
        this.packet = packet;
    }

    public Packet getPacket() { return packet; }
    public boolean toServer() { return packet.toServer(); }

    /** @return true if the carried packet's type name matches {@code type} (case-insensitive). */
    public boolean isType(String type) {
        return packet.typeName() != null && packet.typeName().equalsIgnoreCase(type);
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/event/RenderEvent.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.event;

import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Entity;

/**
 * Fired once per frame for HUD, and again during world render for ESP / tracers.
 * The adapter installs a {@link RenderSink} that turns draw calls into
 * DrawContext primitives or world-space lines.
 *
 * <p>Null-safe: with no sink the draw calls are no-ops.</p>
 */
public final class RenderEvent extends Event {
    private static final RenderEvent INSTANCE = new RenderEvent();

    public static RenderEvent get() { return INSTANCE; }

    /** GL / HUD primitive sink installed by the adapter. */
    public interface RenderSink {
        void box(Entity e, int argb);
        void healthBar(Entity e, float fraction);
        void line(Entity e, int argb);
        void text(int x, int y, String s, int argb);
        void fill(int x, int y, int w, int h, int argb);
        void outline(int x, int y, int w, int h, int argb);
        int width();
        int height();

        /** Short label near an entity box. HUD sinks ignore this. */
        default void label(Entity e, String text, int argb) {}
    }

    private RenderSink sink;
    private boolean worldPass;
    private int fallbackW = 854;
    private int fallbackH = 480;

    /** Adapter calls this once at render init (or null to detach). */
    public void setSink(RenderSink sink) { this.sink = sink; }

    /**
     * True while the world renderer is collecting boxes and lines.
     * HUD modules skip this pass so animations and FPS are not doubled.
     */
    public void setWorldPass(boolean worldPass) { this.worldPass = worldPass; }

    public boolean isWorldPass() { return worldPass; }

    public void setFallbackSize(int w, int h) {
        this.fallbackW = Math.max(1, w);
        this.fallbackH = Math.max(1, h);
    }

    private RenderEvent() {}

    public void drawBox(Entity e, int argb) { if (sink != null) sink.box(e, argb); }
    public void drawHealthBar(Entity e, float fraction) { if (sink != null) sink.healthBar(e, fraction); }
    public void drawLine(Entity e, int argb) { if (sink != null) sink.line(e, argb); }

    /** Name or distance drawn next to a world box, not as a HUD list. */
    public void drawLabel(Entity e, String text, int argb) {
        if (sink != null && e != null && text != null && !text.isEmpty()) sink.label(e, text, argb);
    }

    public void drawText(int x, int y, String s) { drawText(x, y, s, 0xFFE8EEF7); }

    public void drawText(int x, int y, String s, int argb) {
        if (sink != null) sink.text(x, y, s, argb);
    }

    public void fill(int x, int y, int w, int h, int argb) {
        if (sink != null) sink.fill(x, y, w, h, argb);
    }

    public void outline(int x, int y, int w, int h, int argb) {
        if (sink != null) sink.outline(x, y, w, h, argb);
    }

    public int width() { return sink != null ? sink.width() : fallbackW; }
    public int height() { return sink != null ? sink.height() : fallbackH; }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/gui/Anim.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.gui;

/**
 * Small frame-rate independent fades for the ClickGUI and HUD.
 * No extra dependencies; both screens share the same easing.
 */
public final class Anim {
    private Anim() {}

    /**
     * Seconds since the last call on this clock. The first call returns one
     * frame. A stall is capped so an animation cannot jump to the end.
     */
    public static float seconds(long[] clock) {
        long now = System.nanoTime();
        if (clock[0] == 0L) {
            clock[0] = now;
            return 1f / 60f;
        }
        float dt = (now - clock[0]) / 1_000_000_000f;
        clock[0] = now;
        if (dt < 0.001f) return 0.001f;
        if (dt > 0.05f) return 0.05f;
        return dt;
    }

    public static float approach(float current, float target, float dt, float speed) {
        if (Math.abs(target - current) <= 0.001f) return target;
        float t = 1f - (float) Math.exp(-speed * dt);
        return current + (target - current) * t;
    }

    public static double approach(double current, double target, float dt, float speed) {
        if (Math.abs(target - current) <= 0.0005d) return target;
        double t = 1.0 - Math.exp(-speed * dt);
        return current + (target - current) * t;
    }

    public static float clamp01(float t) {
        if (t <= 0f) return 0f;
        if (t >= 1f) return 1f;
        return t;
    }

    public static float easeOut(float t) {
        t = clamp01(t);
        float u = 1f - t;
        return 1f - u * u * u;
    }

    /** Multiplies the color's existing alpha. RGB stays intact. */
    public static int withAlpha(int argb, float opacity) {
        float o = clamp01(opacity);
        int a = (argb >>> 24) & 0xFF;
        int out = Math.round(a * o);
        if (out <= 0) return argb & 0x00FFFFFF;
        if (out >= 255) return (0xFF << 24) | (argb & 0x00FFFFFF);
        return (out << 24) | (argb & 0x00FFFFFF);
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/gui/ClickGui.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.gui;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.event.RenderEvent;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.AbstractModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.Category;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.Module;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Platform;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ColorSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.Setting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.StringSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.version.Versions;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.EnumMap;
import java.util.IdentityHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Category-column ClickGUI for the SP / open-LAN toolkit.
 * Opened as a real screen (Insert, Right Shift, or Right Ctrl). ESC closes.
 * Left-click toggles; right-click expands settings. Panels slide and fade,
 * rows highlight under the cursor, and settings grow open instead of popping.
 */
public final class ClickGui {
    private static final ClickGui INSTANCE = new ClickGui();

    public static ClickGui get() { return INSTANCE; }

    private static final float SLIDE_X = -16f;
    private static final float SLIDE_Y = -12f;

    private boolean open;
    /** True while a real Minecraft screen owns mouse + keyboard. */
    private boolean screenHosted;
    /** Close was requested; the screen stays up until {@link #openAnim} finishes. */
    private boolean closing;
    private float openAnim;
    private boolean wasToggleKey;
    private final long[] clock = new long[1];
    private final Map<Category, Panel> panels = new EnumMap<>(Category.class);
    private final Map<Module, Float> expand = new IdentityHashMap<>();
    private final Map<Object, Float> hover = new IdentityHashMap<>();
    private Module expanded;
    private Panel dragging;
    private int dragOx, dragOy;
    private int pointerX, pointerY;
    private boolean pointerValid;
    private float frameDt;

    private ClickGui() {
        Category[] cats = {
                Category.COMBAT, Category.MOVEMENT, Category.PLAYER,
                Category.RENDER, Category.TROLL, Category.MISC, Category.METEOR
        };
        int x = GuiTheme.PAD;
        for (int i = 0; i < cats.length; i++) {
            panels.put(cats[i], new Panel(cats[i], x, GuiTheme.PAD + 28));
            x += GuiTheme.PANEL_W + GuiTheme.GAP;
        }
    }

    public boolean isOpen() { return open; }

    public boolean isClosing() { return closing; }

    public void setOpen(boolean open) {
        if (open) {
            beginOpen();
            return;
        }
        if (screenHosted) {
            requestClose();
            return;
        }
        this.open = false;
        this.closing = false;
        this.openAnim = 0f;
    }

    public boolean isScreenHosted() { return screenHosted; }

    public void setScreenHosted(boolean screenHosted) { this.screenHosted = screenHosted; }

    /** Screen opened. Mouse stays unlocked because the screen owns the cursor. */
    public void attachScreen() {
        beginOpen();
        screenHosted = true;
    }

    /** Reverse a close, or show the GUI. Does not grab the mouse by itself. */
    public void beginOpen() {
        open = true;
        closing = false;
    }

    /** Fade out. The screen closes itself once {@link #finishedClosing()} is true. */
    public void requestClose() {
        if (!open) return;
        closing = true;
    }

    public boolean finishedClosing() {
        return closing && openAnim <= 0.02f;
    }

    /** Screen is gone. Snap hidden so the HUD pass does not keep drawing it. */
    public void onScreenRemoved() {
        screenHosted = false;
        open = false;
        closing = false;
        openAnim = 0f;
        pointerValid = false;
        dragging = null;
        clock[0] = 0L;
    }

    public void toggle() {
        if (open && !closing) requestClose();
        else beginOpen();
    }

    /** Scaled cursor from the screen. Clicks use the same coordinates. */
    public void setPointer(int x, int y) {
        pointerX = x;
        pointerY = y;
        pointerValid = true;
    }

    /**
     * Screen-driven click. {@code button} 0 = left (toggle / slider), 1 = right (settings).
     */
    public void click(int mx, int my, int button) {
        if (!open || openAnim < 0.08f) return;
        boolean left = button == 0;
        boolean right = button == 1;
        if (!left && !right) return;
        for (Hit hit : layout()) {
            if (hit.reveal < 0.2f) continue;
            if (!contains(mx, my, hit.x, hit.y, hit.w, hit.h)) continue;
            if (hit.header) {
                if (left) {
                    dragging = hit.panel;
                    dragOx = mx - hit.panel.x;
                    dragOy = my - hit.panel.y;
                    hit.panel.userMoved = true;
                }
                if (right) hit.panel.collapsed = !hit.panel.collapsed;
                return;
            }
            if (hit.setting != null) {
                if (left) interactSetting(hit.setting, mx, hit.x);
                if (right && hit.setting instanceof NumberSetting n) n.reset();
                return;
            }
            if (hit.module != null) {
                if (left) ModuleManager.get().toggle(hit.module);
                if (right) expanded = (expanded == hit.module) ? null : hit.module;
            }
            return;
        }
    }

    /** Move a panel that was grabbed by its header. */
    public void drag(int mx, int my) {
        if (dragging == null) return;
        dragging.x = mx - dragOx;
        dragging.y = my - dragOy;
        dragging.userMoved = true;
    }

    public void release() { dragging = null; }

    /** Fallback key edge only. Mouse input comes from the screen, never GLFW polling. */
    public void tick() {
        if (screenHosted) return;
        Platform p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        boolean toggleKey = p.isGuiToggleKeyDown();
        if (toggleKey && !wasToggleKey) toggle();
        wasToggleKey = toggleKey;
    }

    private void interactSetting(Setting<?> s, int mx, int panelX) {
        if (s instanceof BoolSetting b) {
            b.toggle();
        } else if (s instanceof ModeSetting m) {
            m.cycle();
        } else if (s instanceof NumberSetting n) {
            double t = (mx - (panelX + 4.0)) / (GuiTheme.PANEL_W - 8.0);
            t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t));
            n.set(n.min() + t * (n.max() - n.min()));
        } else if (s instanceof StringSetting str) {
            // cycle a few presets if empty interaction
            str.setValue(str.getValue());
        } else if (s instanceof ColorSetting c) {
            int rgb = c.get() & 0x00FFFFFF;
            int next = ((rgb * 3 + 0x2A4F8C) & 0x00FFFFFF) | 0xFF000000;
            c.setValue(next);
        }
    }

    /** Draw after module HUD so the GUI sits on top. */
    public void render(RenderEvent e) {
        if (!open && !closing && openAnim <= 0.001f) {
            clock[0] = 0L;
            return;
        }
        float dt = Anim.seconds(clock);
        step(dt);
        if (!screenHosted && closing && openAnim <= 0.02f) {
            open = false;
            closing = false;
            openAnim = 0f;
            return;
        }
        frameDt = dt;

        int mx = pointerX;
        int my = pointerY;
        if (!pointerValid) {
            Platform p = MC.get();
            mx = p == null ? -9999 : p.getMouseX();
            my = p == null ? -9999 : p.getMouseY();
        }

        float fade = openAnim;
        if (fade > 0.01f) {
            e.fill(0, 0, e.width(), e.height(), Anim.withAlpha(0x66000000, fade));
            int barY = Math.round((1f - Anim.easeOut(fade)) * -20f);
            e.fill(0, barY, e.width(), 24, Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.BG_HEADER, fade));
            e.drawText(GuiTheme.PAD, barY + 7, "JSA  ·  SP / Open LAN", Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.ACCENT, fade));
            String ver = Versions.get().raw() + " · " + Versions.get().era().label();
            e.drawText(e.width() - 8 - ver.length() * 6, barY + 7, ver, Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.TEXT_DIM, fade));
            e.drawText(GuiTheme.PAD + 148, barY + 7, "ESC close  ·  LMB toggle  ·  RMB settings",
                    Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.TEXT_DIM, fade));
        }

        Panel chrome = null;
        for (Hit hit : layout()) {
            if (hit.header) {
                chrome = hit.panel;
                paintChrome(e, hit, mx, my);
                continue;
            }
            if (chrome != hit.panel) continue;
            if (hit.setting != null) paintSetting(e, hit, mx, my);
            else paintModule(e, hit, mx, my);
        }
    }

    private void step(float dt) {
        float target = (open && !closing) ? 1f : 0f;
        openAnim = Anim.approach(openAnim, target, dt, 8f);
        for (Panel panel : panels.values()) {
            panel.body = Anim.approach(panel.body, panel.collapsed ? 0f : 1f, dt, 12f);
        }
        for (Module m : ModuleManager.get().getAllModules()) {
            float cur = expand.getOrDefault(m, 0f);
            boolean show = expanded == m;
            if (!show && cur <= 0f) continue;
            float next = Anim.approach(cur, show ? 1f : 0f, dt, 14f);
            if (!show && next <= 0.001f) expand.remove(m);
            else expand.put(m, next);
        }
    }

    private void paintChrome(RenderEvent e, Hit hit, int mx, int my) {
        float reveal = hit.reveal;
        if (reveal <= 0.02f) return;
        int x = hit.x;
        int y = hit.y;
        int w = hit.w;
        int vis = hit.visH;
        e.fill(x + 2, y + 2, w, vis, Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.SHADOW, reveal));
        e.fill(x, y, w, vis, Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.BG_PANEL, reveal));
        e.fill(x, y, w, GuiTheme.HEADER_H, Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.BG_HEADER, reveal));
        boolean hot = contains(mx, my, x, y, w, GuiTheme.HEADER_H);
        float hov = hoverOf(hit.panel, hot, frameDt);
        if (hov > 0.02f) {
            e.fill(x, y, w, GuiTheme.HEADER_H, Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.BG_HOVER, reveal * hov * 0.7f));
        }
        e.outline(x, y, w, vis, Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.BORDER, reveal));
        e.fill(x, y, 2, vis, Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.ACCENT, reveal));
        String title = hit.panel.category.title() + (hit.panel.collapsed ? "  +" : "  –");
        e.drawText(x + 6, y + 5, title, Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.TEXT, reveal));
    }

    private void paintModule(RenderEvent e, Hit hit, int mx, int my) {
        Module m = hit.module;
        float reveal = hit.reveal;
        int x = hit.x;
        int y = hit.y;
        int w = hit.w;
        int h = hit.h;
        boolean on = m.isEnabled();
        boolean hot = contains(mx, my, x, y, w, h);
        float hov = hoverOf(m, hot, frameDt);
        int bg = on ? GuiTheme.BG_ENABLED : 0x00000000;
        if ((bg >>> 24) != 0) {
            e.fill(x + 2, y, w - 4, Math.max(1, h - 1), Anim.withAlpha(bg, reveal));
        }
        if (hov > 0.02f) {
            e.fill(x + 2, y, w - 4, Math.max(1, h - 1), Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.BG_HOVER, reveal * hov));
        }
        if (on || hov > 0.05f) {
            float bar = on ? Math.max(0.85f, hov) : hov;
            e.fill(x + 2, y, 2, Math.max(1, h - 1), Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.ACCENT, reveal * bar));
        }
        float textA = reveal * Anim.clamp01(h / (float) GuiTheme.ROW_H);
        int textY = y + Math.max(1, (Math.min(h, GuiTheme.ROW_H) - 8) / 2);
        e.drawText(x + 8, textY, m.getName(), Anim.withAlpha(on ? GuiTheme.ACCENT : GuiTheme.TEXT_DIM, textA));
        if (m instanceof AbstractModule am && !am.getSettings().isEmpty()) {
            boolean openRow = expanded == m;
            e.drawText(x + w - 12, textY, openRow ? "▾" : "▸", Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.TEXT_OFF, textA));
        }
    }

    private void paintSetting(RenderEvent e, Hit hit, int mx, int my) {
        float reveal = hit.reveal;
        float grow = Anim.clamp01(hit.h / (float) GuiTheme.ROW_H);
        float alpha = reveal * grow;
        if (alpha <= 0.02f) return;
        int x = hit.x;
        int y = hit.y;
        int w = hit.w;
        int h = hit.h;
        boolean hot = contains(mx, my, x, y, w, h);
        float hov = hoverOf(hit.setting, hot, frameDt);
        e.fill(x + 2, y, w - 4, Math.max(1, h - 1), Anim.withAlpha(0xFF141820, alpha));
        if (hov > 0.02f) {
            e.fill(x + 2, y, w - 4, Math.max(1, h - 1), Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.BG_HOVER, alpha * hov));
        }
        drawSetting(e, hit.setting, x, y, w, h, alpha);
    }

    private void drawSetting(RenderEvent e, Setting<?> s, int x, int y, int w, int h, float alpha) {
        int textY = y + Math.max(1, (Math.min(h, 12) - 8) / 2);
        String label = s.getName();
        if (s instanceof BoolSetting b) {
            e.drawText(x + 8, textY, label, Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.TEXT_DIM, alpha));
            e.drawText(x + w - 28, textY, b.get() ? "ON" : "OFF",
                    Anim.withAlpha(b.get() ? GuiTheme.ACCENT : GuiTheme.TEXT_OFF, alpha));
        } else if (s instanceof ModeSetting m) {
            e.drawText(x + 8, textY, label + ": " + m.get(), Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.TEXT_DIM, alpha));
        } else if (s instanceof NumberSetting n) {
            e.drawText(x + 8, y + 1, label, Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.TEXT_OFF, alpha));
            if (h >= 13) {
                int barX = x + 8;
                int barY = y + Math.min(11, h - 4);
                int barW = w - 16;
                e.fill(barX, barY, barW, 3, Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.SLIDER_BG, alpha));
                double t = (n.get() - n.min()) / Math.max(0.0001, n.max() - n.min());
                e.fill(barX, barY, (int) (barW * t), 3, Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.SLIDER_FILL, alpha));
            }
            String val = n.isInteger() ? String.valueOf(n.getI()) : String.format("%.2f", n.get());
            e.drawText(x + w - 8 - val.length() * 6, y + 1, val, Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.ACCENT, alpha));
        } else if (s instanceof ColorSetting c) {
            e.drawText(x + 8, textY, label, Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.TEXT_DIM, alpha));
            int swatch = Math.min(10, Math.max(4, h - 4));
            e.fill(x + w - 18, y + 3, swatch, swatch, Anim.withAlpha(c.get(), alpha));
        } else {
            e.drawText(x + 8, textY, label + ": " + s, Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.TEXT_DIM, alpha));
        }
    }

    private List<Hit> layout() {
        List<Hit> out = new ArrayList<>();
        int index = 0;
        for (Panel panel : panels.values()) {
            float reveal = reveal(index++);
            int x = panel.x + Math.round((1f - reveal) * SLIDE_X);
            int y = panel.y + Math.round((1f - reveal) * SLIDE_Y);
            int w = GuiTheme.PANEL_W;
            List<Module> mods = modulesOf(panel.category);
            int vis = GuiTheme.HEADER_H + Math.round(contentHeight(mods) * panel.body);
            out.add(new Hit(panel, null, null, x, y, w, GuiTheme.HEADER_H, true, reveal, vis));
            if (reveal < 0.12f || panel.body <= 0.03f) continue;
            int clip = y + vis;
            int cursor = y + GuiTheme.HEADER_H;
            for (Module m : mods) {
                int room = clip - cursor;
                if (room > 1) {
                    out.add(new Hit(panel, m, null, x, cursor, w, Math.min(GuiTheme.ROW_H, room), false, reveal, 0));
                }
                cursor += GuiTheme.ROW_H;
                if (cursor >= clip) break;
                float ex = expand.getOrDefault(m, 0f);
                if (ex > 0.01f && m instanceof AbstractModule am) {
                    int sh = Math.max(1, Math.round(GuiTheme.ROW_H * ex));
                    for (Setting<?> s : am.getSettings().values()) {
                        int sRoom = clip - cursor;
                        if (sRoom <= 1) {
                            cursor = clip;
                            break;
                        }
                        out.add(new Hit(panel, m, s, x, cursor, w, Math.min(sh, sRoom), false, reveal, 0));
                        cursor += sh;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return out;
    }

    private int contentHeight(List<Module> mods) {
        int h = 2;
        for (Module m : mods) {
            h += GuiTheme.ROW_H;
            float ex = expand.getOrDefault(m, 0f);
            if (ex > 0.01f && m instanceof AbstractModule am) {
                h += Math.round(am.getSettings().size() * GuiTheme.ROW_H * ex);
            }
        }
        return h;
    }

    /** Staggered 0..1. Later columns follow the first. */
    private float reveal(int index) {
        float delay = index * 0.06f;
        float span = Math.max(0.4f, 1f - delay);
        return Anim.easeOut(Anim.clamp01((openAnim - delay) / span));
    }

    private float hoverOf(Object key, boolean hot, float dt) {
        float cur = hover.getOrDefault(key, 0f);
        float next = Anim.approach(cur, hot ? 1f : 0f, dt, 16f);
        if (!hot && next <= 0.02f) {
            hover.remove(key);
            return 0f;
        }
        hover.put(key, next);
        return next;
    }

    private static boolean contains(int mx, int my, int x, int y, int w, int h) {
        return mx >= x && mx < x + w && my >= y && my < y + h;
    }

    private List<Module> modulesOf(Category c) {
        return new ArrayList<>(ModuleManager.get().getModulesByCategory(c));
    }

    private static final class Hit {
        final Panel panel;
        final Module module;
        final Setting<?> setting;
        final int x, y, w, h;
        final boolean header;
        final float reveal;
        /** Full animated panel height. Set on the header row only. */
        final int visH;

        Hit(Panel panel, Module module, Setting<?> setting, int x, int y, int w, int h,
            boolean header, float reveal, int visH) {
            this.panel = panel;
            this.module = module;
            this.setting = setting;
            this.x = x;
            this.y = y;
            this.w = w;
            this.h = h;
            this.header = header;
            this.reveal = reveal;
            this.visH = visH;
        }
    }

    private static final class Panel {
        final Category category;
        int x, y;
        boolean collapsed;
        boolean userMoved;
        /** 0 collapsed, 1 fully open. Height eases toward {@link #collapsed}. */
        float body = 1f;

        Panel(Category category, int x, int y) {
            this.category = category;
            this.x = x;
            this.y = y;
        }
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/gui/GuiTheme.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.gui;

/**
 * Warm slate + amber accent — readable on bright and dark worlds, not the
 * usual purple-glow cheat-client look.
 */
public final class GuiTheme {
    private GuiTheme() {}

    public static final int BG          = 0xE6121418;
    public static final int BG_PANEL    = 0xF0181B22;
    public static final int BG_HEADER   = 0xFF1E232C;
    public static final int BG_HOVER    = 0xFF2A3140;
    public static final int BG_ENABLED  = 0xFF2E3A28;
    public static final int ACCENT      = 0xFFE8A54B;
    public static final int ACCENT_DIM  = 0xFFB07A35;
    public static final int TEXT        = 0xFFE8EEF7;
    public static final int TEXT_DIM    = 0xFF9AA3B2;
    public static final int TEXT_OFF    = 0xFF6B7380;
    public static final int BORDER      = 0xFF3A4250;
    public static final int SLIDER_BG   = 0xFF10131A;
    public static final int SLIDER_FILL = 0xFFE8A54B;
    public static final int SHADOW      = 0x66000000;
    /** HUD cards. Opaque enough to read over bright terrain. */
    public static final int BG_CARD     = 0xD010141C;

    public static final int PANEL_W     = 118;
    public static final int ROW_H       = 16;
    public static final int HEADER_H    = 18;
    public static final int PAD         = 8;
    /** Extra air between category panels. */
    public static final int GAP         = 10;
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/AbstractModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Platform;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ColorSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.Setting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.StringSetting;

import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Base for modules with settings. The registry keeps settings in insertion
 * order so the ClickGUI renders them deterministically.
 *
 * <p><b>Two ways to use settings, both supported:</b></p>
 * <ul>
 *   <li><b>Factories</b> — declare a field, and the setting is created and
 *       registered for you:
 *       <pre>
 *       private final NumberSetting speed = num("speed", 1.0, 0.1, 5.0, 0.1, false);
 *       private final ModeSetting mode    = mode("Mode", "Vanilla", "Packet");
 *       private final BoolSetting  swing  = bool("Swing", true);
 *       </pre>
 *   </li>
 *   <li><b>Accessors</b> — read a live setting by name and call {@code .get()}:
 *       <pre>
 *       double s = num("speed").get();
 *       String  m = mode("Mode").get();
 *       boolean b = bool("Swing").get();
 *       </pre>
 *   </li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p>Subclasses need no constructor: identity (name + category) is derived by
 * {@link Module} from the class name and package.</p>
 */
public abstract class AbstractModule extends Module {
    private final Map<String, Setting<?>> settings = new LinkedHashMap<>();

    protected AbstractModule() { super(); }
    protected AbstractModule(String name, Category category) { super(name, category); }

    /** Shorthand for the live platform adapter — {@code platform().isGrounded()} etc. */
    protected Platform platform() { return MC.get(); }

    /** Registers a setting under its own name and returns it (fluent). */
    protected <T extends Setting<?>> T setting(T s) {
        settings.put(s.getName().toLowerCase(), s);
        return s;
    }

    /** Raw lookup (case-insensitive). */
    public Setting<?> get(String name) { return settings.get(name.toLowerCase()); }

    /** Ordered view of this module's settings (GUI/config). */
    public Map<String, Setting<?>> getSettings() { return settings; }

    // -- accessors: return the live setting; call .get() for the value -------
    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    protected BoolSetting bool(String name) { return (BoolSetting) get(name); }
    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    protected NumberSetting num(String name) { return (NumberSetting) get(name); }
    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    protected ModeSetting mode(String name) { return (ModeSetting) get(name); }
    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    protected StringSetting str(String name) { return (StringSetting) get(name); }
    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    protected ColorSetting color(String name) { return (ColorSetting) get(name); }

    // -- factories: create + register; use as field initializers -------------
    protected BoolSetting bool(String name, boolean def) {
        return setting(new BoolSetting(name, def));
    }

    protected NumberSetting num(String name, double def, double min, double max,
                                double step, boolean integer) {
        return setting(new NumberSetting(name, def, min, max, step, integer));
    }

    /** Mode list; the first mode is the default. */
    protected ModeSetting mode(String name, String... modes) {
        return setting(new ModeSetting(name, modes, modes.length > 0 ? modes[0] : name));
    }

    /** Mode list with an explicit default (may differ from the first mode). */
    protected ModeSetting mode(String name, String[] modes, String def) {
        return setting(new ModeSetting(name, modes, def));
    }

    protected StringSetting str(String name, String def) {
        return setting(new StringSetting(name, def));
    }

    protected ColorSetting color(String name, int def) {
        return setting(new ColorSetting(name, def));
    }
}

```

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module;

/** ClickGUI panel categories for the single-player / open-LAN toolkit. */
public enum Category {
    COMBAT("Combat"),
    MOVEMENT("Movement"),
    PLAYER("Player"),
    WORLD("World"),
    RENDER("Render"),
    TROLL("Pranks"),
    MISC("Misc"),
    METEOR("Meteor Addons");

    private final String title;

    Category(String title) { this.title = title; }

    public String title() { return title; }

    @Override public String toString() { return name(); }
}

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/Module.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module;

import dev.jsa.client.core.event.AttackEvent;
import dev.jsa.client.core.event.MotionEvent;
import dev.jsa.client.core.event.PacketEvent;
import dev.jsa.client.core.event.RenderEvent;

/**
 * Base contract for all modules. Subclasses normally extend
 * {@link AbstractModule}; this class carries identity, the enabled flag, and
 * the dispatch hooks.
 *
 * <p>Identity is derived automatically: display name = class name minus
 * {@code Module} suffix; {@link Category} from the package
 * ({@code module.combat.*} → COMBAT, …).</p>
 */
public abstract class Module {
    private final String name;
    private final Category category;
    private boolean enabled;

    protected Module() {
        this.name = nameFromClass(getClass());
        this.category = categoryFromPackage(getClass().getPackage() != null
                ? getClass().getPackage().getName() : "");
    }

    protected Module(String name, Category category) {
        this.name = name;
        this.category = category;
    }

    private static String nameFromClass(Class<?> c) {
        String s = c.getSimpleName();
        if (s.endsWith("Module")) s = s.substring(0, s.length() - "Module".length());
        return s;
    }

    private static Category categoryFromPackage(String pkg) {
        if (pkg.endsWith(".combat"))   return Category.COMBAT;
        if (pkg.endsWith(".movement")) return Category.MOVEMENT;
        if (pkg.endsWith(".player"))   return Category.PLAYER;
        if (pkg.endsWith(".render"))   return Category.RENDER;
        if (pkg.endsWith(".troll"))    return Category.TROLL;
        if (pkg.endsWith(".misc"))     return Category.MISC;
        if (pkg.endsWith(".world"))    return Category.WORLD;
        return Category.MISC;
    }

    public String getName() { return name; }
    public Category getCategory() { return category; }
    public boolean isEnabled() { return enabled; }

    public void setEnabled(boolean enabled) { this.enabled = enabled; }

    public final void tick() {
        if (enabled) onTick();
    }

    protected void onEnable() {}
    protected void onDisable() {}

    protected void onTick() {}
    protected void onMotion(MotionEvent event) {}
    protected void onRender(RenderEvent event) {}
    protected void onPacketSend(PacketEvent event) {}
    protected void onPacketReceive(PacketEvent event) {}
    protected void onAttack(AttackEvent event) {}

    @Override public String toString() { return "Module<" + name + ">"; }
}

```

    /** Idempotent: safe to call from boot and from tests. */

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/ModuleManager.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module;

import dev.jsa.client.core.event.AttackEvent;
import dev.jsa.client.core.event.Event;
import dev.jsa.client.core.event.EventBus;
import dev.jsa.client.core.event.MotionEvent;
import dev.jsa.client.core.event.PacketEvent;
import dev.jsa.client.core.event.RenderEvent;
import dev.jsa.client.core.gui.ClickGui;
import dev.jsa.client.core.util.FpsCounter;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList;

/**
 * Central registry for all modules. The adapter calls tick / motion / render /
 * packet / attack hooks; this class forwards to enabled modules in
 * registration order and posts events to {@link EventBus}.
 */
public final class ModuleManager {
    private static final ModuleManager INSTANCE = new ModuleManager();

    private final Map<String, Module> modules = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final List<Module> order = new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();
    private final Map<Category, List<Module>> byCategory = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public static ModuleManager get() { return INSTANCE; }
    private ModuleManager() {}

    public void register(Module module) {
        modules.put(module.getName().toLowerCase(), module);
        order.add(module);
        byCategory.computeIfAbsent(module.getCategory(), k -> new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>()).add(module);
    }

    public void unregister(Module module) {
        modules.remove(module.getName().toLowerCase());
        order.remove(module);
        List<Module> list = byCategory.get(module.getCategory());
        if (list != null) list.remove(module);
    }

    public void toggle(Module module) {
        boolean now = !module.isEnabled();
        module.setEnabled(now);
        if (now) module.onEnable();
        else module.onDisable();
    }

    public void tick() {
        ClickGui.get().tick();
        for (Module m : order) m.tick();
    }

    public void onMotion(MotionEvent event) {
        for (Module m : enabled()) m.onMotion(event);
        EventBus.get().post(event);
    }

    public void onRender(RenderEvent event) {
        if (event.isWorldPass()) {
            for (Module m : enabled()) m.onRender(event);
            return;
        }
        FpsCounter.getInstance().frame();
        for (Module m : enabled()) m.onRender(event);
        EventBus.get().post(event);
        if (!ClickGui.get().isScreenHosted()) ClickGui.get().render(event);
    }

    public void onPacketSend(PacketEvent event) {
        for (Module m : enabled()) m.onPacketSend(event);
        EventBus.get().post(event);
    }

    public void onPacketReceive(PacketEvent event) {
        for (Module m : enabled()) m.onPacketReceive(event);
        EventBus.get().post(event);
    }

    public void onAttack(AttackEvent event) {
        for (Module m : enabled()) m.onAttack(event);
        EventBus.get().post(event);
    }

    public void dispatch(Event event) {
        if (event instanceof MotionEvent m) onMotion(m);
        else if (event instanceof RenderEvent r) onRender(r);
        else if (event instanceof AttackEvent a) onAttack(a);
        else if (event instanceof PacketEvent p) onPacketSend(p);
        else EventBus.get().post(event);
    }

    private List<Module> enabled() {
        List<Module> out = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Module m : order) if (m.isEnabled()) out.add(m);
        return out;
    }

    public Collection<Module> getAllModules() {
        return Collections.unmodifiableList(order);
    }

    public Collection<Module> getModulesByCategory(Category category) {
        return byCategory.getOrDefault(category, List.of());
    }

    public Module getModule(String name) { return modules.get(name.toLowerCase()); }

    public Module getModule(Category category, String name) {
        return byCategory.getOrDefault(category, List.of()).stream()
                .filter(m -> m.getName().equalsIgnoreCase(name))
                .findFirst().orElse(null);
    }
}

package dev.jsa.client.core.module;

/**
 * A module whose state is driven programmatically rather than toggled by the
 * user (e.g. a helper that activates while another module is on). The
 * ClickGUI may render it read-only.
 */
public abstract class StateModule extends AbstractModule {
    protected StateModule() { super(); }
    protected StateModule(String name, Category category) { super(name, category); }

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module;

/**
 * A module toggled on/off by the user. The enabled flag lives on {@link Module};
 * this class exists so the ClickGUI can distinguish on/off modules from
 * state-only ones.
 *
 * <p>Subclasses need no constructor — name and category are derived from the
 * class name and package by {@link Module}.</p>
 */
public abstract class ToggledModule extends AbstractModule {
    protected ToggledModule() { super(); }
    protected ToggledModule(String name, Category category) { super(name, category); }
}

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/combat/AntiAFKModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.combat;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;

/**
 * Keeps you from looking AFK while AFK on a LAN world (or waiting for your
 * brother to reconnect). Does a tiny action on an interval.
 */
public class AntiAFKModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final ModeSetting mode = mode("Mode", "Sneak", "Swing", "Rotate", "Jump");
    private final NumberSetting interval = num("Interval Sec", 8.0, 2.0, 120.0, 1.0, true);

    private long lastAction;

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || !p.inGame()) return;
        long wait = (long) (interval.get() * 1000L);
        if (System.currentTimeMillis() - lastAction < wait) return;
        lastAction = System.currentTimeMillis();

        switch (mode.get()) {
            case "Sneak" -> p.sendSneak(!p.isSneaking());
            case "Swing" -> p.swingArm();
            case "Rotate" -> p.setRotation(p.getYaw() + 2.0f, p.getPitch());
            case "Jump" -> {
                if (p.isGrounded()) p.setJumping(true);
            }
            default -> {}
        }
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/combat/AuraModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.combat;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Entity;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Platform;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.util.RotationUtils;
import dev.jsa.client.core.util.TargetUtils;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Auto-attack for single-player / open LAN. Tuned for snappy friend PvP and
 * mob clearing — no anti-cheat jitter or range caps.
 *
 * <ul>
 *   <li><b>Range</b> — how far to look for targets (default 4.5).</li>
 *   <li><b>CPS</b> — attacks per second; optional light jitter for feel.</li>
 *   <li><b>Players / Mobs</b> — who to hit (great for brother duels).</li>
 *   <li><b>Rotate</b> — snap or smooth aim toward the target.</li>
 *   <li><b>Switch</b> — Distance, Health, or ClosestAngle.</li>
 * </ul>
 */
public class AuraModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final NumberSetting range = num("Range", 4.5, 2.0, 8.0, 0.1, false);
    private final NumberSetting wallRange = num("Wall Range", 3.0, 0.0, 6.0, 0.1, false);
    private final NumberSetting cps = num("CPS", 12, 1, 25, 1, true);
    private final BoolSetting players = bool("Players", true);
    private final BoolSetting mobs = bool("Mobs", true);
    private final BoolSetting armorStands = bool("Armor Stands", false);
    private final BoolSetting throughWalls = bool("Through Walls", true);
    private final BoolSetting rotate = bool("Rotate", true);
    private final BoolSetting swingJitter = bool("Swing Jitter", false);
    private final BoolSetting requireCooldown = bool("Require Cooldown", false);
    private final ModeSetting aim = mode("Aim", "Smooth", "Snap");
    private final ModeSetting switchMode = mode("Switch", "Distance", "Health", "ClosestAngle");

    private long lastAttackMs;

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        Platform p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || p.getLocalPlayer() == null || p.isInventoryOpen() || p.isDead()) return;

        List<Entity> targets = TargetUtils.targetable(
                range.get(), wallRange.get(),
                players.get(), mobs.get(), armorStands.get(), throughWalls.get());
        if (targets.isEmpty()) return;

        Entity target = TargetUtils.pick(targets, switchMode.get());
        if (target == null) return;

        if (requireCooldown.get() && p.getAttackCooldown() < 0.9f) return;

        long interval = Math.max(40L, 1000L / Math.max(1, cps.getI()));
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (swingJitter.get()) {
            interval += (long) (interval * 0.08 * (Math.random() * 2 - 1));
        }
        if (now - lastAttackMs < interval) return;
        lastAttackMs = now;

        if (rotate.get()) {
            if ("Snap".equals(aim.get())) RotationUtils.snapTo(target);
            else RotationUtils.smoothTo(target, 180.0);
        }
        p.attack(target);
        p.swingArm();
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/combat/CriticalsModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.combat;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.event.AttackEvent;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;

/**
 * Forces critical hits on every valid swing — great for LAN duels and mob
 * farms. No anti-cheat tuning; pick the feel you like.
 *
 * <ul>
 *   <li><b>Packet</b> — spoof a tiny airborne tick (no visible hop).</li>
 *   <li><b>MiniJump</b> — small physical hop before the hit.</li>
 *   <li><b>Jump</b> — full jump crit every attack when on ground.</li>
 * </ul>
 */
public class CriticalsModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final ModeSetting mode = mode("Mode", "Packet", "MiniJump", "Jump");
    private final NumberSetting hop = num("Hop Height", 0.0625, 0.01, 0.42, 0.001, false);

    @Override protected void onAttack(AttackEvent event) {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || p.getLocalPlayer() == null) return;
        if (p.isEating() || p.isBlocking()) return;
        if (p.getAttackCooldown() < 0.85f) return;

        switch (mode.get()) {
            case "Packet" -> p.sendPositionPacket(false);
            case "MiniJump" -> {
                double y = p.getY();
                p.sendPositionPacket(p.getX(), y + hop.get(), p.getZ(), false, false, false);
                p.sendPositionPacket(p.getX(), y, p.getZ(), false, false, false);
            }
            case "Jump" -> {
                if (p.isGrounded()) {
                    p.setVelocity(p.getVelocityX(), Math.max(0.42, hop.get() * 6), p.getVelocityZ());
                }
            }
            default -> {}
        }
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/combat/HoldModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.combat;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Item;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.version.ModuleGates;

/**
 * Keeps the attack indicator near-full while you hold attack. Meaningful on
 * 1.9+ combat; harmless no-op on 1.6.4–1.8 (no cooldown bar).
 */
public class HoldModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final NumberSetting holdAt = num("Hold At", 0.95, 0.5, 1.0, 0.01, false);
    private final BoolSetting weaponsOnly = bool("Weapons Only", true);

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || !p.isAttacking()) return;
        if (!ModuleGates.caps().canUseAttackCooldownHold()) return;
        if (p.getAttackCooldown() > holdAt.get()) {
            Item held = p.getHeldItem();
            if (weaponsOnly.get() && (held == null || !held.isWeapon())) return;
            p.setAttackCooldown((float) holdAt.get());
        }
    }
}

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/combat/ReachModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.combat;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;

/**
 * Extends client-side attack / interact range for SP and open LAN.
 * On an integrated or open-to-LAN world the host accepts the longer reach.
 */
public class ReachModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final NumberSetting range = num("Range", 4.5, 3.0, 8.0, 0.1, false);
    private final NumberSetting blockReach = num("Block Reach", 5.5, 4.5, 10.0, 0.1, false);
    private final BoolSetting creativeBoost = bool("Creative Boost", true);

    @Override protected void onEnable() { apply(); }
    @Override protected void onTick() { apply(); }

    @Override protected void onDisable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        p.setEntityReach(3.0);
        p.setBlockReach(4.5);
    }

    private void apply() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        double ent = range.get();
        double blk = blockReach.get();
        if (creativeBoost.get() && p.isCreative()) {
            ent = Math.max(ent, 6.0);
            blk = Math.max(blk, 7.0);
        }
        p.setEntityReach(ent);
        p.setBlockReach(blk);
    }

    /** Effective entity attack reach in blocks. */
    public double effectiveReach() { return range.get(); }

    /** Effective block interact reach in blocks. */
    public double effectiveBlockReach() { return blockReach.get(); }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/combat/TrampolineModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.combat;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;

/**
 * Super-bounce jumps — hold Space and launch. Fun for parkour maps and
 * brother chase games on LAN.
 */
public class TrampolineModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final NumberSetting power = num("Power", 1.2, 0.4, 4.0, 0.1, false);
    private final BoolSetting resetFall = bool("Reset Fall", true);
    private final BoolSetting onlyGround = bool("Only On Ground", true);

    private boolean jumping;

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || p.getLocalPlayer() == null) return;

        if (p.isJumping() && !jumping) {
            if (onlyGround.get() && !p.isGrounded()) {
                jumping = true;
                return;
            }
            jumping = true;
            p.setVelocity(p.getVelocityX(), power.get(), p.getVelocityZ());
            if (resetFall.get()) p.sendPositionPacket(true);
        }
        if (!p.isJumping()) jumping = false;
    }
}

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/combat/VelocityModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.combat;

import dev.jsa.client.core.event.PacketEvent;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;

/**
 * Knockback control for LAN fights. Split horizontal / vertical so you can
 * cancel shove while keeping a little hop, or go full tank at 0/0.
 */
public class VelocityModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final NumberSetting horizontal = num("Horizontal %", 0.0, 0.0, 100.0, 1.0, true);
    private final NumberSetting vertical = num("Vertical %", 0.0, 0.0, 100.0, 1.0, true);
    private final BoolSetting explosions = bool("Explosions Too", true);

    @Override protected void onPacketReceive(PacketEvent event) {
        if (!event.isType("PlayerVelocityC2SPacket.Vel")
                && !(explosions.get() && event.isType("ExplosionS2CPacket"))) {
            return;
        }
        if (event.getPacket().entityId() != localId() && event.isType("PlayerVelocityC2SPacket.Vel")) {
            return;
        }

        double scaleH = 1.0 - horizontal.get() / 100.0;
        double scaleV = 1.0 - vertical.get() / 100.0;
        platform().setPacketVelocity(event.getPacket(),
                event.getPacket().velocityX() * scaleH,
                event.getPacket().velocityY() * scaleV,
                event.getPacket().velocityZ() * scaleH);
    }

    private int localId() {
        var p = platform();
        return p.getLocalPlayer() == null ? -1 : p.getLocalPlayer().getId();
    }
}

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/movement/AutoSprintModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.movement;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;

/**
 * Keeps sprint on while you move — less sticky W-tap for LAN runs and builds.
 */
public class AutoSprintModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final ModeSetting mode = mode("Mode", "Legit", "Rage");
    private final BoolSetting omni = bool("OmniDirectional", false);
    private final BoolSetting inWater = bool("In Water", true);
    private final BoolSetting hungryStop = bool("Stop When Hungry", true);

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || p.getLocalPlayer() == null || p.isDead()) return;
        if (hungryStop.get() && p.getHunger() <= 6) {
            p.setSprinting(false);
            return;
        }
        if (!inWater.get() && p.isInWater()) return;

        boolean forward = p.isKeyDown('w');
        boolean back = p.isKeyDown('s');
        boolean strafing = p.isKeyDown('a') || p.isKeyDown('d');
        boolean moving = forward || (omni.get() && (back || strafing)) || p.isMoving();

        if ("Rage".equals(mode.get())) {
            if (moving) {
                p.setSprinting(true);
                p.sendSprint(true);
            }
            return;
        }

        // Legit: only when holding W and not sneaking
        if (forward && !p.isSneaking() && !back) {
            p.setSprinting(true);
            p.sendSprint(true);
        }
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/movement/ElytraFlyModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.movement;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.util.MathUtils;
import dev.jsa.client.core.version.ModuleGates;

/**
 * Boosted elytra flight for long trips across your shared world.
 * Requires Minecraft 1.9+; no-ops with a hint on older eras (e.g. 1.6.4–1.8).
 */
public class ElytraFlyModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final ModeSetting mode = mode("Mode", "Control", "Boost");
    private final NumberSetting speed = num("Speed", 2.5, 0.5, 8.0, 0.1, false);
    private final NumberSetting climb = num("Climb", 1.2, 0.2, 4.0, 0.1, false);
    private final BoolSetting autoStart = bool("Auto Glide", true);

    private boolean warned;

    @Override protected void onEnable() { warned = false; }

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || p.getLocalPlayer() == null) return;
        if (!ModuleGates.caps().canUseElytraFly()) {
            if (!warned) { ModuleGates.requireElytra(); warned = true; }
            return;
        }

        if (!p.isGliding()) {
            if (autoStart.get() && p.isKeyDown(' ') && !p.isGrounded()) {
                p.setFlying(true); // adapter maps to elytra start when chest is elytra
            }
            return;
        }

        double hSpeed = speed.get() / 20.0;
        float yaw = p.getYaw();
        double vx = MathUtils.dirX(yaw) * hSpeed * 20;
        double vz = MathUtils.dirZ(yaw) * hSpeed * 20;
        double vy = p.getVelocityY();

        if ("Control".equals(mode.get())) {
            if (p.isKeyDown(' ')) vy = climb.get() / 20.0 * 20;
            else if (p.isShiftDown()) vy = -climb.get() / 20.0 * 20;
            else vy = Math.min(vy, 0.05);
            p.setVelocity(vx, vy, vz);
        } else {
            double curH = Math.sqrt(p.getVelocityX() * p.getVelocityX() + p.getVelocityZ() * p.getVelocityZ());
            if (curH < 0.01) {
                p.setVelocity(vx, Math.min(vy, 0.1), vz);
            } else {
                double scale = (hSpeed * 20) / curH;
                p.setVelocity(p.getVelocityX() * scale, Math.min(vy, 0.15), p.getVelocityZ() * scale);
            }
        }
    }
}

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.movement;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;

/**
 * Hold the drop key to plunge quickly without taking fall damage —
 * useful for getting down from builds fast.
 */
public class FastFallModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final NumberSetting speed = num("Fall Speed", 2.5, 0.5, 8.0, 0.1, false);
    private final BoolSetting resetFall = bool("No Fall Damage", true);
    private final BoolSetting holdKey = bool("Hold R", true);

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || p.getLocalPlayer() == null) return;
        if (holdKey.get() && !p.isKeyDown('r')) return;
        if (p.isGrounded()) return;

        p.setVelocity(p.getVelocityX(), -speed.get(), p.getVelocityZ());
        if (resetFall.get()) p.sendPositionPacket(true);
    }
}

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/movement/FlyModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.movement;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;

/**
 * Flight for the world you are hosting (singleplayer or open LAN).
 * Both modes set the host player's flying ability. Speed is blocks per second.
 * The client does not spoof movement packets.
 */
public class FlyModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final ModeSetting mode = mode("Mode", "Creative", "Velocity");
    private final NumberSetting speed = num("Speed", 1.5, 0.2, 10.0, 0.1, false);
    private final NumberSetting vertical = num("Vertical", 1.0, 0.1, 5.0, 0.1, false);
    private final BoolSetting stopOnDisable = bool("Stop On Disable", true);

    /** Blocks per second. The host hook turns this into fly speed. */
    public float flySpeedBlocks() {
        float blocks = speed.getF();
        if ("Velocity".equals(mode.get())) blocks *= Math.max(0.5f, vertical.getF());
        return blocks;
    }

    @Override protected void onEnable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        p.setFlying(true);
    }

    @Override protected void onDisable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        p.setFlying(false);
        if (stopOnDisable.get()) p.setVelocity(0, 0, 0);
    }

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || p.getLocalPlayer() == null) return;
        p.setFlying(true);
    }
}

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/movement/FreecamModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.movement;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.util.MathUtils;

/**
 * Detach the camera and fly around the world while your body stays put —
 * scout builds, find your brother, or film a cinematic without moving.
 */
public class FreecamModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final NumberSetting speed = num("Speed", 1.5, 0.2, 8.0, 0.1, false);
    private final BoolSetting freezeBody = bool("Freeze Body", true);
    private final BoolSetting noclip = bool("NoClip Camera", true);

    private double camX, camY, camZ;
    private float camYaw, camPitch;
    private boolean active;

    @Override protected void onEnable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || p.getLocalPlayer() == null) return;
        camX = p.getX();
        camY = p.getY() + 1.62;
        camZ = p.getZ();
        camYaw = p.getYaw();
        camPitch = p.getPitch();
        active = true;
        if (noclip.get()) p.setNoClip(true);
        p.actionbar("Freecam on — body stays here");
    }

    @Override protected void onDisable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        p.setNoClip(false);
        active = false;
        p.actionbar("Freecam off");
    }

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || !active || p.getLocalPlayer() == null) return;

        if (freezeBody.get()) {
            p.setVelocity(0, 0, 0);
            p.sendPositionPacket(true);
        }

        double step = speed.get() / 5.0;
        camYaw = p.getYaw();
        camPitch = p.getPitch();

        if (p.isKeyDown('w')) { camX += MathUtils.dirX(camYaw) * step; camZ += MathUtils.dirZ(camYaw) * step; }
        if (p.isKeyDown('s')) { camX -= MathUtils.dirX(camYaw) * step; camZ -= MathUtils.dirZ(camYaw) * step; }
        if (p.isKeyDown('a')) { camX += MathUtils.dirX(camYaw - 90) * step; camZ += MathUtils.dirZ(camYaw - 90) * step; }
        if (p.isKeyDown('d')) { camX += MathUtils.dirX(camYaw + 90) * step; camZ += MathUtils.dirZ(camYaw + 90) * step; }
        if (p.isKeyDown(' ')) camY += step;
        if (p.isShiftDown()) camY -= step;

        // Adapter should bind camera to these coords; we also nudge pos for mock adapters.
        if (noclip.get()) {
            p.setPos(camX, camY - 1.62, camZ);
        }
    }

    public double camX() { return camX; }
    public double camY() { return camY; }
    public double camZ() { return camZ; }
}

```

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.movement;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;

/**
 * Fast ladder / vine climbing — Space up, Shift down. Speeds up tower builds
 * and cave escapes on your LAN world.
 */
public class LadderModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final BoolSetting up = bool("Climb Up", true);
    private final BoolSetting down = bool("Slide Down", true);
    private final NumberSetting speed = num("Speed", 0.4, 0.1, 1.5, 0.05, false);

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();

        if (p.isKeyDown(' ') && up.get()) {
            p.setVelocity(p.getVelocityX(), speed.get(), p.getVelocityZ());
        }
        if (p.isShiftDown() && down.get()) {
            p.setVelocity(p.getVelocityX(), -speed.get(), p.getVelocityZ());
        }
    }
}

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/movement/NoFallModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.movement;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;

/**
 * Cancels fall damage in your world / LAN. Pick a mode that matches how you
 * like to fly and build.
 *
 * <ul>
 *   <li><b>Packet</b> — tell the server you're grounded once fall exceeds Height.</li>
 *   <li><b>Always</b> — ground spoof every tick while airborne (safest for builders).</li>
 *   <li><b>Rubber</b> — soft bounce just before impact.</li>
 * </ul>
 */
public class NoFallModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final ModeSetting mode = mode("Mode", "Always", "Packet", "Rubber");
    private final NumberSetting height = num("Height", 3.0, 1.0, 20.0, 0.5, false);

    private double fallStartY = Double.NaN;

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || p.getLocalPlayer() == null) return;

        if (p.isGrounded()) {
            fallStartY = p.getY();
            return;
        }
        if (Double.isNaN(fallStartY)) fallStartY = p.getY();
        double fall = fallStartY - p.getY();

        switch (mode.get()) {
            case "Always" -> p.sendPositionPacket(true);
            case "Packet" -> {
                if (fall >= height.get()) p.sendPositionPacket(true);
            }
            case "Rubber" -> {
                if (fall >= height.get() - 0.8) {
                    p.setVelocity(p.getVelocityX(), 0.15, p.getVelocityZ());
                    p.sendPositionPacket(true);
                }
            }
            default -> {}
        }
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/movement/NoSlowModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.movement;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;

/**
 * Removes movement slowdown from eating, blocking, using items, and webs —
 * handy while building or fighting on LAN.
 */
public class NoSlowModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final BoolSetting food = bool("While Eating", true);
    private final BoolSetting shield = bool("While Blocking", true);
    private final BoolSetting items = bool("While Using Items", true);
    private final NumberSetting eatSpeed = num("Eat Speed", 0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 0.05, false);

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || p.getLocalPlayer() == null) return;

        if (p.isEating() && food.get()) p.setUseItemSpeed(eatSpeed.getF());
        if (p.isBlocking() && shield.get()) {
            // Keep full walk speed while blocking (adapter suppresses slowdown).
            p.sendSprint(p.isKeyDown('w'));
        }
        if (p.isUsingItem() && items.get() && !p.isEating()) {
            p.setUseItemSpeed(eatSpeed.getF());
        }
    }

    @Override protected void onDisable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p != null) p.setUseItemSpeed(1.0f);
    }
}
