`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/player/ArmorDyeModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.player;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ColorSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;

/**
 * Recolors leather armor — match outfits with your brother or go full rainbow.
 */
public class ArmorDyeModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final ModeSetting mode = mode("Mode", "Solid", "Rainbow");
    private final ColorSetting color = color("Color", ColorSetting.of(255, 60, 180, 220));

    private int hue;

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;

        int rgb;
        if ("Rainbow".equals(mode.get())) {
            hue = (hue + 3) % 360;
            rgb = hsbToRgb(hue / 360f, 0.85f, 1f);
        } else {
            rgb = color.get() & 0xFFFFFF;
        }

        for (int slot = 0; slot < 4; slot++) {
            var armor = p.getArmorItem(slot);
            if (armor != null && armor.getArmorSlot() == slot) {
                p.dyeArmor(slot, rgb);
            }
        }
    }

    private static int hsbToRgb(float h, float s, float b) {
        float r = 0, g = 0, bl = 0;
        if (s == 0) {
            r = g = bl = b;
        } else {
            float hf = (h - (float) Math.floor(h)) * 6f;
            float f = hf - (float) Math.floor(hf);
            float p = b * (1f - s);
            float q = b * (1f - s * f);
            float t = b * (1f - s * (1f - f));
            switch ((int) hf) {
                case 0 -> { r = b; g = t; bl = p; }
                case 1 -> { r = q; g = b; bl = p; }
                case 2 -> { r = p; g = b; bl = t; }
                case 3 -> { r = p; g = q; bl = b; }
                case 4 -> { r = t; g = p; bl = b; }
                case 5 -> { r = b; g = p; bl = q; }
                default -> {}
            }
        }
        return ((int) (r * 255) << 16) | ((int) (g * 255) << 8) | (int) (bl * 255);
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/player/AutoToolModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.player;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;

/**
 * Swaps to the best hotbar tool for the block under the crosshair —
 * less inventory fiddling while mining together.
 */
public class AutoToolModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final ModeSetting mode = mode("Mode", "Instant", "Swing");
    private final BoolSetting onlyWhenMining = bool("Only When Mining", true);
    private final BoolSetting switchBack = bool("Switch Back", false);

    private int previousSlot = -1;

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || p.getLocalPlayer() == null) return;
        if (onlyWhenMining.get() && !p.isAttacking()) {
            if (switchBack.get() && previousSlot >= 0) {
                p.setHotbarSlot(previousSlot);
                previousSlot = -1;
            }
            return;
        }

        int block = p.getBlockIdAtCrosshair();
        if (block == 0) return;

        int best = p.bestToolSlot(block);
        if (best < 0 || best == p.getHotbarSlot()) return;

        if (previousSlot < 0) previousSlot = p.getHotbarSlot();
        p.setHotbarSlot(best);
        if ("Swing".equals(mode.get())) p.swingArm();
    }

    @Override protected void onDisable() {
        previousSlot = -1;
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/player/AutoTotemModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.player;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.version.ModuleGates;

/**
 * Keeps a totem in the offhand. Requires 1.11+ (totems) with offhand (1.9+);
 * no-ops with a hint on older eras.
 */
public class AutoTotemModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final BoolSetting alert = bool("Alert", true);
    private final BoolSetting preferHotbar = bool("Prefer Hotbar", true);

    private boolean hadOffhandTotem;
    private boolean warned;

    @Override protected void onEnable() { warned = false; }

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || p.getLocalPlayer() == null) return;
        if (!ModuleGates.caps().canUseAutoTotem()) {
            if (!warned) { ModuleGates.requireOffhandTotem(); warned = true; }
            return;
        }

        boolean offhandIsTotem = p.getOffhandItem() != null && p.getOffhandItem().isTotem();
        int hotbarSlot = -1;
        for (int i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
            var item = p.getHotbarItem(i);
            if (item != null && item.isTotem()) {
                hotbarSlot = i;
                break;
            }
        }

        if (!offhandIsTotem && hotbarSlot >= 0 && preferHotbar.get()) {
            p.setHotbarSlot(hotbarSlot);
            p.swapOffhand();
            if (alert.get()) p.actionbar("Totem equipped");
        } else if (hadOffhandTotem && !offhandIsTotem && alert.get()) {
            p.actionbar("Totem popped!");
        }
        hadOffhandTotem = offhandIsTotem;
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/player/ChestStealerModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.player;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;

/**
 * Loot open chests into your inventory automatically — useful after dungeon
 * runs or when sorting bases on LAN.
 */
public class ChestStealerModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final NumberSetting delayMs = num("Delay Ms", 50, 0, 500, 5, true);
    private final BoolSetting closeWhenDone = bool("Close When Done", true);
    private final BoolSetting reverse = bool("Dump Inventory", false);

    private int clickIndex;
    private long lastClick;

    @Override protected void onEnable() {
        clickIndex = 0;
        lastClick = 0;
    }

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || !p.isInventoryOpen()) {
            clickIndex = 0;
            return;
        }

        int size = p.getContainerSize();
        if (size <= 0) return;

        if (clickIndex >= size) {
            if (closeWhenDone.get()) p.closeInventory();
            clickIndex = 0;
            return;
        }

        if (System.currentTimeMillis() - lastClick < delayMs.getI()) return;
        // reverse currently reserved for future dump-to-chest direction
        p.shiftClickFromContainer(reverse.get() ? (size - 1 - clickIndex) : clickIndex);
        lastClick = System.currentTimeMillis();
        clickIndex++;
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/player/NoClipModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.player;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.util.MathUtils;

/**
 * Walk through blocks in your own world — debug builds, escape caves, or
 * sneak into sealed rooms on LAN. Host must allow it (integrated / open LAN).
 */
public class NoClipModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final ModeSetting mode = mode("Mode", "Full", "Fly");
    private final NumberSetting speed = num("Speed", 1.0, 0.2, 5.0, 0.1, false);
    private final BoolSetting showHint = bool("Actionbar Hint", true);

    public float flySpeedBlocks() { return speed.getF(); }

    @Override protected void onEnable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        p.setNoClip(true);
        if (showHint.get()) p.actionbar("NoClip on");
    }

    @Override protected void onDisable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        p.setNoClip(false);
        if (showHint.get()) p.actionbar("NoClip off");
    }

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || p.getLocalPlayer() == null) return;
        p.setNoClip(true);

        if (!"Fly".equals(mode.get())) return;

        double step = speed.get() / 20.0 * 20;
        float yaw = p.getYaw();
        double vx = 0, vz = 0, vy = 0;
        if (p.isKeyDown('w')) { vx += MathUtils.dirX(yaw); vz += MathUtils.dirZ(yaw); }
        if (p.isKeyDown('s')) { vx -= MathUtils.dirX(yaw); vz -= MathUtils.dirZ(yaw); }
        if (p.isKeyDown('a')) { vx += MathUtils.dirX(yaw - 90); vz += MathUtils.dirZ(yaw - 90); }
        if (p.isKeyDown('d')) { vx += MathUtils.dirX(yaw + 90); vz += MathUtils.dirZ(yaw + 90); }
        if (p.isKeyDown(' ')) vy += step;
        if (p.isShiftDown()) vy -= step;
        double len = Math.sqrt(vx * vx + vz * vz);
        if (len > 0.001) {
            vx = vx / len * step;
            vz = vz / len * step;
        }
        p.setVelocity(vx, vy, vz);
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/player/NoHungerModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.player;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;

/**
 * Hunger QoL for creative-ish survival on LAN — freeze the bar or auto-eat.
 */
public class NoHungerModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final ModeSetting mode = mode("Mode", "Freeze", "Keep Full", "Auto Eat");
    private final NumberSetting keepAt = num("Keep At", 20, 1, 20, 1, true);
    private final NumberSetting eatBelow = num("Eat Below", 14, 1, 20, 1, true);

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || p.getLocalPlayer() == null) return;

        int hunger = p.getHunger();
        switch (mode.get()) {
            case "Freeze" -> p.setHunger(hunger);
            case "Keep Full" -> p.setHunger(keepAt.getI());
            case "Auto Eat" -> {
                if (hunger <= eatBelow.getI()) {
                    for (int slot = 0; slot < 9; slot++) {
                        var item = p.getHotbarItem(slot);
                        if (item != null && item.isFood()) {
                            p.setHotbarSlot(slot);
                            p.setUseItemSpeed(0.0f);
                            p.setEating(true);
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            default -> {}
        }
    }

    @Override protected void onDisable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p != null) p.setUseItemSpeed(1.0f);
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/player/ScaffoldModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.player;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;

/**
 * Places blocks under your feet as you walk — bridge builders and sky
 * paths on LAN without looking down the whole time.
 */
public class ScaffoldModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final BoolSetting rotate = bool("Look Down", true);
    private final BoolSetting tower = bool("Tower", true);
    private final BoolSetting swing = bool("Swing Arm", true);
    private final BoolSetting autoSwitch = bool("Auto Switch", true);
    private final NumberSetting placeDelay = num("Place Delay Ticks", 0, 0, 10, 1, true);

    private int cooldown;

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || p.getLocalPlayer() == null) return;
        if (cooldown > 0) { cooldown--; return; }

        double x = Math.floor(p.getX());
        double y = Math.floor(p.getY() - 0.05);
        double z = Math.floor(p.getZ());

        if (tower.get() && p.isKeyDown(' ') && !p.isShiftDown()) {
            int slot = p.findPlaceableSlot();
            if (slot < 0) return;
            if (autoSwitch.get() && p.getHotbarSlot() != slot) p.setHotbarSlot(slot);
            if (rotate.get()) p.setRotation(p.getYaw(), 90f);
            p.placeBlockAt(x, Math.floor(p.getY()) - 1, z, 1);
            if (swing.get()) p.swingArm();
            p.setVelocity(p.getVelocityX(), 0.42, p.getVelocityZ());
            cooldown = placeDelay.getI();
            return;
        }

        if (p.getBlockIdAt(x, y, z) != 0) return;

        int slot = p.findPlaceableSlot();
        if (slot < 0) return;
        if (autoSwitch.get() && p.getHotbarSlot() != slot) p.setHotbarSlot(slot);

        if (rotate.get()) p.setRotation(p.getYaw(), 78f);
        p.placeBlockAt(x, y, z, 1);
        if (swing.get()) p.swingArm();
        cooldown = placeDelay.getI();
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/player/XRayModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.player;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.StringSetting;

/**
 * Highlights ores / useful blocks through the world — share cave finds with
 * your brother without strip-mining the entire mountain.
 */
public class XRayModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final ModeSetting preset = mode("Preset", "Default", "Ores Only", "Valuable", "Building", "Custom");
    private final StringSetting customList = str("Custom Blocks",
            "diamond_ore,deepslate_diamond_ore,emerald_ore,ancient_debris");
    private final BoolSetting hint = bool("Actionbar On Toggle", true);

    @Override protected void onEnable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p != null && hint.get()) p.actionbar("XRay: " + preset.get());
    }

    @Override protected void onDisable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p != null && hint.get()) p.actionbar("XRay off");
    }

    /** Block name hashes for the adapter's render filter. */
    public int[] activeBlocks() {
        String list = switch (preset.get()) {
            case "Default" -> "diamond_ore,deepslate_diamond_ore,emerald_ore,iron_ore,gold_ore,"
                    + "coal_ore,lapis_ore,redstone_ore,copper_ore,ancient_debris,nether_quartz_ore";
            case "Ores Only" -> "diamond_ore,deepslate_diamond_ore,emerald_ore,iron_ore,gold_ore,"
                    + "coal_ore,lapis_ore,redstone_ore,copper_ore";
            case "Valuable" -> "diamond_ore,deepslate_diamond_ore,emerald_ore,ancient_debris";
            case "Building" -> "chest,barrel,spawner,end_portal_frame,beacon,crafting_table";
            default -> customList.get();
        };
        if (list == null || list.isEmpty()) return new int[0];
        String[] parts = list.split(",");
        int[] ids = new int[parts.length];
        for (int i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) ids[i] = parts[i].trim().hashCode();
        return ids;
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/player/ZoomModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.player;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;

/**
 * Hold C to zoom — spot distant builds or your brother across the map.
 */
public class ZoomModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final ModeSetting mode = mode("Mode", "Smooth", "Instant");
    private final NumberSetting zoom = num("Factor", 0.35, 0.05, 1.0, 0.05, false);
    private final NumberSetting baseFov = num("Base FOV", 70, 30, 120, 1, true);

    private boolean zooming;
    private float savedFov = 70f;

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;

        boolean held = p.isKeyDown('c');
        float target = baseFov.getF() * zoom.getF();

        if (held && !zooming) {
            zooming = true;
            savedFov = p.getFov() > 1 ? p.getFov() : baseFov.getF();
            if ("Instant".equals(mode.get())) p.setFov(target);
        }
        if (!held && zooming) {
            zooming = false;
            p.setFov(savedFov);
            return;
        }
        if (zooming && "Smooth".equals(mode.get())) {
            float cur = p.getFov();
            p.setFov(cur + (target - cur) * 0.35f);
        }
    }

    @Override protected void onDisable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p != null && zooming) p.setFov(savedFov);
        zooming = false;
    }
}

```

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.render;

public class AmbientOcclusionModule extends ToggledModule {

    @Override protected void onEnable() { apply(); }
    @Override protected void onTick() { apply(); }

    @Override protected void onDisable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p != null) p.setAmbientOcclusion(1);
    }

    private void apply() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p != null) p.setAmbientOcclusion(level());
    }

    /** 0 off / 1 smooth / 2 max for the adapter. */

            case "Off" -> 0;
            case "Max" -> 2;
            default -> 1;
        };
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/render/EspModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.render;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.event.RenderEvent;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Entity;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ColorSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;

/**
 * Boxes through walls — find your brother, mobs, or armor stands across the
 * LAN world without hunting forever.
 */
public class EspModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final BoolSetting players = bool("Players", true);
    private final BoolSetting mobs = bool("Mobs", true);
    private final BoolSetting armorStands = bool("Armor Stands", false);
    private final BoolSetting healthBar = bool("Health Bar", true);
    private final BoolSetting distance = bool("Show Distance", true);
    private final NumberSetting maxDist = num("Max Distance", 128, 16, 512, 8, true);
    private final ColorSetting playerColor = color("Player Color", ColorSetting.of(255, 80, 220, 255));
    private final ColorSetting mobColor = color("Mob Color", ColorSetting.of(255, 90, 90, 255));
    private final ColorSetting standColor = color("Stand Color", ColorSetting.of(200, 255, 255, 220));

    @Override protected void onRender(RenderEvent event) {
        if (!event.isWorldPass()) return;
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;

        for (Entity e : p.getEntities()) {
            if (e == null || e.isDead()) continue;
            if (p.getLocalPlayer() != null && e.getId() == p.getLocalPlayer().getId()) continue;
            double d = p.distanceTo(e);
            if (d > maxDist.get()) continue;

            if (e.isPlayer() && players.get()) {
                event.drawBox(e, playerColor.get());
                if (healthBar.get()) event.drawHealthBar(e, e.getHealth() / e.getMaxHealth());
                if (distance.get()) event.drawLabel(e, String.format("%.0fm", d), playerColor.get());
            } else if (e.isArmorStand() && armorStands.get()) {
                event.drawBox(e, standColor.get());
            } else if (e.isMob() && mobs.get()) {
                event.drawBox(e, mobColor.get());
                if (healthBar.get()) event.drawHealthBar(e, e.getHealth() / e.getMaxHealth());
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/render/FullbrightModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.render;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;

/**
 * Bright caves and night — client-side gamma or night vision. Perfect for
 * co-op mining without torches everywhere.
 */
public class FullbrightModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final ModeSetting mode = mode("Mode", "Gamma", "NightVision", "Both");
    private final NumberSetting gamma = num("Gamma", 16.0, 1.0, 16.0, 0.5, false);

    /** Gamma and Both paint the lightmap. The slider cannot go high enough. */
    public boolean washesLightmap() {
        return isEnabled() && !"NightVision".equals(mode.get()) && gamma.get() > 1.0;
    }

    @Override protected void onEnable() { apply(); }
    @Override protected void onTick() { apply(); }

    @Override protected void onDisable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        p.setFullbright(1.0f);
        p.setNightVision(false);
    }

    private void apply() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        switch (mode.get()) {
            case "NightVision" -> {
                p.setFullbright(1.0f);
                p.setNightVision(true);
            }
            case "Both" -> {
                p.setFullbright(gamma.getF());
                p.setNightVision(true);
            }
            default -> {
                p.setFullbright(gamma.getF());
                p.setNightVision(false);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/render/HudModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.render;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.event.RenderEvent;
import dev.jsa.client.core.gui.Anim;
import dev.jsa.client.core.gui.ClickGui;
import dev.jsa.client.core.gui.GuiTheme;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Entity;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Platform;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ColorSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.version.Versions;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;

/**
 * In-game HUD for single-player / open LAN.
 * Watermark sits top-left, enabled modules slide in from the top-right,
 * and coords / FPS sit in the lower corners above the hotbar. Nothing is
 * drawn through the crosshair or across the hotbar.
 */
public class HudModule extends ToggledModule {
    private static final int HOTBAR_GAP = 44;
    private static final int HOTBAR_HALF = 100;
    private static final int LINE = 12;

    private final BoolSetting watermark = bool("Watermark", true);
    private final BoolSetting moduleList = bool("Module List", true);
    private final BoolSetting fps = bool("FPS", true);
    private final BoolSetting coords = bool("Coords", true);
    private final BoolSetting ping = bool("Ping", true);
    private final BoolSetting players = bool("Nearby Players", true);
    private final BoolSetting session = bool("Session Info", true);
    private final BoolSetting mcVersion = bool("MC Version", true);
    private final BoolSetting guiHint = bool("GUI Hint", true);
    private final NumberSetting scale = num("Padding", 4, 2, 16, 1, true);
    private final ColorSetting accent = color("Accent", GuiTheme.ACCENT);

    private final long[] clock = new long[1];
    private final Map<String, RowAnim> rows = new LinkedHashMap<>();
    private float fade;
    private float fpsShown = -1f;
    private float pingShown = -1f;
    private double xShown, yShown, zShown;
    private boolean primed;

    @Override protected void onRender(RenderEvent event) {
        if (event.isWorldPass()) return;
        renderHud(event);
    }

    /** Also callable from an adapter GL pass. */
    public void renderHud(RenderEvent event) {
        Platform p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || ClickGui.get().isOpen()) return;

        float dt = Anim.seconds(clock);
        fade = Anim.approach(fade, 1f, dt, 3.2f);
        smoothStats(p, dt);

        int pad = scale.getI();
        int accentCol = accent.get();
        int sw = event.width();
        int sh = event.height();
        float alpha = Anim.easeOut(fade);
        int topBottom = drawTopCard(event, pad, accentCol, sw, sh, alpha);
        if (moduleList.get()) drawModules(event, dt, pad, accentCol, sw, sh, alpha);
        drawStats(event, p, pad, accentCol, sw, sh, alpha, topBottom);
        if (players.get()) drawPlayers(event, p, pad, accentCol, sw, sh, alpha);
    }

    private void smoothStats(Platform p, float dt) {
        int liveFps = Math.max(0, p.getFps());
        if (fpsShown < 0f) fpsShown = liveFps;
        else fpsShown = Anim.approach(fpsShown, liveFps, dt, 7f);

        if (p.onServer()) {
            float livePing = Math.max(0, p.getPing());
            if (pingShown < 0f) pingShown = livePing;
            else pingShown = Anim.approach(pingShown, livePing, dt, 6f);
        } else {
            pingShown = -1f;
        }

        var lp = p.getLocalPlayer();
        if (lp == null) return;
        if (!primed) {
            xShown = lp.getX();
            yShown = lp.getY();
            zShown = lp.getZ();
            primed = true;
            return;
        }
        xShown = dampCoord(xShown, lp.getX(), dt);
        yShown = dampCoord(yShown, lp.getY(), dt);
        zShown = dampCoord(zShown, lp.getZ(), dt);
    }

    /** Follow the player smoothly. A teleport snaps instead of smearing. */
    private static double dampCoord(double shown, double live, float dt) {
        if (Math.abs(live - shown) > 12d) return live;
        return Anim.approach(shown, live, dt, 12f);
    }

    /** Compact top-left card. Returns the y just under it. */
    private int drawTopCard(RenderEvent event, int pad, int accentCol, int sw, int sh, float alpha) {
        List<String> lines = new ArrayList<>();
        List<Integer> colors = new ArrayList<>();
        if (watermark.get()) {
            lines.add("JSA  ·  SP / Open LAN");
            colors.add(accentCol);
        }
        if (mcVersion.get()) {
            lines.add("MC " + Versions.get().raw() + "  ·  " + Versions.get().era().label());
            colors.add(GuiTheme.TEXT_DIM);
        }
        if (session.get()) {
            Platform p = MC.get();
            String where = p != null && p.onServer() ? p.getServerName() : "Singleplayer";
            String mode = p == null ? "" : p.getGameMode();
            lines.add(where + "  ·  " + mode);
            colors.add(GuiTheme.TEXT_DIM);
        }
        if (guiHint.get()) {
            lines.add("Insert  ·  RShift  ·  RCtrl");
            colors.add(GuiTheme.TEXT_OFF);
        }
        if (lines.isEmpty()) return pad;

        int maxText = 0;
        for (String s : lines) maxText = Math.max(maxText, s.length() * 6);
        int maxW = Math.max(48, sw / 2 - HOTBAR_HALF - pad);
        int cardW = Math.min(Math.max(maxText + 16, 96), maxW);
        int x = pad + Math.round((1f - alpha) * -8f);
        int y = pad;
        // Keep the card in the top half so it cannot reach the crosshair.
        if (y + cardHeight(lines.size()) > sh / 2 - 8) return y;
        drawCard(event, x, y, cardW, lines, colors, alpha, accentCol, false);
        return y + cardHeight(lines.size());
    }

    private void drawModules(RenderEvent event, float dt, int pad, int accentCol, int sw, int sh, float alpha) {
        List<String> enabled = new ArrayList<>();
        for (var m : ModuleManager.get().getAllModules()) {
            if (m.isEnabled() && m != this) enabled.add(m.getName());
        }
        Set<String> want = new HashSet<>(enabled);
        for (String name : enabled) {
            RowAnim row = rows.computeIfAbsent(name, k -> new RowAnim());
            row.in = Anim.approach(row.in, 1f, dt, 12f);
        }
        Iterator<Map.Entry<String, RowAnim>> it = rows.entrySet().iterator();
        while (it.hasNext()) {
            Map.Entry<String, RowAnim> en = it.next();
            if (want.contains(en.getKey())) continue;
            en.getValue().in = Anim.approach(en.getValue().in, 0f, dt, 12f);
            if (en.getValue().in <= 0.02f) it.remove();
        }

        List<String> names = new ArrayList<>(rows.keySet());
        names.sort(Comparator.naturalOrder());

        int topLimit = sh / 2 - 10;
        int y = pad;
        List<String> visible = new ArrayList<>();
        int hidden = 0;
        for (String name : names) {
            if (y + LINE > topLimit || visible.size() >= 14) {
                if (rows.get(name).in > 0.15f) hidden++;
                continue;
            }
            visible.add(name);
            y += LINE + 1;
        }

        int target = pad;
        for (String name : visible) {
            RowAnim row = rows.get(name);
            if (row.y < 0f) row.y = target;
            else row.y = Anim.approach(row.y, target, dt, 14f);
            target += LINE + 1;
        }

        int minX = sw / 2 + HOTBAR_HALF;
        for (String name : visible) {
            RowAnim row = rows.get(name);
            float in = Anim.easeOut(Anim.clamp01(row.in)) * alpha;
            if (in <= 0.02f) continue;
            String shown = fit(name, sw - pad - minX - 12);
            int boxW = shown.length() * 6 + 12;
            int x = sw - pad - boxW + Math.round((1f - Anim.easeOut(Anim.clamp01(row.in))) * (boxW + 8));
            if (x < minX) x = minX;
            int iy = Math.round(row.y);
            event.fill(x, iy, boxW, LINE, Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.BG_CARD, in));
            event.fill(x + boxW - 2, iy, 2, LINE, Anim.withAlpha(accentCol, in));
            event.drawText(x + 4, iy + 2, shown, Anim.withAlpha(accentCol, in));
        }
        if (hidden > 0 && alpha > 0.2f && target + LINE <= topLimit) {
            String more = "+" + hidden;
            int boxW = more.length() * 6 + 12;
            int x = Math.max(minX, sw - pad - boxW);
            event.drawText(x + 4, target + 2, more, Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.TEXT_OFF, alpha));
        }
    }

    private void drawStats(RenderEvent event, Platform p, int pad, int accentCol,
                           int sw, int sh, float alpha, int topBottom) {
        List<String> lines = new ArrayList<>();
        List<Integer> colors = new ArrayList<>();
        if (fps.get() && fpsShown >= 0f) {
            int shown = Math.round(fpsShown);
            String line = "FPS  " + shown;
            if (ping.get() && p.onServer() && pingShown >= 0f) {
                line += "    Ping  " + Math.round(pingShown) + " ms";
            }
            lines.add(line);
            colors.add(shown >= 100 ? accentCol : GuiTheme.TEXT);
        } else if (ping.get() && p.onServer() && pingShown >= 0f) {
            lines.add("Ping  " + Math.round(pingShown) + " ms");
            colors.add(GuiTheme.TEXT);
        }
        if (coords.get() && primed) {
            lines.add(String.format("XYZ  %.1f   %.1f   %.1f", xShown, yShown, zShown));
            colors.add(GuiTheme.TEXT);
        }
        if (lines.isEmpty()) return;

        int desired = 0;
        for (String s : lines) desired = Math.max(desired, s.length() * 6 + 16);
        int leftLimit = sw / 2 - HOTBAR_HALF;
        int maxW = leftLimit - pad;
        int cardH = cardHeight(lines.size());
        if (maxW >= 72) {
            int w = Math.min(desired, maxW);
            int y = sh - HOTBAR_GAP - cardH;
            if (y > sh / 2 + 4) {
                drawCard(event, pad, y, w, lines, colors, alpha, accentCol, false);
                return;
            }
        }
        int y = topBottom + 6;
        if (y + cardH < sh / 2 - 6) {
            int w = Math.min(desired, Math.max(48, sw / 2 - 16));
            drawCard(event, pad, y, w, lines, colors, alpha, accentCol, false);
        }
    }

    private void drawPlayers(RenderEvent event, Platform p, int pad, int accentCol, int sw, int sh, float alpha) {
        List<String> lines = new ArrayList<>();
        List<Integer> colors = new ArrayList<>();
        lines.add("Nearby");
        colors.add(GuiTheme.TEXT_DIM);
        int count = 0;
        var self = p.getLocalPlayer();
        for (Entity e : p.getEntities()) {
            if (e == null || e.isDead() || !e.isPlayer()) continue;
            if (self != null && e.getId() == self.getId()) continue;
            double d = p.distanceTo(e);
            lines.add(String.format("%s   %.0fm", e.getName(), d));
            colors.add(GuiTheme.TEXT);
            if (++count >= 4) break;
        }
        if (count == 0) {
            lines.add("Nobody nearby");
            colors.add(GuiTheme.TEXT_OFF);
        }

        int desired = 0;
        for (String s : lines) desired = Math.max(desired, s.length() * 6 + 16);
        int rightStart = sw / 2 + HOTBAR_HALF;
        int maxW = sw - pad - rightStart;
        int cardH = cardHeight(lines.size());
        int roomAboveHotbar = (sh - HOTBAR_GAP) - (sh / 2 + 8);
        if (cardH > roomAboveHotbar && lines.size() > 2) {
            while (lines.size() > 2 && cardHeight(lines.size()) > roomAboveHotbar) {
                lines.remove(lines.size() - 1);
                colors.remove(colors.size() - 1);
            }
            cardH = cardHeight(lines.size());
        }
        if (maxW >= 72) {
            int w = Math.min(Math.max(desired, 88), maxW);
            int x = sw - pad - w;
            int y = sh - HOTBAR_GAP - cardH;
            if (y > sh / 2 + 4 && x >= rightStart - 2) {
                drawCard(event, x, y, w, lines, colors, alpha, accentCol, true);
            }
        }
    }

    private void drawCard(RenderEvent event, int x, int y, int w, List<String> lines, List<Integer> colors,
                          float alpha, int accentCol, boolean accentOnRight) {
        if (lines.isEmpty() || alpha < 0.02f || w < 8) return;
        int h = cardHeight(lines.size());
        event.fill(x + 1, y + 1, w, h, Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.SHADOW, alpha));
        event.fill(x, y, w, h, Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.BG_CARD, alpha));
        event.outline(x, y, w, h, Anim.withAlpha(GuiTheme.BORDER, alpha));
        event.fill(x, y, w, 2, Anim.withAlpha(accentCol, alpha));
        if (accentOnRight) event.fill(x + w - 2, y, 2, h, Anim.withAlpha(accentCol, alpha));
        else event.fill(x, y, 2, h, Anim.withAlpha(accentCol, alpha));
        int ty = y + 6;
        for (int i = 0; i < lines.size(); i++) {
            String s = fit(lines.get(i), w - 14);
            int tx = accentOnRight ? x + w - 8 - s.length() * 6 : x + 8;
            if (tx < x + 4) tx = x + 4;
            event.drawText(tx, ty, s, Anim.withAlpha(colors.get(i), alpha));
            ty += LINE;
        }
    }

    private static int cardHeight(int lines) {
        return lines * LINE + 8;
    }

    private static String fit(String s, int px) {
        int max = Math.max(1, px / 6);
        if (s.length() <= max) return s;
        if (max <= 1) return "…";
        return s.substring(0, max - 1) + "…";
    }

    private static final class RowAnim {
        float in;
        float y = -1f;
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/render/NoRenderModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.render;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;

/**
 * Hides visual clutter — fireworks spam, particles, or armor stands while you build.
 */
public class NoRenderModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final BoolSetting projectiles = bool("Projectiles", false);
    private final BoolSetting players = bool("Players", false);
    private final BoolSetting mobs = bool("Mobs", false);
    private final BoolSetting armorStands = bool("Armor Stands", false);
    private final BoolSetting particles = bool("Particles", true);
    private final BoolSetting heldItem = bool("Held Item", false);
    private final BoolSetting weather = bool("Weather", false);
    private final BoolSetting fireOverlay = bool("Fire Overlay", true);
    private final BoolSetting bossBar = bool("Boss Bar", false);
    private final BoolSetting scoreboard = bool("Scoreboard", false);

    @Override protected void onEnable() { apply(); }
    @Override protected void onTick() { apply(); }

    @Override protected void onDisable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p != null) p.setNoRenderMask(0);
    }

    private void apply() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p != null) p.setNoRenderMask(hidden());
    }

    /** Bitmask of hidden categories for the adapter. */
    public int hidden() {
        int mask = 0;
        if (projectiles.get()) mask |= 1;
        if (players.get()) mask |= 2;
        if (mobs.get()) mask |= 4;
        if (armorStands.get()) mask |= 8;
        if (particles.get()) mask |= 16;
        if (heldItem.get()) mask |= 32;
        if (weather.get()) mask |= 64;
        if (fireOverlay.get()) mask |= 128;
        if (bossBar.get()) mask |= 256;
        if (scoreboard.get()) mask |= 512;
        return mask;
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/render/SprintDecayModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.render;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;

/**
 * Keeps sprint alive briefly after you release W — smoother sprint-jumps
 * while exploring.
 */
public class SprintDecayModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final NumberSetting holdTicks = num("Hold Ticks", 8, 1, 40, 1, true);

    private int remaining;

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || p.getLocalPlayer() == null) return;

        if (p.isKeyDown('w')) {
            remaining = holdTicks.getI();
            p.sendSprint(true);
            return;
        }
        if (remaining > 0 && p.isMoving()) {
            remaining--;
            p.sendSprint(true);
        }
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/render/TracersModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.render;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.event.RenderEvent;
import dev.jsa.client.core.gui.GuiTheme;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Entity;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ColorSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;

/**
 * Lines / labels toward entities — quick "where is my brother?" compass.
 */
public class TracersModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final BoolSetting playersOnly = bool("Players Only", true);
    private final BoolSetting showDistance = bool("Show Distance", true);
    private final BoolSetting showName = bool("Show Name", true);
    private final NumberSetting maxDist = num("Max Distance", 256, 16, 512, 8, true);
    private final ColorSetting color = color("Color", ColorSetting.of(200, 160, 80, 220));
    private final ColorSetting playerColor = color("Player Color", GuiTheme.ACCENT);

    @Override protected void onRender(RenderEvent event) {
        if (!event.isWorldPass()) return;
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;

        for (Entity e : p.getEntities()) {
            if (e == null || e.isDead()) continue;
            if (p.getLocalPlayer() != null && e.getId() == p.getLocalPlayer().getId()) continue;
            if (playersOnly.get() && !e.isPlayer()) continue;
            double d = p.distanceTo(e);
            if (d > maxDist.get()) continue;

            int col = e.isPlayer() ? playerColor.get() : color.get();
            event.drawLine(e, col);
            if (!showName.get() && !showDistance.get()) continue;
            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            if (showName.get()) sb.append(e.getName());
            if (showDistance.get()) {
                if (sb.length() > 0) sb.append(" ");
                sb.append(String.format("%.0fm", d));
            }
            event.drawLabel(e, sb.toString(), col);
        }
    }
}
```

```java
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
```

```java
/**
 * Render-only FOV zoom while holding C. Prefer {@code Zoom} in Player if you
 * also want smooth restore; this one is a lightweight overlay option.
 */
public class ZoomRenderModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final NumberSetting zoom = num("Zoom", 0.4, 0.05, 1.0, 0.05, false);
    private final NumberSetting baseFov = num("Base FOV", 70, 30, 120, 1, true);

    @Override protected void onTick() {

        if (p.isKeyDown('c')) p.setFov(baseFov.getF() * zoom.getF());
        else p.setFov(baseFov.getF());
    }

    @Override protected void onDisable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p != null) p.setFov(baseFov.getF());
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/troll/AnnoyActionbarModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.troll;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.StringSetting;

/**
 * Spams silly action-bar tips - harmless HUD annoyance for co-op chaos.
 */
public class AnnoyActionbarModule extends ToggledModule {
    private static final String[] TIPS = {
            "Tip: hit your brother with a fish",
            "Remember to breathe",
            "LAN party intensity: 110%",
            "The cake is a lie",
            "Press F to pay respects",
            "Your inventory feels watched",
            "Behind you.",
            "Achievement unlocked: Patience"
    };

    private final ModeSetting mode = mode("Mode", "Tips", "Custom");
    private final StringSetting custom = str("Custom", "hehe");
    private final NumberSetting interval = num("Interval Sec", 3.0, 0.5, 30.0, 0.5, false);

    private long last;
    private int idx;

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        long wait = (long) (interval.get() * 1000L);
        if (System.currentTimeMillis() - last < wait) return;
        last = System.currentTimeMillis();
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        if ("Custom".equals(mode.get())) p.actionbar(custom.get());
        else p.actionbar(TIPS[Math.floorMod(idx++, TIPS.length)]);
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/troll/BlinkTpModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.troll;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.util.MathUtils;

/**
 * Safe client-side blink teleport joke: jump the camera briefly then snap
 * back. Does not grief the world - body restores via {@code restoreClientPos}.
 */
public class BlinkTpModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final ModeSetting mode = mode("Mode", "Up", "Forward", "Random", "Sky");
    private final NumberSetting distance = num("Distance", 8, 2, 40, 1, true);
    private final NumberSetting holdTicks = num("Hold Ticks", 25, 5, 100, 1, true);
    private final BoolSetting particles = bool("Particles", true);
    private final BoolSetting autoOff = bool("Auto Disable", true);

    private int left;
    private boolean blinked;

    @Override protected void onEnable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || p.getLocalPlayer() == null) return;
        p.saveClientPos();
        double x = p.getX(), y = p.getY(), z = p.getZ();
        float yaw = p.getYaw();
        double d = distance.get();
        switch (mode.get()) {
            case "Forward" -> {
                x += MathUtils.dirX(yaw) * d;
                z += MathUtils.dirZ(yaw) * d;
            }
            case "Sky" -> y += d * 2;
            case "Random" -> {
                x += MathUtils.rand((float) -d, (float) d);
                z += MathUtils.rand((float) -d, (float) d);
                y += MathUtils.rand(0, (float) d);
            }
            default -> y += d;
        }
        p.blinkClientPos(x, y, z);
        if (particles.get()) {
            p.spawnParticle(0, p.getX(), p.getY(), p.getZ(), 0, 0.2, 0);
        }
        p.actionbar("Blink!");
        left = holdTicks.getI();
        blinked = true;
    }

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        if (!blinked) return;
        if (left-- > 0) return;
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p != null) {
            p.restoreClientPos();
            p.actionbar("Back!");
        }
        blinked = false;
        if (autoOff.get()) {
            setEnabled(false);
            onDisable();
        }
    }

    @Override protected void onDisable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p != null && blinked) p.restoreClientPos();
        blinked = false;
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/troll/ConfettiModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.troll;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.util.MathUtils;

/**
 * Spawns a particle storm around you - celebration or "what is happening"
 * chaos on LAN.
 */
public class ConfettiModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final NumberSetting rate = num("Bursts / Sec", 4, 1, 20, 1, true);
    private final NumberSetting spread = num("Spread", 1.5, 0.3, 5.0, 0.1, false);
    private final BoolSetting sound = bool("Pop Sound", true);

    private long last;

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || p.getLocalPlayer() == null) return;
        long interval = 1000L / Math.max(1, rate.getI());
        if (System.currentTimeMillis() - last < interval) return;
        last = System.currentTimeMillis();

        double s = spread.get();
        for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
            p.spawnParticle(
                    MathUtils.randInt(0, 20),
                    p.getX() + MathUtils.rand((float) -s, (float) s),
                    p.getY() + 1 + MathUtils.rand(0, (float) s),
                    p.getZ() + MathUtils.rand((float) -s, (float) s),
                    MathUtils.rand(-0.1f, 0.1f),
                    MathUtils.rand(0.05f, 0.25f),
                    MathUtils.rand(-0.1f, 0.1f));
        }
        if (sound.get()) p.playSoundAtPlayer("entity.firework_rocket.blast", 0.35f, MathUtils.rand(0.9f, 1.3f));
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/troll/DerpModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.troll;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.util.MathUtils;

/**
 * Spins / flops your head around - classic derp for when your brother is
 * watching your screen or standing next to you on LAN.
 */
public class DerpModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final ModeSetting mode = mode("Mode", "Spin", "Nod", "Random");
    private final NumberSetting speed = num("Speed", 25, 5, 90, 1, true);
    private final BoolSetting restore = bool("Restore On Disable", true);

    private float savedYaw, savedPitch;
    private float spin;

    @Override protected void onEnable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        savedYaw = p.getYaw();
        savedPitch = p.getPitch();
        p.setDerp(true);
    }

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        spin += speed.getF();
        switch (mode.get()) {
            case "Nod" -> p.setRotation(savedYaw, (float) (Math.sin(spin / 20.0) * 60));
            case "Random" -> p.setRotation(
                    savedYaw + MathUtils.rand(-180f, 180f),
                    MathUtils.rand(-90f, 90f));
            default -> p.setRotation(spin % 360f, (float) (Math.sin(spin / 15.0) * 40));
        }
    }

    @Override protected void onDisable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        p.setDerp(false);
        if (restore.get()) p.setRotation(savedYaw, savedPitch);
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/troll/FakeChatModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.troll;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.StringSetting;

/**
 * Injects fake chat lines on your screen only - perfect for "did you see that?"
 * moments on LAN when your brother is looking over.
 */
public class FakeChatModule extends ToggledModule {
    private static final String[] PRESETS = {
            "<Herobrine> Looking at you.",
            "[Server] Your brother has left the game",
            "<Notch> Nice base.",
            "[LAN] Connection lost...",
            "<System> Achievement unlocked: Sibling Rivalry",
            "[Warning] Low TPS - lag spike incoming"
    };

    private final ModeSetting mode = mode("Mode", "Preset", "Custom", "Spam");
    private final StringSetting custom = str("Custom Line", "<Herobrine> Hi.");
    private final NumberSetting interval = num("Interval Sec", 6.0, 1.0, 60.0, 0.5, false);
    private final NumberSetting presetIndex = num("Preset #", 0, 0, PRESETS.length - 1, 1, true);

    private long last;
    private int spamIdx;

    @Override protected void onEnable() { pushOnce(); }

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        long wait = (long) (interval.get() * 1000L);
        if (System.currentTimeMillis() - last < wait) return;
        pushOnce();
    }

    private void pushOnce() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        last = System.currentTimeMillis();
        String line = switch (mode.get()) {
            case "Custom" -> custom.get();
            case "Spam" -> PRESETS[Math.floorMod(spamIdx++, PRESETS.length)];
            default -> PRESETS[Math.min(presetIndex.getI(), PRESETS.length - 1)];
        };
        p.pushFakeChat(line);
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.troll;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.StringSetting;

/**
 * Brief fake crash / error overlay - does not actually crash the game.
 * Auto-clears so you can laugh and keep playing.
 */
public class FakeCrashModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final StringSetting title = str("Title", "Minecraft has crashed!");
    private final StringSetting detail = str("Detail", "java.lang.SiblingException: too much fun");
    private final NumberSetting duration = num("Duration Ticks", 40, 15, 120, 5, true);
    private final BoolSetting autoOff = bool("Auto Disable", true);

    private int left;

    @Override protected void onEnable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        left = duration.getI();
        p.showOverlay(title.get(), detail.get(), left);
        p.playSoundAtPlayer("block.anvil.land", 0.6f, 0.5f);
    }

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        if (--left > 0) return;
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p != null) p.clearOverlay();
        if (autoOff.get()) {
            setEnabled(false);
            onDisable();
        }
    }

    @Override protected void onDisable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p != null) p.clearOverlay();
    }
}
```

```java
 * Great for screen-share jumpscares.

public class FakeDeathModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final StringSetting title = str("Title", "You Died!");
    private final StringSetting subtitle = str("Subtitle", "Score: 0");
    private final NumberSetting duration = num("Duration Ticks", 60, 20, 200, 5, true);
    private final BoolSetting sound = bool("Play Hurt Sound", true);
    private final BoolSetting autoOff = bool("Auto Disable", true);

    private int ticksLeft;

    @Override protected void onEnable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        ticksLeft = duration.getI();
        p.showOverlay(title.get(), subtitle.get(), ticksLeft);

        p.setSpoofedHealth(0, 20);

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        if (ticksLeft-- <= 0) {
            var p = MC.get();

                p.clearOverlay();
                p.setSpoofedHealth(-1, -1);
            }
            if (autoOff.get()) {
                setEnabled(false);
                onDisable();
            }
        }
    }

    @Override protected void onDisable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        p.clearOverlay();
        p.setSpoofedHealth(-1, -1);
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/troll/FakeLagModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.troll;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.util.MathUtils;

/**
 * Fake lag feel: camera shake + tiny rubber-band hops. Client-side joke for
 * "bro the LAN is dying" theater.
 */
public class FakeLagModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final ModeSetting mode = mode("Mode", "Shake", "Rubberband", "Both");
    private final NumberSetting intensity = num("Intensity", 0.4, 0.05, 2.0, 0.05, false);
    private final NumberSetting everyTicks = num("Every Ticks", 15, 5, 80, 1, true);

    private int tick;
    private double anchorX, anchorY, anchorZ;

    @Override protected void onEnable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || p.getLocalPlayer() == null) return;
        anchorX = p.getX();
        anchorY = p.getY();
        anchorZ = p.getZ();
    }

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || p.getLocalPlayer() == null) return;
        tick++;

        String m = mode.get();
        if ("Shake".equals(m) || "Both".equals(m)) {
            p.setCameraShake(intensity.getF());
        }
        if (("Rubberband".equals(m) || "Both".equals(m)) && tick % everyTicks.getI() == 0) {
            double j = intensity.get() * 0.35;
            p.setPos(
                    anchorX + MathUtils.rand((float) -j, (float) j),
                    anchorY,
                    anchorZ + MathUtils.rand((float) -j, (float) j));
            // snap back next tick feel
        }
        if (tick % 40 == 0) {
            anchorX = p.getX();
            anchorY = p.getY();
            anchorZ = p.getZ();
        }
    }

    @Override protected void onDisable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p != null) p.setCameraShake(0f);
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/troll/FakeNameModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.troll;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.StringSetting;

/**
 * Client-side display name prank — confuse your brother for thirty seconds,
 * then switch to Herobrine. Tab list may still show the real name (half the fun).
 */
public class FakeNameModule extends ToggledModule {
    private static final String[] TROLL_NAMES = {
            "Notch", "Herobrine", "Steve", "Alex", "Jeb_", "Dinnerbone",
            "Technoblade", "YourBrother", "Admin", "NotAHacker", "DefinitelySteve",
            "Ghost", "TheHost", "LAN_Legend"
    };

    private final ModeSetting mode = mode("Mode", "Custom", "Random");
    private final StringSetting name = str("Name", "Notch");
    private final NumberSetting interval = num("Cycle Sec", 4.0, 1.0, 30.0, 0.5, false);

    private int randomIndex;
    private long lastCycle;

    @Override protected void onEnable() {
        applyOnce();
    }

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;

        if ("Random".equals(mode.get())) {
            long wait = (long) (interval.get() * 1000L);
            if (System.currentTimeMillis() - lastCycle >= wait) {
                randomIndex = Math.floorMod(randomIndex + 1, TROLL_NAMES.length);
                lastCycle = System.currentTimeMillis();
                p.setDisplayName(TROLL_NAMES[randomIndex]);
            }
        } else if (!name.isEmpty()) {
            p.setDisplayName(name.get());
        }
    }

    @Override protected void onDisable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p != null) p.setDisplayName("");
    }

    private void applyOnce() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        if ("Random".equals(mode.get())) p.setDisplayName(TROLL_NAMES[0]);
        else if (!name.isEmpty()) p.setDisplayName(name.get());
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/troll/HerobrineModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.troll;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;

/**
 * Combo prank: rename to Herobrine, flicker render, occasional stare sound.
 * One toggle for the full mythic bit on LAN.
 */
public class HerobrineModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final BoolSetting rename = bool("Rename", true);
    private final BoolSetting flicker = bool("Flicker", true);
    private final BoolSetting sounds = bool("Stare Sounds", true);
    private final NumberSetting flickerRate = num("Flicker Sec", 1.2, 0.3, 4.0, 0.1, false);

    private long lastFlicker;
    private long lastSound;
    private String previousName = "";

    @Override protected void onEnable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        previousName = p.getLocalName() == null ? "" : p.getLocalName();
        if (rename.get()) p.setDisplayName("Herobrine");
        p.actionbar("...");
    }

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        if (rename.get()) p.setDisplayName("Herobrine");

        if (flicker.get()) {
            long wait = (long) (flickerRate.get() * 1000L);
            if (System.currentTimeMillis() - lastFlicker > wait) {
                lastFlicker = System.currentTimeMillis();
                p.setRenderPhase(p.getRenderPhase() == 0 ? 1 : 0);
            }
        }

        if (sounds.get() && System.currentTimeMillis() - lastSound > 12000L) {
            lastSound = System.currentTimeMillis();
            p.playSoundAtPlayer("entity.enderman.stare", 0.7f, 0.6f);
        }
    }

    @Override protected void onDisable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        p.setDisplayName(previousName);
        p.setRenderPhase(0);
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/troll/ItemDropModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.troll;

import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Item;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;

/**
 * Periodically drops a visual item stack — harmless LAN spam / celebration FX.
 * Adapter performs the real drop; this module schedules it.
 */
public class ItemDropModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final ModeSetting item = mode("Item", "Diamond", "Totem", "Cooked Beef", "TNT");
    private final NumberSetting amount = num("Amount", 1, 1, 64, 1, true);
    private final NumberSetting interval = num("Interval Ticks", 40, 5, 400, 5, true);
    private final BoolSetting onlyWhenStill = bool("Only When Still", false);

    private int tickCounter;

    @Override protected void onEnable() {
        tickCounter = 0;
        dropOnce();
    }

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        tickCounter++;
        if (tickCounter < interval.getI()) return;
        tickCounter = 0;
        dropOnce();
    }

    private void dropOnce() {
        var p = platform();
        if (p == null || p.getLocalPlayer() == null) return;
        if (onlyWhenStill.get() && p.isMoving()) return;
        p.dropItem(p.getLocalPlayer(), fakeItem(), amount.getI());
    }

    private Item fakeItem() {
        int id = switch (item.get()) {
            case "Totem" -> 321;
            case "Cooked Beef" -> 320;
            case "TNT" -> 46;
            default -> 264; // diamond
        };
        String name = item.get();
        return new Item() {
            @Override public int getId() { return id; }
            @Override public String getName() { return name; }
            @Override public boolean isFood() { return id == 320; }
            @Override public int getFoodValue() { return 8; }
            @Override public boolean isCookedFood() { return id == 320; }
            @Override public float getAttackDamage() { return 1f; }
            @Override public float getAttackSpeed() { return 1f; }
            @Override public int getDurability() { return 0; }
            @Override public int getMaxDurability() { return 0; }
            @Override public int getArmorSlot() { return -1; }
            @Override public boolean isEnderPearl() { return false; }
            @Override public boolean isTotem() { return id == 321; }
            @Override public boolean isShulkerBox() { return false; }
            @Override public boolean isAxe() { return false; }
            @Override public boolean isSword() { return false; }
            @Override public boolean isWeapon() { return false; }
        };
    }
}

```

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.troll;

 */
public class MuteTrollModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final ModeSetting category = mode("Category",
            "Master", "Music", "Record", "Weather", "Block",
            "Hostile", "Neutral", "Player", "UI", "Voice");
    private final NumberSetting volume = num("Volume", 0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 0.05, false);

    @Override protected void onEnable() { apply(); }
    @Override protected void onTick() { apply(); }

    @Override protected void onDisable() {

            case "Music" -> "music";
            case "Record" -> "record";
            case "Weather" -> "weather";
            case "Block" -> "block";
            case "Hostile" -> "hostile";
            case "Neutral" -> "neutral";
            case "Player" -> "player";
            case "UI" -> "ui";
            case "Voice" -> "voice";
            default -> "master";
        };
    }

    private void apply() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p != null) p.setSoundCategoryVolume(mcCategory(), volume.getF());
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.troll;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;

/**
 * Flicker your model for nearby players - classic "am I lagging?" prank on LAN.
 * Server still tracks you, so you can still get hit.
 */
public class PhantomTrollModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final NumberSetting rate = num("Rate Sec", 1.0, 0.2, 5.0, 0.1, false);
    private final BoolSetting ghost = bool("Ghost Bob", false);
    private final BoolSetting hint = bool("Actionbar", true);

    private long toggleTick;

    @Override protected void onEnable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p != null && hint.get()) p.actionbar("Phantom flicker on");
    }

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;

        long rateMs = (long) (rate.get() * 1000L);
        if (System.currentTimeMillis() - toggleTick > rateMs) {
            toggleTick = System.currentTimeMillis();
            p.setRenderPhase(p.getRenderPhase() == 0 ? 1 : 0);
        }
        if (ghost.get() && p.getRenderPhase() == 0) {
            p.setVelocity(p.getVelocityX(), 0.03, p.getVelocityZ());
        }
    }

    @Override protected void onDisable() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        p.setRenderPhase(0);
        if (hint.get()) p.actionbar("Phantom off");
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/troll/ScareSoundModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.troll;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ModeSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.util.MathUtils;

/**
 * Plays spooky local sounds at random intervals - creeper hiss, enderman,
 * wither, thunder. Only you (and anyone sharing headphones) hear it.
 */
public class ScareSoundModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final ModeSetting sound = mode("Sound", "Creeper", "Enderman", "Wither", "Thunder", "Ghasts", "Random");
    private final NumberSetting minSec = num("Min Sec", 8, 2, 60, 1, true);
    private final NumberSetting maxSec = num("Max Sec", 25, 5, 120, 1, true);
    private final NumberSetting volume = num("Volume", 1.0, 0.1, 2.0, 0.1, false);

    private long nextAt;

    @Override protected void onEnable() { scheduleNext(); }

    @Override protected void onTick() {
        if (System.currentTimeMillis() < nextAt) return;
        play();
        scheduleNext();
    }

    private void scheduleNext() {
        int lo = Math.min(minSec.getI(), maxSec.getI());
        int hi = Math.max(minSec.getI(), maxSec.getI());
        nextAt = System.currentTimeMillis() + MathUtils.randInt(lo, hi) * 1000L;
    }

    private void play() {
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        String pick = sound.get();
        if ("Random".equals(pick)) {
            String[] all = {"Creeper", "Enderman", "Wither", "Thunder", "Ghasts"};
            pick = all[MathUtils.randInt(0, all.length - 1)];
        }
        String id = switch (pick) {
            case "Enderman" -> "entity.enderman.stare";
            case "Wither" -> "entity.wither.spawn";
            case "Thunder" -> "entity.lightning_bolt.thunder";
            case "Ghasts" -> "entity.ghast.scream";
            default -> "entity.creeper.primed";
        };
        p.playSoundAtPlayer(id, volume.getF(), MathUtils.rand(0.85f, 1.15f));
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/module/troll/SpookyEspModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.module.troll;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.event.RenderEvent;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Entity;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.BoolSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.ColorSetting;
import dev.jsa.client.core.setting.NumberSetting;

/**
 * Creepy glowing boxes + tracers on nearby players - Herobrine vibes for LAN.
 */
public class SpookyEspModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final BoolSetting playersOnly = bool("Players Only", true);
    private final BoolSetting pulse = bool("Pulse Color", true);
    private final BoolSetting tracers = bool("Tracers", true);
    private final BoolSetting label = bool("Herobrine Label", true);
    private final NumberSetting maxDist = num("Max Distance", 96, 16, 256, 8, true);
    private final ColorSetting color = color("Color", ColorSetting.of(220, 255, 255, 255));

    private int pulseTick;

    @Override protected void onRender(RenderEvent event) {
        if (!event.isWorldPass()) return;
        var p = MC.get();
        if (p == null) return;
        pulseTick++;
        int argb = color.get();
        if (pulse.get()) {
            int a = 140 + (int) (80 * Math.abs(Math.sin(pulseTick / 12.0)));
            argb = (a << 24) | (argb & 0x00FFFFFF);
        }

        for (Entity e : p.getEntities()) {
            if (e == null || e.isDead()) continue;
            if (p.getLocalPlayer() != null && e.getId() == p.getLocalPlayer().getId()) continue;
            if (playersOnly.get() && !e.isPlayer()) continue;
            if (p.distanceTo(e) > maxDist.get()) continue;

            event.drawBox(e, argb);
            if (tracers.get()) event.drawLine(e, argb);
            if (label.get()) event.drawLabel(e, "???", argb);
        }
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/platform/Entity.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.platform;

/**
 * Version-agnostic entity abstraction. Fabric and the multi-version loader map
 * the real living entity onto this. Modules only see this interface, which keeps
 * them portable across Minecraft versions.
 */
public interface Entity {
    int getId();
    String getName();

    double getX();
    double getY();
    double getZ();

    float getHealth();
    float getMaxHealth();
    float getHurtTime();          // ticks since last hit (for flash rendering)
    int getArmorValue();

    boolean isPlayer();
    boolean isMob();
    boolean isArmorStand();
    boolean isBlocking();
    boolean isDead();
    boolean isSneaking();
    boolean isSprinting();

    double getMotionX();
    double getMotionY();
    double getMotionZ();

    /** Distance (squared-ish, plain euclidean) to the local player. */
    default double distanceTo(double px, double py, double pz) {
        double dx = getX() - px, dy = getY() - py, dz = getZ() - pz;
        return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz);
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.platform;

/**
 * Version-agnostic packet handle. Adapters wrap the real network packet and
 * report a stable {@link #typeName()} modules can key on
 * (e.g. {@code "PlayerPositionC2SPacket.PosRot"}).
 */
public interface Packet {
    String typeName();

    default int entityId() { return -1; }

    default double velocityX() { return 0; }
    default double velocityY() { return 0; }
    default double velocityZ() { return 0; }

    boolean toServer();
}
```
