`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/platform/Platform.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.platform;

import dev.jsa.client.core.event.RenderEvent;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * The single contract modules depend on. A {@link Platform} is supplied by
 * the adapter (Fabric for 1.20.1, or the jar-injecting loader). Everything
 * version-specific lives behind this interface so {@code core} stays
 * version-agnostic.
 *
 * <p>Built for single-player and open LAN — packet helpers exist so movement
 * and combat QoL modules can talk to the integrated / LAN server cleanly,
 * not to dodge anti-cheat.</p>
 *
 * <p>Conventions:</p>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>Position/motion getters return the <em>local player's</em> values.</li>
 *   <li>{@code slot} is the 0-8 hotbar index unless stated otherwise.</li>
 *   <li>Packet helpers are no-ops on adapters that cannot identify the packet.</li>
 * </ul>
 */
public interface Platform {

    // -- context -------------------------------------------------------------
    boolean inGame();
    boolean onServer();
    String getServerName();
    int getPing();

    Entity getLocalPlayer();
    World getWorld();

    int getTick();
    long nanoTime();
    float getPartialTick();

    // -- position / motion ---------------------------------------------------
    double getX();
    double getY();
    double getZ();
    void setPos(double x, double y, double z);

    double getMotionX();
    double getMotionY();
    double getMotionZ();
    void setMotion(double x, double y, double z);
    void addMotion(double x, double y, double z);

    boolean isOnGround();
    void setOnGround(boolean onGround);

    // -- rotation ------------------------------------------------------------
    float getYaw();
    float getPitch();
    void setRotation(float yaw, float pitch);

    // -- movement state ------------------------------------------------------
    boolean isSprinting();
    void setSprinting(boolean sprinting);
    boolean isSneaking();
    void setSneaking(boolean sneaking);
    boolean isUsingItem();
    boolean isMoving();
    boolean isOnLadder();
    boolean isInWater();
    boolean isInLava();

    void swingHand(boolean mainHand);
    void openInventory();
    void closeInventory();

    // -- inventory -----------------------------------------------------------
    int getHeldSlot();
    void setHeldSlot(int slot);
    int getOffhandSlot();

    Item getItem(int slot);
    void setItem(int slot, Item item);
    Item getHeldItem();

    /** First hotbar slot [from..to] holding an item with the same id, else -1. */
    int findItemSlot(int from, int to, Item item);
    int findFoodSlot(int from, int to);
    int findWeaponSlot(int from, int to);
    int findArmorSlot(int armorSlot, Item item);
    int findBlockSlot(int from, int to, int blockId);

    // -- entities ------------------------------------------------------------
    List<Entity> getEntities();
    List<Entity> getEntitiesInRadius(double radius);
    boolean canSee(Entity e);
    boolean canSeePos(double x, double y, double z);

    // -- blocks --------------------------------------------------------------
    int getBlockId(double x, double y, double z);
    boolean isAir(double x, double y, double z);
    boolean canPlace(double x, double y, double z);
    void placeBlock(double x, double y, double z, int face);

    // -- sound / particles ---------------------------------------------------
    void playSound(String name, double x, double y, double z, float volume, float pitch);
    void playSoundAtPlayer(String name, float volume, float pitch);
    void spawnParticle(int particleId, double x, double y, double z, double vx, double vy, double vz);

    // -- chat ----------------------------------------------------------------
    void chat(String msg);
    void serverMessage(String msg);
    void actionbar(String msg);

    // -- vitals --------------------------------------------------------------
    float getHealth();
    int getFoodLevel();
    boolean isDead();
    String getLocalName();

    // -- packet hooks (movement / combat QoL) --------------------------------
    void sendPacket(Packet p);
    boolean isPacketPositionC2S(Packet p);
    boolean isPacketLookC2S(Packet p);
    boolean isPacketVelocityS2C(Packet p);
    boolean isPacketSprintC2S(Packet p);
    boolean isPacketRespawnS2C(Packet p);
    void setPacketPositionY(Packet p, double y);
    void setPacketOnGround(Packet p, boolean onGround);
    void setPacketVelocity(Packet p, double x, double y, double z);

    // -- packet factories ----------------------------------------------------
    /** Send a position-only C2S with the given onGround flag. */
    void sendPositionPacket(boolean onGround);
    /** Send a full position C2S (x/y/z + onGround). */
    void sendPositionPacket(double x, double y, double z, boolean onGround, boolean hasX, boolean hasY);
    /** Send a look (yaw/pitch) C2S. */
    void sendLookPacket(float yaw, float pitch);
    /** Client command: sneak on/off. */
    void sendSneak(boolean sneaking);
    /** Client command: sprint on/off. */
    void sendSprint(boolean sprinting);
    /** Override reported ping (debug / HUD only). */
    void spoofPing(int ping);
    /** Send a packet after {@code delayTicks} client ticks (0 = ASAP). */
    void queuePacket(Packet p, int delayTicks);

    // -- combat --------------------------------------------------------------
    void attack(Entity target);
    float getAttackCooldown();
    void setAttackCooldown(float v);
    double distanceTo(Entity e);

    default double distanceTo(double px, double py, double pz) {
        double dx = getX() - px, dy = getY() - py, dz = getZ() - pz;
        return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz);
    }

    /** Shared render event for modules that draw outside the dispatch path. */
    default RenderEvent getRenderEvent() { return RenderEvent.get(); }

    /** Drop an item stack at the local player (adapter sends the drop packet). */
    default void dropItem(Entity e, Item item, int amount) {
        // Adapters override; default is a no-op stub for unit tests.
    }

    boolean isCreative();

    // -- speed / use ---------------------------------------------------------
    float getTimer();
    void setTimer(float multiplier);
    void setUseItemSpeed(float speed);
    void setEating(boolean eating);
    boolean isJumping();

    // -- armor ---------------------------------------------------------------
    Item getArmorItem(int armorSlot);
    void equipFromHotbar(int hotbarSlot, int armorSlot);

    // -- misc state ----------------------------------------------------------
    void respawn();
    String getGameMode();
    void setRenderDistance(int blocks);
    int getRenderDistance();
    float getFov();
    void setFov(float fov);
    void setFullbright(float gamma);
    void setNightVision(boolean on);
    boolean isInventoryOpen();

    // -- container -----------------------------------------------------------
    int getContainerSize();
    void shiftClickFromContainer(int slot);

    // -- blocks (XRay / AutoTool) --------------------------------------------
    java.util.List<int[]> getBlockIdsInRadius(double radius);
    void breakBlock(double x, double y, double z);
    int getBlockIdAtCrosshair();

    // -- prank helpers -------------------------------------------------------
    void setSoundCategoryVolume(String category, float volume);
    void setDisplayName(String name);
    void setSpoofedHealth(double hp, double max);
    void setFakeOffhand(boolean on);

    // -- motion (module-facing names) ----------------------------------------
    void setVelocity(double x, double y, double z);
    double getVelocityX();
    double getVelocityY();
    double getVelocityZ();
    boolean isGrounded();
    void setFlying(boolean flying);
    void setJumping(boolean jumping);

    // -- input ---------------------------------------------------------------
    boolean isKeyDown(char key);
    boolean isShiftDown();
    boolean isAttacking();
    boolean isEating();
    boolean isBlocking();
    boolean isGliding();
    void setNoClip(boolean enabled);

    // -- vitals (module names) -----------------------------------------------
    int getHunger();
    void setHunger(int level);
    /**
     * Live client FPS. Default uses {@link dev.jsa.client.core.util.FpsCounter}
     * (updated every HUD/world render). Adapters may prefer Minecraft's counter.
     */
    default int getFps() {
        return dev.jsa.client.core.util.FpsCounter.getInstance().get();
    }

    // -- hotbar / items ------------------------------------------------------
    Item getHotbarItem(int slot);
    int getHotbarSlot();
    void setHotbarSlot(int slot);
    Item getOffhandItem();
    void swapOffhand();
    int findPlaceableSlot();
    int bestToolSlot(int blockId);
    void dyeArmor(int armorSlot, int color);

    // -- blocks / placement --------------------------------------------------
    int getBlockIdAt(double x, double y, double z);
    void placeBlockAt(double x, double y, double z, int face);
    void setStepHeight(float height);

    // -- chat ----------------------------------------------------------------
    void sendMessage(String msg, boolean actionBar);

    // -- render phase (Phantom prank) ----------------------------------------
    int getRenderPhase();
    void setRenderPhase(int phase);

    // -- arm swing -----------------------------------------------------------
    void swingArm();

    // -- LAN prank helpers (defaults = no-op / chat fallback) ----------------
    /** Full-screen overlay (fake death, fake crash). ticks = lifetime. */
    default void showOverlay(String title, String subtitle, int ticks) {
        actionbar(title + (subtitle == null || subtitle.isEmpty() ? "" : " - " + subtitle));
    }
    default void clearOverlay() {}

    /** Client-only camera shake intensity (0 = off). */
    default void setCameraShake(float intensity) {}

    /** Flip the local camera upside-down (Dinnerbone joke). */
    default void setScreenFlip(boolean flip) {}

    /** Inject a client-side chat line (not sent to the server). */
    default void pushFakeChat(String line) { chat(line); }

    /** Spoof HUD inventory as empty / full of junk. */
    default void setSpoofedInventoryMode(String mode) {} // "off" | "empty" | "junk"

    /** Soft client-only blink teleport; restore with {@link #restoreClientPos()}. */
    default void blinkClientPos(double x, double y, double z) { setPos(x, y, z); }
    default void saveClientPos() {}
    default void restoreClientPos() {}

    /** Spin / derp the local head for onlookers sharing your screen. */
    default void setDerp(boolean on) {}

    /** Detected Minecraft version string from the adapter ("1.20.1", "unknown"). */
    default String getMinecraftVersion() { return "unknown"; }

    /** Adapter family: "fabric", "loader", "mock". */
    default String getAdapterId() { return "unknown"; }

    // -- ClickGUI / input ----------------------------------------------------
    /** Adapters bind this to Right Shift (or a custom key). */
    default boolean isGuiToggleKeyDown() { return false; }

    default int getMouseX() { return 0; }
    default int getMouseY() { return 0; }
    /** 0 = LMB, 1 = RMB, 2 = MMB */
    default boolean isMouseButtonDown(int button) { return false; }
    default int getScreenWidth() { return 854; }
    default int getScreenHeight() { return 480; }

    // -- module effect sinks (adapters apply; defaults store nothing) --------
    default void setEntityReach(double blocks) {}
    default void setBlockReach(double blocks) {}
    default double getEntityReach() { return 3.0; }
    default double getBlockReach() { return 4.5; }

    /** Ambient occlusion: 0 off / 1 smooth / 2 max. */
    default void setAmbientOcclusion(int level) {}

    /** Bitmask from {@code NoRenderModule#hidden()}. */
    default void setNoRenderMask(int mask) {}
}
```

```java
        super(name, defaultValue);
    }
```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/setting/ColorSetting.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.setting;

/** RGB color, 0xAARRGGBB (ESP boxes, HUD, watermarks). */
public class ColorSetting extends Setting<Integer> {
    public ColorSetting(String name, int defaultValue) {
        super(name, defaultValue);
    }

    public int get() { return getValue(); }

    public int alpha() { return (getValue() >> 24) & 0xFF; }
    public int red()   { return (getValue() >> 16) & 0xFF; }
    public int green() { return (getValue() >> 8) & 0xFF; }
    public int blue()  { return getValue() & 0xFF; }

    public static int of(int alpha, int r, int g, int b) {
        return ((alpha & 0xFF) << 24) | ((r & 0xFF) << 16) | ((g & 0xFF) << 8) | (b & 0xFF);
    }

    @Override public String toString() {
        return String.format("#%08X", getValue());
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/setting/ModeSetting.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.setting;

/** Named list of modes; modules branch on {@link #get()} strings. */
public class ModeSetting extends Setting<String> {
    private final String[] modes;

    public ModeSetting(String name, String[] modes, String defaultValue) {
        super(name, defaultValue);
        this.modes = modes.clone();
    }

    public String get() { return getValue(); }
    public String[] modes() { return modes.clone(); }

    public boolean is(String mode) { return getValue().equalsIgnoreCase(mode); }

    public void set(String mode) {
        for (String m : modes) if (m.equalsIgnoreCase(mode)) { setValue(m); return; }
    }

    public void cycle() {
        int i = 0;
        for (int k = 0; k < modes.length; k++) {
            if (modes[k].equalsIgnoreCase(getValue())) i = (k + 1) % modes.length;
        }
        setValue(modes[i]);
    }

    @Override public String toString() { return getValue(); }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/setting/NumberSetting.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.setting;

/**
 * Bounded number with min/max. Integer steps snap; fractional steps are kept
 * as given (e.g. timer speed 1.0-2.5 step 0.1).
 */
public class NumberSetting extends Setting<Double> {
    private final double min;
    private final double max;
    private final double step;
    private final boolean integer;

    public NumberSetting(String name, double defaultValue, double min, double max, double step, boolean integer) {
        super(name, defaultValue);
        this.min = min;
        this.max = max;
        this.step = step;
        this.integer = integer;
    }

    public double get() { return getValue(); }
    public float getF() { return getValue().floatValue(); }
    public int getI() { return getValue().intValue(); }

    public void set(double v) {
        double clamped = Math.max(min, Math.min(max, v));
        if (step > 0) {
            double snapped = Math.round(clamped / step) * step;
            clamped = Math.max(min, Math.min(max, snapped));
        }
        setValue(clamped);
    }

    public double min() { return min; }
    public double max() { return max; }
    public double step() { return step; }
    public boolean isInteger() { return integer; }

    @Override public String toString() {
        return integer ? String.valueOf(getI()) : String.valueOf(get());
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/setting/Setting.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.setting;

/**
 * A user-tunable value attached to a module. Values are persisted by the
 * config system; the typed accessors in AbstractModule delegate here.
 */
public abstract class Setting<T> {
    private final String name;
    private final T defaultValue;
    private T value;

    protected Setting(String name, T defaultValue) {
        this.name = name;
        this.defaultValue = defaultValue;
        this.value = defaultValue;
    }

    public String getName() { return name; }
    public T getValue() { return value; }

    public void setValue(T value) { this.value = value; }

    /** Resets to the compiled-in default (used by config reload / reset command). */
    public void reset() { this.value = defaultValue; }

    public T defaultValue() { return defaultValue; }

    @Override public String toString() { return value + ""; }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/util/FpsCounter.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.util;

/**
 * Real-time FPS sampler. Call {@link #frame()} once per rendered frame;
 * {@link #get()} returns frames counted over the last ~1 second.
 */
public final class FpsCounter {
    private static final FpsCounter INSTANCE = new FpsCounter();

    public static FpsCounter getInstance() { return INSTANCE; }

    private int frames;
    private int fps;
    private long windowStartNs = System.nanoTime();

    private FpsCounter() {}

    /** Record one rendered frame. */
    public void frame() {
        frames++;
        long now = System.nanoTime();
        long elapsed = now - windowStartNs;
        if (elapsed >= 1_000_000_000L) {
            fps = (int) Math.round(frames * 1_000_000_000.0 / elapsed);
            frames = 0;
            windowStartNs = now;
        }
    }

    /** Last measured FPS (0 until the first full second). */
    public int get() { return fps; }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/util/MathUtils.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.util;

import java.util.Random;

/** Shared math helpers for movement and combat modules. */
public final class MathUtils {
    private static final Random RNG = new Random();

    private MathUtils() {}

    public static double clamp(double v, double min, double max) {
        return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, v));
    }

    public static float clamp(float v, float min, float max) {
        return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, v));
    }

    /** Random float in [min, max). */
    public static float rand(float min, float max) {
        return min + RNG.nextFloat() * (max - min);
    }

    /** Random int in [min, max] inclusive. */
    public static int randInt(int min, int max) {
        return min + RNG.nextInt(max - min + 1);
    }

    /**
     * Rotate {@code from} toward {@code to} by at most {@code step} degrees
     * along the shortest arc.
     */
    public static float lerpAngle(float from, float to, double step) {
        float diff = ((to - from + 540f) % 360f) - 180f;
        if (Math.abs(diff) <= step) return to;
        return from + (diff > 0 ? (float) step : -(float) step);
    }

    public static double distH(double x1, double z1, double x2, double z2) {
        double dx = x1 - x2, dz = z1 - z2;
        return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz);
    }

    public static double dist(double x1, double y1, double z1, double x2, double y2, double z2) {
        double dx = x1 - x2, dy = y1 - y2, dz = z1 - z2;
        return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz);
    }

    /** Horizontal unit direction for a yaw (MC: -90 = north). */
    public static double dirX(float yawDeg) { return -Math.sin(Math.toRadians(yawDeg)); }
    public static double dirZ(float yawDeg) { return Math.cos(Math.toRadians(yawDeg)); }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/util/RotationUtils.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.util;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Entity;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Platform;

/** Yaw/pitch helpers. Eye height 1.62; aim point mid-body (y + 0.5). */
public final class RotationUtils {
    private RotationUtils() {}

    public static float[] to(double x, double y, double z) {
        Platform p = MC.get();
        double dx = x - p.getX();
        double dy = (y + 0.5) - (p.getY() + 1.62);
        double dz = z - p.getZ();
        double h = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz);
        float yaw = (float) Math.toDegrees(Math.atan2(dz, dx)) - 90f;
        float pitch = (float) -Math.toDegrees(Math.atan2(dy, h));
        return new float[]{yaw, pitch};
    }

    public static float[] to(Entity e) {
        return to(e.getX(), e.getY() + 0.5, e.getZ());
    }

    public static void snapTo(Entity e) {
        float[] r = to(e);
        MC.get().setRotation(r[0], r[1]);
    }

    public static void snap(float yaw, float pitch) {
        MC.get().setRotation(yaw, pitch);
    }

    /** Smooth rotation toward an entity by at most {@code step} degrees. */
    public static void smoothTo(Entity e, double step) {
        Platform p = MC.get();
        float[] r = to(e);
        p.setRotation(
                MathUtils.lerpAngle(p.getYaw(), r[0], step),
                MathUtils.lerpAngle(p.getPitch(), r[1], step));
    }

    public static boolean facing(Entity e, double deg) {
        float[] r = to(e);
        float dyaw = Math.abs(MathUtils.lerpAngle(MC.get().getYaw(), r[0], 360) - MC.get().getYaw());
        float dpitch = Math.abs(MathUtils.lerpAngle(MC.get().getPitch(), r[1], 90) - MC.get().getPitch());
        return dyaw <= deg && dpitch <= deg;
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/util/TargetUtils.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.util;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Entity;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Platform;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;

/** Entity selection for KillAura & co. */
public final class TargetUtils {
    private TargetUtils() {}

    /**
     * All alive, targetable entities within {@code range} (wall-aware:
     * entities behind a wall need line-of-sight or the wallRange rule).
     */
    public static List<Entity> targetable(double range, double wallRange,
                                          boolean players, boolean mobs,
                                          boolean armorStands, boolean throughGlass) {
        Platform p = MC.get();
        if (p == null || p.getLocalPlayer() == null) return List.of();
        List<Entity> out = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Entity e : p.getEntities()) {
            if (e == null || e.isDead()) continue;
            if (e.getId() == p.getLocalPlayer().getId()) continue;

            boolean isPlayer = e.isPlayer();
            boolean isStand = e.isArmorStand();
            if (isPlayer && !players) continue;
            if (isStand && !armorStands) continue;
            if (!isPlayer && !isStand && !mobs) continue;

            double d = p.distanceTo(e);
            if (d > range) continue;
            if (!p.canSee(e)) {
                if (!throughGlass) continue;
                if (d > wallRange && wallRange > 0) continue;
            }
            out.add(e);
        }
        return out;
    }

    /** Pick the best target per the KillAura switch mode. */
    public static Entity pick(List<Entity> targets, String switchMode) {
        if (targets.isEmpty()) return null;
        Platform p = MC.get();
        return switch (switchMode) {
            case "Health" -> targets.stream()
                    .min(Comparator.comparingDouble(Entity::getHealth)).orElse(null);
            case "ClosestAngle" -> targets.stream()
                    .min(Comparator.comparingDouble(e -> MathUtils.lerpAngle(p.getYaw(), RotationUtils.to(e)[0], 360)))
                    .orElse(null);
            default -> targets.stream()
                    .min(Comparator.comparingDouble(p::distanceTo)).orElse(null);
        };
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/version/MinecraftEra.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.version;

/**
 * Coarse Minecraft eras from {@code 1.6.4} through {@code 26.3}.
 * One reflective adapter covers all eras; this enum drives class-name picks
 * and {@link VersionCapabilities} honesty — not a separate jar per patch.
 */
public enum MinecraftEra {
    /** 1.6.4 — last of the pre-1.7 world format. MCP client names. */
    LEGACY_1_6(1, 6, 4, 1, 6, 4, "1.6.4", "Pre-1.7 legacy (MCP)"),

    /** 1.7.2 – 1.7.10 — resource packs, new launcher era. */
    RELEASE_1_7(1, 7, 0, 1, 7, 10, "1.7.x", "1.7 resource-pack era"),

    /** 1.8 – 1.8.9 — spectator, block IDs still numeric. */
    RELEASE_1_8(1, 8, 0, 1, 8, 9, "1.8.x", "1.8 spectator / combat-classic"),

    /** 1.9 – 1.12.2 — combat update (offhand, cooldown, elytra). */
    COMBAT_1_9(1, 9, 0, 1, 12, 2, "1.9-1.12", "Combat update through World of Color"),

    /** 1.13 – 1.13.2 — Flattening (string IDs, waterlogged). */
    FLATTENING_1_13(1, 13, 0, 1, 13, 2, "1.13.x", "The Flattening"),

    /** 1.14 – 1.15.2 — Yarn {@code MinecraftClient}, villages & pillagers. */
    YARN_1_14(1, 14, 0, 1, 15, 2, "1.14-1.15", "Village & Pillage / Buzzy Bees"),

    /** 1.16 – 1.16.5 — Nether update; {@code world.level} still absent. */
    NETHER_1_16(1, 16, 0, 1, 16, 5, "1.16.x", "Nether Update"),

    /** 1.17 – 1.17.1 — Java 16+, Mojmap {@code world.level.*} packages. */
    CAVES_1_17(1, 17, 0, 1, 17, 1, "1.17.x", "Caves & Cliffs Pt1 / Java 16"),

    /** 1.18 – 1.18.2 — tall worlds (-64..320). */
    TALL_1_18(1, 18, 0, 1, 18, 2, "1.18.x", "Caves & Cliffs Pt2 (tall world)"),

    /** 1.19 – 1.19.4 — Deep Dark / chat signing era. */
    WILD_1_19(1, 19, 0, 1, 19, 4, "1.19.x", "The Wild Update"),

    /** 1.20 – 1.20.6 — Trails & Tales / 1.20.x line. */
    TRAILS_1_20(1, 20, 0, 1, 20, 6, "1.20.x", "Trails & Tales"),

    /** 1.21 – 1.25.x — Tricky Trials and follow-ups under classic numbering. */
    TRIALS_1_21(1, 21, 0, 1, 25, 99, "1.21-1.25", "1.21+ classic numbering"),

    /** 1.26 – 1.99 — classic {@code 1.x} past 1.25 until any calendar cutover. */
    CLASSIC_1_26(1, 26, 0, 1, 99, 99, "1.26+", "Classic 1.26+ (pre-calendar)"),

    /**
     * Post-1.x numbering (e.g. {@code 26.3}) when Mojang drops the leading
     * {@code 1.} — treated as newer than any {@code 1.x} release.
     */
    CALENDAR_26(26, 0, 0, 26, 3, 99, "26.0-26.3", "Post-1.x / calendar-style releases"),

    /** Outside the supported window or unparsed. */
    UNKNOWN(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, "unknown", "Unrecognized / unsupported");

    private final int minMaj, minMin, minPat;
    private final int maxMaj, maxMin, maxPat;
    private final String label;
    private final String description;

    MinecraftEra(int minMaj, int minMin, int minPat,
                 int maxMaj, int maxMin, int maxPat,
                 String label, String description) {
        this.minMaj = minMaj; this.minMin = minMin; this.minPat = minPat;
        this.maxMaj = maxMaj; this.maxMin = maxMin; this.maxPat = maxPat;
        this.label = label;
        this.description = description;
    }

    public String label() { return label; }
    public String description() { return description; }

    public boolean contains(VersionInfo v) {
        if (v == null || this == UNKNOWN) return this == UNKNOWN;
        int k = v.orderKey();
        return k >= key(minMaj, minMin, minPat) && k <= key(maxMaj, maxMin, maxPat);
    }

    public static MinecraftEra of(VersionInfo v) {
        if (v == null || v.orderKey() == 0) return UNKNOWN;
        for (MinecraftEra era : values()) {
            if (era != UNKNOWN && era.contains(v)) return era;
        }
        return UNKNOWN;
    }

    /** Inclusive supported window for this project. */
    public static boolean isSupported(VersionInfo v) {
        if (v == null || v.orderKey() == 0) return false;
        int k = v.orderKey();
        return k >= key(1, 6, 4) && k <= key(26, 3, 99);
    }

    private static int key(int maj, int min, int pat) {
        return maj * 1_000_000 + min * 1_000 + pat;
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/version/ModuleGates.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.version;

import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Platform;

/**
 * Shared graceful-degradation helpers. Modules call these instead of crashing
 * when a feature does not exist on the running era (e.g. elytra on 1.6.4).
 */
public final class ModuleGates {
    private ModuleGates() {}

    public static VersionCapabilities caps() { return Versions.caps(); }

    /** @return true if allowed; otherwise shows a one-shot actionbar and false. */
    public static boolean require(boolean allowed, String featureLabel) {
        if (allowed) return true;
        Platform p = MC.get();
        if (p != null) {
            p.actionbar(featureLabel + " needs " + hint(featureLabel)
                    + " (you are on " + Versions.get().raw() + ")");
        }
        return false;
    }

    public static boolean requireElytra() {
        return require(caps().canUseElytraFly(), "ElytraFly");
    }

    public static boolean requireOffhandTotem() {
        return require(caps().canUseAutoTotem(), "AutoTotem");
    }

    public static boolean requireAttackCooldown() {
        return require(caps().canUseAttackCooldownHold(), "Hold/Cooldown");
    }

    public static boolean requireOffhand() {
        return require(caps().canUseOffhandSwap(), "Offhand");
    }

    private static String hint(String feature) {
        return switch (feature) {
            case "ElytraFly" -> "MC 1.9+";
            case "AutoTotem" -> "MC 1.11+ (offhand)";
            case "Hold/Cooldown" -> "MC 1.9+ combat";
            case "Offhand" -> "MC 1.9+";
            default -> "a newer Minecraft";
        };
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/version/VersionCapabilities.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.version;

/**
 * Honest feature flags for SP/open-LAN modules across 1.6.4 – 26.3.
 * Modules must degrade (no-op / message) when a flag is false — never assume
 * modern APIs on legacy clients.
 */
public final class VersionCapabilities {
    private final VersionInfo info;
    private final MinecraftEra era;

    public VersionCapabilities(VersionInfo info) {
        this.info = info == null ? VersionInfo.unknown("unknown") : info;
        this.era = this.info.era();
    }

    public VersionInfo info() { return info; }
    public MinecraftEra era() { return era; }

    public boolean supported() { return info.inSupportedRange(); }

    // ---- movement / player ------------------------------------------------
    /** Creative-style flight flag (usable ~1.8+; unreliable on 1.6). */
    public boolean hasCreativeFlightFlag() { return info.atLeast(1, 8, 0); }

    /** Elytra glide (1.9+). */
    public boolean hasElytra() { return info.atLeast(1, 9, 0); }

    /** Offhand slot (1.9+). */
    public boolean hasOffhand() { return info.atLeast(1, 9, 0); }

    /** Attack cooldown / 1.9 combat (1.9+). */
    public boolean hasAttackCooldown() { return info.atLeast(1, 9, 0); }

    /** Shields (1.9+). */
    public boolean hasShields() { return info.atLeast(1, 9, 0); }

    /** Totems of undying (1.11+). */
    public boolean hasTotems() { return info.atLeast(1, 11, 0); }

    /** Swim / crawl (1.13+). */
    public boolean hasSwim() { return info.atLeast(1, 13, 0); }

    /** Open to LAN (all supported; present since classic multiplayer host). */
    public boolean hasOpenToLan() { return supported() || info.isKnown(); }

    // ---- world / blocks ---------------------------------------------------
    /** Numeric block IDs (pre-flattening). */
    public boolean hasNumericBlockIds() { return info.atMost(1, 12, 2); }

    /** String / namespaced block IDs (1.13+). */
    public boolean hasFlattenedIds() { return info.atLeast(1, 13, 0); }

    /** Nether update biomes / ancient debris (1.16+). */
    public boolean hasNetherUpdate() { return info.atLeast(1, 16, 0); }

    /** Tall world height -64..320 (1.18+). */
    public boolean hasTallWorld() { return info.atLeast(1, 18, 0); }

    // ---- mappings / runtime -----------------------------------------------
    /** Yarn-style {@code MinecraftClient} class (1.14+ intermediary/yarn). */
    public boolean hasYarnClientName() { return info.atLeast(1, 14, 0); }

    /** Mojmap {@code net.minecraft.world.level} packages (1.17+). */
    public boolean hasWorldLevelPackages() { return info.atLeast(1, 17, 0); }

    /** Needs Java 16+ runtime (1.17+). */
    public boolean requiresJava16() { return info.atLeast(1, 17, 0); }

    /** Needs Java 17+ runtime (1.18+ typically). */
    public boolean requiresJava17() { return info.atLeast(1, 18, 0); }

    /** Needs Java 21+ runtime (1.20.5+ / modern). */
    public boolean requiresJava21() { return info.atLeast(1, 20, 5) || info.major() >= 21; }

    /** Post-1.x calendar numbering (major &gt;= 26). */
    public boolean isCalendarNumbering() { return info.major() >= 26; }

    // ---- module gates -----------------------------------------------------
    public boolean canUseElytraFly() { return hasElytra(); }
    public boolean canUseAutoTotem() { return hasOffhand() && hasTotems(); }
    public boolean canUseOffhandSwap() { return hasOffhand(); }
    public boolean canUseAttackCooldownHold() { return hasAttackCooldown(); }
    public boolean canUseSwimHelpers() { return hasSwim(); }

    /** Human-readable summary for HUD / loader CLI. */
    public String summary() {
        return "era=" + era.label()
                + " supported=" + supported()
                + " offhand=" + hasOffhand()
                + " elytra=" + hasElytra()
                + " cooldown=" + hasAttackCooldown()
                + " flat=" + hasFlattenedIds()
                + " tall=" + hasTallWorld()
                + " mojmapLevel=" + hasWorldLevelPackages();
    }

    @Override public String toString() { return "caps{" + summary() + "}"; }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/version/VersionInfo.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.version;

/**
 * Parsed Minecraft version. Adapters fill this via {@link VersionProbe} so
 * modules branch on capabilities without importing version-specific classes.
 *
 * <p>Supported window: <b>1.6.4</b> through <b>26.3</b> (inclusive), covering
 * classic {@code 1.x.y} and post-1.x calendar-style numbers like {@code 26.3}.</p>
 */
public final class VersionInfo {
    public static final VersionInfo MIN_SUPPORTED = new VersionInfo("1.6.4", 1, 6, 4, "range");
    public static final VersionInfo MAX_SUPPORTED = new VersionInfo("26.3", 26, 3, 0, "range");

    private final String raw;
    private final int major;
    private final int minor;
    private final int patch;
    private final String adapterId;

    public VersionInfo(String raw, int major, int minor, int patch, String adapterId) {
        this.raw = raw == null ? "unknown" : raw;
        this.major = major;
        this.minor = minor;
        this.patch = patch;
        this.adapterId = adapterId == null ? "unknown" : adapterId;
    }

    public static VersionInfo unknown(String adapterId) {
        return new VersionInfo("unknown", 0, 0, 0, adapterId);
    }

    public String raw() { return raw; }
    public int major() { return major; }
    public int minor() { return minor; }
    public int patch() { return patch; }
    public String adapterId() { return adapterId; }

    /** Ordering key: 1.20.1 -> 1_020_001, 26.3 -> 26_003_000. */
    public int orderKey() {
        return major * 1_000_000 + minor * 1_000 + patch;
    }

    public boolean atLeast(int maj, int min, int pat) {
        return orderKey() >= (maj * 1_000_000 + min * 1_000 + pat);
    }

    public boolean atMost(int maj, int min, int pat) {
        return orderKey() <= (maj * 1_000_000 + min * 1_000 + pat);
    }

    public boolean isKnown() { return orderKey() != 0; }

    public MinecraftEra era() { return MinecraftEra.of(this); }

    public boolean inSupportedRange() { return MinecraftEra.isSupported(this); }

    @Override public String toString() {
        return raw + " [" + era().label() + "] via " + adapterId;
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.version;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Canonical smoke-check matrix for every era from 1.6.4 through 26.3.
 * Used by unit tests and {@code LoaderMain --matrix}.
 */
public final class VersionMatrix {
    private VersionMatrix() {}

    /** Representative releases covering each {@link MinecraftEra}. */
    public static List<String> representativeReleases() {
        List<String> list = new ArrayList<>();
        list.add("1.6.4");
        list.add("1.7.10");
        list.add("1.8.9");
        list.add("1.9.4");
        list.add("1.10.2");
        list.add("1.11.2");
        list.add("1.12.2");
        list.add("1.13.2");
        list.add("1.14.4");
        list.add("1.15.2");
        list.add("1.16.5");
        list.add("1.17.1");
        list.add("1.18.2");
        list.add("1.19.4");
        list.add("1.20.1");
        list.add("1.20.4");
        list.add("1.20.6");
        list.add("1.21.0");
        list.add("1.21.3");
        list.add("1.21.8");
        list.add("26.0");
        list.add("26.1");
        list.add("26.3");
        return list;
    }

    public static VersionInfo parseAs(String raw) {
        return VersionProbe.parse(raw, "matrix");
    }

    /** @return human lines: version -> era -> key caps */
    public static List<String> smokeReport() {
        List<String> out = new ArrayList<>();
        out.add("JSA supported range: " + VersionInfo.MIN_SUPPORTED.raw()
                + " .. " + VersionInfo.MAX_SUPPORTED.raw());
        for (String raw : representativeReleases()) {
            VersionInfo v = parseAs(raw);
            VersionCapabilities c = new VersionCapabilities(v);
            out.add(String.format("%-8s  era=%-12s  supported=%-5s  offhand=%-5s  elytra=%-5s  flat=%-5s  tall=%-5s  levelPkg=%-5s",
                    raw,
                    v.era().label(),
                    c.supported(),
                    c.hasOffhand(),
                    c.hasElytra(),
                    c.hasFlattenedIds(),
                    c.hasTallWorld(),
                    c.hasWorldLevelPackages()));
        }
        return out;
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/version/VersionProbe.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.version;

import java.io.InputStream;
import java.util.regex.Matcher;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

/**
 * Detects the running Minecraft version without compile-time MC deps.
 * Fingerprints eras from 1.6.4 through 26.3 via classpath class presence,
 * {@code version.json}, Fabric shared constants, and system properties.
 */
public final class VersionProbe {
    private static final Pattern SEMVER = Pattern.compile("(\\d+)\\.(\\d+)(?:\\.(\\d+))?");

    private VersionProbe() {}

    public static VersionInfo probe(String adapterId) {
        String raw = firstNonBlank(
                systemProp("minecraft.version"),
                systemProp("jsa.mc.version"),
                fabricSharedConstants(),
                brandProperties(),
                classPresenceGuess()
        );
        return parse(raw, adapterId);
    }

    public static VersionInfo parse(String raw, String adapterId) {
        if (raw == null || raw.isBlank() || "unknown".equalsIgnoreCase(raw)) {
            return VersionInfo.unknown(adapterId);
        }
        String cleaned = raw.trim();
        // Strip trailing + / pre markers: "1.17+", "1.20.1-rc1" -> keep digits
        Matcher m = SEMVER.matcher(cleaned);
        if (!m.find()) return new VersionInfo(cleaned, 0, 0, 0, adapterId);
        int major = Integer.parseInt(m.group(1));
        int minor = Integer.parseInt(m.group(2));
        int patch = m.group(3) == null ? 0 : Integer.parseInt(m.group(3));
        // Normalize bare "1.17+" style guesses already captured as patch 0
        String display = major + "." + minor + (m.group(3) != null ? "." + patch : "");
        if (cleaned.contains("+") && m.group(3) == null) display = cleaned; // keep label
        return new VersionInfo(display.equals(cleaned) ? cleaned : cleaned, major, minor, patch, adapterId);
    }

    /**
     * Ordered fingerprints — most specific / newest first.
     * Returns a parseable version string representative of that era.
     */
    static String classPresenceGuess() {
        // Calendar / very new (post-1.x) — if present alongside modern packages
        if (classExists("net.minecraft.world.level.block.Block")
                && classExists("net.minecraft.client.Minecraft")
                && systemProp("jsa.force.calendar") != null) {
            return systemProp("jsa.force.calendar");
        }

        // 1.17+ Mojmap world.level
        if (classExists("net.minecraft.world.level.block.Block")) {
            if (classExists("net.minecraft.world.level.levelgen.DensityFunction")) return "1.18.2";
            if (classExists("net.minecraft.world.entity.monster.warden.Warden")) return "1.19.4";
            if (classExists("net.minecraft.world.item.SmithingTemplateItem")) return "1.20.1";
            if (classExists("net.minecraft.world.entity.monster.breeze.Breeze")) return "1.21.0";
            return "1.17.1";
        }

        // 1.16 yarn/mojmap Level without world.level.block yet on some channels
        if (classExists("net.minecraft.world.level.Level")
                && !classExists("net.minecraft.world.level.block.Block")) {
            return "1.16.5";
        }

        // 1.14+ Yarn MinecraftClient
        if (classExists("net.minecraft.client.MinecraftClient")) {
            if (classExists("net.minecraft.entity.boss.dragon.EnderDragonEntity")
                    || classExists("net.minecraft.entity.boss.dragon.EnderDragon")) {
                return "1.15.2";
            }
            return "1.14.4";
        }

        // 1.13 flattened: Block has getTranslationKey-style / fluid logged markers hard;
        // detect via registry class names common in 1.13 MCP/Yarn
        if (classExists("net.minecraft.util.registry.Registry")
                && classExists("net.minecraft.block.Blocks")
                && classExists("net.minecraft.client.Minecraft")) {
            return "1.13.2";
        }

        // 1.9–1.12: EntityPlayerSP + offhand field era — EnumHand appears 1.9
        if (classExists("net.minecraft.util.EnumHand")
                || classExists("net.minecraft.util.Hand")) {
            if (classExists("net.minecraft.entity.item.EntityElytra")
                    || classExists("net.minecraft.entity.EquipmentSlot")
                    || classExists("net.minecraft.inventory.EntityEquipmentSlot")) {
                return "1.11.2";
            }
            return "1.9.4";
        }

        // 1.8: spectator / world border
        if (classExists("net.minecraft.world.WorldSettings$GameType")
                || classExists("net.minecraft.world.level.GameType")
                || classExists("net.minecraft.world.WorldBorder")) {
            if (classExists("net.minecraft.client.entity.EntityPlayerSP")
                    || classExists("net.minecraft.client.entity.player.ClientPlayerEntity")) {
                return "1.8.9";
            }
        }

        // 1.7
        if (classExists("net.minecraft.client.resources.IResourcePack")
                || classExists("net.minecraft.client.resources.ResourcePack")) {
            return "1.7.10";
        }

        // 1.6.4 classic MCP client
        if (classExists("net.minecraft.client.Minecraft")
                && (classExists("net.minecraft.client.entity.EntityClientPlayerMP")
                || classExists("net.minecraft.client.entity.EntityPlayerSP"))) {
            return "1.6.4";
        }

        if (classExists("net.minecraft.client.Minecraft")) return "1.8.9";
        return null;
    }

    private static String systemProp(String key) {
        try { return System.getProperty(key); } catch (SecurityException e) { return null; }
    }

    private static String fabricSharedConstants() {
        try {
            Class<?> c = Class.forName("net.minecraft.SharedConstants");
            Object v = c.getMethod("getGameVersion").invoke(null);
            if (v == null) return null;
            for (String method : new String[]{"getId", "getName", "getReleaseTarget"}) {
                try {
                    Object id = v.getClass().getMethod(method).invoke(v);
                    if (id != null && SEMVER.matcher(id.toString()).find()) return id.toString();
                } catch (ReflectiveOperationException ignored) {}
            }
            return v.toString();
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    private static String brandProperties() {
        String[] resources = {
                "version.json",
                "META-INF/minecraft.versions.json",
                "mcversion"
        };
        for (String res : resources) {
            try (InputStream in = VersionProbe.class.getClassLoader().getResourceAsStream(res)) {
                if (in == null) continue;
                String s = new String(in.readAllBytes());
                Matcher named = Pattern.compile("\"name\"\\s*:\\s*\"([^\"]+)\"").matcher(s);
                if (named.find() && SEMVER.matcher(named.group(1)).find()) return named.group(1);
                Matcher id = Pattern.compile("\"id\"\\s*:\\s*\"([^\"]+)\"").matcher(s);
                if (id.find() && SEMVER.matcher(id.group(1)).find()) return id.group(1);
                Matcher bare = SEMVER.matcher(s);
                if (bare.find()) return bare.group(0);
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        }
        return null;
    }

    private static boolean classExists(String name) {
        try {
            Class.forName(name, false, VersionProbe.class.getClassLoader());
            return true;
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    private static String firstNonBlank(String... values) {
        if (values == null) return null;
        for (String v : values) {
            if (v != null && !v.isBlank()) return v.trim();
        }
        return null;
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/core/version/Versions.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.version;

/**
 * Process-wide detected Minecraft version. Adapters call {@link #install}
 * during boot; modules read {@link #get()} / {@link #caps()}.
 */
public final class Versions {
    private static volatile VersionInfo info = VersionInfo.unknown("unset");
    private static volatile VersionCapabilities caps = new VersionCapabilities(info);

    private Versions() {}

    public static void install(VersionInfo version) {
        info = version == null ? VersionInfo.unknown("unknown") : version;
        caps = new VersionCapabilities(info);
    }

    public static VersionInfo get() { return info; }
    public static VersionCapabilities caps() { return caps; }
}

```

`core/src/test/java/dev/jsa/client/core/gui/ClickGuiAnimTest.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.gui;

import dev.jsa.client.core.event.RenderEvent;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

/** Open fades in before a close request can finish, then the fade completes. */
class ClickGuiAnimTest {

    @AfterEach
    void reset() {
        ClickGui.get().onScreenRemoved();
    }

    @Test
    void openFadeBlocksInstantClose() {
        ClickGui gui = ClickGui.get();
        RenderEvent event = RenderEvent.get();
        event.setFallbackSize(960, 540);
        event.setSink(null);

        gui.attachScreen();
        gui.setPointer(30, 40);
        for (int i = 0; i < 700; i++) gui.render(event);

        assertTrue(gui.isOpen());
        assertTrue(gui.isScreenHosted());
        gui.requestClose();
        // Still visible, so the screen must not drop on the same frame.
        assertTrue(gui.isClosing());
        assertFalse(gui.finishedClosing());

        for (int i = 0; i < 900; i++) gui.render(event);
        assertTrue(gui.finishedClosing());

        gui.beginOpen();
        assertFalse(gui.finishedClosing());
        gui.click(12, 36, 0);
        gui.release();
    }
}
```

`core/src/test/java/dev/jsa/client/core/version/VersionMatrixTest.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.core.version;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

/**
 * Smoke-check: every representative release from 1.6.4 through 26.3 parses,
 * maps to a known era, and exposes honest capabilities.
 */
class VersionMatrixTest {

    @Test
    void supportedRangeBounds() {
        assertTrue(VersionProbe.parse("1.6.4", "t").inSupportedRange());
        assertTrue(VersionProbe.parse("26.3", "t").inSupportedRange());
        assertFalse(VersionProbe.parse("1.5.2", "t").inSupportedRange());
        assertFalse(VersionProbe.parse("27.0", "t").inSupportedRange());
    }

    @Test
    void everyRepresentativeParsesAndHasEra() {
        for (String raw : VersionMatrix.representativeReleases()) {
            VersionInfo v = VersionProbe.parse(raw, "test");
            assertTrue(v.isKnown(), raw + " should parse");
            assertNotEquals(MinecraftEra.UNKNOWN, v.era(), raw + " should map to an era");
            assertTrue(v.inSupportedRange(), raw + " should be in 1.6.4..26.3");
        }
    }

    @Test
    void legacyHasNoOffhandOrElytra() {
        VersionCapabilities c = new VersionCapabilities(VersionProbe.parse("1.6.4", "t"));
        assertEquals(MinecraftEra.LEGACY_1_6, c.era());
        assertFalse(c.hasOffhand());
        assertFalse(c.hasElytra());
        assertFalse(c.hasAttackCooldown());
        assertFalse(c.hasFlattenedIds());
        assertTrue(c.hasNumericBlockIds());
        assertTrue(c.hasOpenToLan());
    }

    @Test
    void combatUpdateGates() {
        VersionCapabilities c9 = new VersionCapabilities(VersionProbe.parse("1.9.4", "t"));
        assertTrue(c9.hasOffhand());
        assertTrue(c9.hasElytra());
        assertTrue(c9.hasAttackCooldown());
        assertFalse(c9.hasTotems()); // totems are 1.11+

        VersionCapabilities c11 = new VersionCapabilities(VersionProbe.parse("1.11.2", "t"));
        assertTrue(c11.hasTotems());
        assertTrue(c11.canUseAutoTotem());
    }

    @Test
    void flatteningAndTallWorld() {
        VersionCapabilities c12 = new VersionCapabilities(VersionProbe.parse("1.12.2", "t"));
        assertFalse(c12.hasFlattenedIds());
        assertTrue(c12.hasNumericBlockIds());

        VersionCapabilities c13 = new VersionCapabilities(VersionProbe.parse("1.13.2", "t"));
        assertTrue(c13.hasFlattenedIds());
        assertTrue(c13.hasSwim());

        VersionCapabilities c18 = new VersionCapabilities(VersionProbe.parse("1.18.2", "t"));
        assertTrue(c18.hasTallWorld());
        assertTrue(c18.hasWorldLevelPackages());
        assertTrue(c18.requiresJava17());
    }

    @Test
    void calendarStyle26() {
        VersionInfo v = VersionProbe.parse("26.3", "t");
        assertEquals(MinecraftEra.CALENDAR_26, v.era());
        assertTrue(v.inSupportedRange());
        VersionCapabilities c = new VersionCapabilities(v);
        assertTrue(c.hasElytra());
        assertTrue(c.hasTallWorld());
        assertTrue(c.isCalendarNumbering());
        assertTrue(c.hasOffhand());
    }

    @Test
    void classic126StillSupported() {
        VersionInfo v = VersionProbe.parse("1.26.3", "t");
        assertEquals(MinecraftEra.CLASSIC_1_26, v.era());
        assertTrue(v.inSupportedRange());
    }

    @Test
    void orderingAcrossCutover() {
        VersionInfo classic = VersionProbe.parse("1.21.8", "t");
        VersionInfo calendar = VersionProbe.parse("26.0", "t");
        assertTrue(calendar.orderKey() > classic.orderKey());
    }

    @Test
    void smokeReportNonEmpty() {
        assertFalse(VersionMatrix.smokeReport().isEmpty());
    }
}
```

`fabric/build.gradle`:

```gradle
// JSA Client `fabric` adapter — binds the version-agnostic core to Fabric 1.20.1.
plugins {
    id 'fabric-loom' version "${loom_version}"
    id 'java'
}

version = project.property('mod_version')
group = project.property('maven_group')

repositories {
    maven { url 'https://maven.fabricmc.net/' }
    mavenCentral()
}

dependencies {
    // Version-agnostic SP/LAN toolkit engine (bundled into the remapped mod jar).
    implementation project(':core')
    include project(':core')

    minecraft "com.mojang:minecraft:${project.minecraft_version}"
    mappings "net.fabricmc:yarn:${project.yarn_mappings}:v2"
    modImplementation "net.fabricmc:fabric-loader:${project.loader_version}"
    modImplementation "net.fabricmc.fabric-api:fabric-api:${project.fabric_version}"
}

processResources {
    inputs.property 'version', project.version

    filesMatching('fabric.mod.json') {
        expand 'version': project.version
    }
}

java {
    withSourcesJar()
    sourceCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_17
    targetCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_17
}

tasks.withType(JavaCompile).configureEach {
    it.options.release = 17
    it.options.encoding = 'UTF-8'
}

loom {
    // Add an access widener here (src/main/resources/jsaclient.accesswidener)
    // when the adapter needs to widen a vanilla/Fabric accessor.
}

jar {
    from("README.md") {
        rename { 'JSA-CLIENT-README.md' }
    }
    archiveBaseName.set('jsa-client')
    archiveClassifier.set('')
}

remapJar {
    archiveBaseName.set('jsa-client')
    archiveClassifier.set('')
    archiveVersion.set("${project.minecraft_version}-${project.version}")
}
```

`fabric/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/fabric/FabricPlatformAdapter.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.fabric;

import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Entity;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Item;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Packet;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Platform;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.World;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Reference {@link Platform} implementation for Fabric 1.20.1.
 *
 * <p>Self-contained mock adapter: binds the version-agnostic core without a
 * hard Minecraft class surface so the project compiles and unit-tests before
 * full Loom wiring. Swap {@link MockState} for a live {@code MinecraftClient}
 * to go live.
 *
 * <p>Packet type names use a stable convention
 * (e.g. {@code PlayerPositionC2SPacket.PosRot}).
 */
public class FabricPlatformAdapter implements Platform {

    private final MockState state = new MockState();
    private final List<Packet> pendingPackets = new ArrayList<>();

    // ================= context =============================================
    @Override public boolean inGame() { return state.inGame; }
    @Override public boolean onServer() { return state.onServer; }
    @Override public String getServerName() { return state.serverName; }
    @Override public int getPing() { return state.ping; }
    @Override public Entity getLocalPlayer() { return state.localPlayer; }
    @Override public World getWorld() { return state.world; }
    @Override public int getTick() { return state.tick; }
    @Override public long nanoTime() { return System.nanoTime(); }
    @Override public float getPartialTick() { return state.partialTick; }

    // ================= position / motion ===================================
    @Override public double getX() { return state.localPlayer.getX(); }
    @Override public double getY() { return state.localPlayer.getY(); }
    @Override public double getZ() { return state.localPlayer.getZ(); }
    @Override public void setPos(double x, double y, double z) { state.localPlayer.setPos(x, y, z); }
    @Override public double getMotionX() { return state.localPlayer.getMotionX(); }
    @Override public double getMotionY() { return state.localPlayer.getMotionY(); }
    @Override public double getMotionZ() { return state.localPlayer.getMotionZ(); }
    @Override public void setMotion(double x, double y, double z) { state.localPlayer.setMotion(x, y, z); }
    @Override public void addMotion(double x, double y, double z) {
        state.localPlayer.setMotion(state.localPlayer.getMotionX() + x,
                state.localPlayer.getMotionY() + y, state.localPlayer.getMotionZ() + z);
    }
    @Override public boolean isOnGround() { return state.localPlayer.isOnGround(); }
    @Override public void setOnGround(boolean onGround) { state.localPlayer.setOnGround(onGround); }

    // ================= rotation ============================================
    @Override public float getYaw() { return state.localPlayer.getYaw(); }
    @Override public float getPitch() { return state.localPlayer.getPitch(); }
    @Override public void setRotation(float yaw, float pitch) { state.localPlayer.setRotation(yaw, pitch); }

    // ================= movement state ======================================
    @Override public boolean isSprinting() { return state.localPlayer.isSprinting(); }
    @Override public void setSprinting(boolean sprinting) { state.localPlayer.setSprinting(sprinting); }
    @Override public boolean isSneaking() { return state.localPlayer.isSneaking(); }
    @Override public void setSneaking(boolean sneaking) { state.localPlayer.setSneaking(sneaking); }
    @Override public boolean isUsingItem() { return state.eating; }
    @Override public boolean isMoving() { return false; }
    @Override public boolean isOnLadder() { return false; }
    @Override public boolean isInWater() { return false; }
    @Override public boolean isInLava() { return false; }
    @Override public void swingHand(boolean mainHand) { /* adapter: swing hand */ }
    @Override public void openInventory() { state.inventoryOpen = true; }
    @Override public void closeInventory() { state.inventoryOpen = false; }

    // ================= inventory ===========================================
    @Override public int getHeldSlot() { return state.heldSlot; }
    @Override public void setHeldSlot(int slot) { state.heldSlot = slot; }
    @Override public int getOffhandSlot() { return 45; }
    @Override public Item getItem(int slot) { return state.items.get(slot); }
    @Override public void setItem(int slot, Item item) { state.items.put(slot, item); }
    @Override public Item getHeldItem() { return state.items.get(state.heldSlot); }
    @Override public int findItemSlot(int from, int to, Item item) { return -1; }
    @Override public int findFoodSlot(int from, int to) { return -1; }
    @Override public int findWeaponSlot(int from, int to) { return -1; }
    @Override public int findArmorSlot(int armorSlot, Item item) { return -1; }
    @Override public int findBlockSlot(int from, int to, int blockId) { return -1; }

    // ================= entities ============================================
    @Override public List<Entity> getEntities() { return state.world.getEntities(); }
    @Override public List<Entity> getEntitiesInRadius(double radius) {
        List<Entity> out = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Entity e : state.world.getEntities()) {
            if (e.distanceTo(getX(), getY(), getZ()) <= radius) out.add(e);
        }
        return out;
    }
    @Override public boolean canSee(Entity e) { return true; }
    @Override public boolean canSeePos(double x, double y, double z) { return true; }

    // ================= blocks ==============================================
    @Override public int getBlockId(double x, double y, double z) { return 0; }
    @Override public boolean isAir(double x, double y, double z) { return true; }
    @Override public boolean canPlace(double x, double y, double z) { return true; }
    @Override public void placeBlock(double x, double y, double z, int face) { /* adapter: place */ }

    // ================= sound / particles ===================================
    @Override public void playSound(String name, double x, double y, double z, float volume, float pitch) { /* adapter */ }
    @Override public void playSoundAtPlayer(String name, float volume, float pitch) { /* adapter */ }
    @Override public void spawnParticle(int particleId, double x, double y, double z, double vx, double vy, double vz) { /* adapter */ }

    // ================= chat =================================================
    @Override public void chat(String msg) { System.out.println("[chat] " + msg); }
    @Override public void serverMessage(String msg) { System.out.println("[server] " + msg); }
    @Override public void actionbar(String msg) { System.out.println("[actionbar] " + msg); }

    // ================= vitals ==============================================
    @Override public float getHealth() { return state.localPlayer.getHealth(); }
    @Override public int getFoodLevel() { return state.hunger; }
    @Override public boolean isDead() { return state.localPlayer.isDead(); }
    @Override public String getLocalName() { return state.localPlayer.getName(); }

    @Override public String getMinecraftVersion() { return "1.20.1"; }
    @Override public String getAdapterId() { return "fabric"; }

    // ================= packet hooks ========================================
    @Override public void sendPacket(Packet p) { pendingPackets.add(p); }
    @Override public boolean isPacketPositionC2S(Packet p) { return "PlayerPositionC2SPacket.PosRot".equals(p.typeName()); }
    @Override public boolean isPacketLookC2S(Packet p) { return "PlayerLookC2SPacket.LookRot".equals(p.typeName()); }
    @Override public boolean isPacketVelocityS2C(Packet p) { return "PlayerVelocityC2SPacket.Vel".equals(p.typeName()); }
    @Override public boolean isPacketSprintC2S(Packet p) { return "PlayerSprintC2SPacket.Sprint".equals(p.typeName()); }
    @Override public boolean isPacketRespawnS2C(Packet p) { return "PlayerRespawnC2SPacket.Respawn".equals(p.typeName()); }
    @Override public void setPacketPositionY(Packet p, double y) { /* rewrite Y field */ }
    @Override public void setPacketOnGround(Packet p, boolean onGround) { /* rewrite onGround flag */ }
    @Override public void setPacketVelocity(Packet p, double x, double y, double z) { /* rewrite velocity */ }

    // ================= packet factories ====================================
    @Override public void sendPositionPacket(boolean onGround) { /* adapter: send pos C2S */ }
    @Override public void sendPositionPacket(double x, double y, double z, boolean onGround, boolean hasX, boolean hasY) { /* adapter */ }
    @Override public void sendLookPacket(float yaw, float pitch) { /* adapter: send look C2S */ }
    @Override public void sendSneak(boolean sneaking) { /* adapter: client command */ }
    @Override public void sendSprint(boolean sprinting) { /* adapter: client command */ }
    @Override public void spoofPing(int ping) { state.ping = ping; }
    @Override public void queuePacket(Packet p, int delayTicks) { pendingPackets.add(p); }

    // ================= combat ==============================================
    @Override public void attack(Entity target) { /* adapter: swing + hit */ }
    @Override public float getAttackCooldown() { return state.attackCooldown; }
    @Override public void setAttackCooldown(float v) { state.attackCooldown = v; }
    @Override public double distanceTo(Entity e) { return e.distanceTo(getX(), getY(), getZ()); }
    @Override public double distanceTo(double x, double y, double z) {
        double dx = getX() - x, dy = getY() - y, dz = getZ() - z;
        return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz);
    }
    @Override public boolean isCreative() { return false; }

    // ================= speed / use =========================================
    @Override public float getTimer() { return state.timer; }
    @Override public void setTimer(float multiplier) { state.timer = multiplier; }
    @Override public void setUseItemSpeed(float speed) { /* adapter */ }
    @Override public void setEating(boolean eating) { state.eating = eating; }
    @Override public boolean isJumping() { return false; }

    // ================= armor ===============================================
    @Override public Item getArmorItem(int armorSlot) { return null; }
    @Override public void equipFromHotbar(int hotbarSlot, int armorSlot) { /* adapter */ }

    // ================= misc state ==========================================
    @Override public void respawn() { /* adapter */ }
    @Override public String getGameMode() { return "S"; }
    @Override public void setRenderDistance(int blocks) { state.renderDistance = blocks; }
    @Override public int getRenderDistance() { return state.renderDistance; }
    @Override public float getFov() { return state.fov; }
    @Override public void setFov(float fov) { state.fov = fov; }
    @Override public void setFullbright(float gamma) { state.gamma = gamma; }
    @Override public void setNightVision(boolean on) { state.nightVision = on; }
    @Override public boolean isInventoryOpen() { return state.inventoryOpen; }

    // ================= container (ChestStealer) ============================
    @Override public int getContainerSize() { return 0; }
    @Override public void shiftClickFromContainer(int slot) { /* adapter */ }

    // ================= blocks (XRay / Nuker / AutoTool) ====================
    @Override public List<int[]> getBlockIdsInRadius(double radius) { return new ArrayList<>(); }
    @Override public void breakBlock(double x, double y, double z) { /* adapter */ }
    @Override public int getBlockIdAtCrosshair() { return 0; }

    // ================= troll helpers =======================================
    @Override public void setSoundCategoryVolume(String category, float volume) { /* adapter */ }
    @Override public void setDisplayName(String name) { state.displayName = name; }
    @Override public void setSpoofedHealth(double hp, double max) { state.spoofedHp = hp; }
    @Override public void setFakeOffhand(boolean on) { state.fakeOffhand = on; }
    @Override public void showOverlay(String title, String subtitle, int ticks) {
        actionbar(title + (subtitle == null || subtitle.isEmpty() ? "" : " | " + subtitle));
    }
    @Override public void pushFakeChat(String line) { chat("[fake] " + line); }
    @Override public void setSpoofedInventoryMode(String mode) { state.invMode = mode; }
    @Override public void saveClientPos() {
        state.savedX = getX(); state.savedY = getY(); state.savedZ = getZ(); state.hasSaved = true;
    }
    @Override public void blinkClientPos(double x, double y, double z) {
        if (!state.hasSaved) saveClientPos();
        setPos(x, y, z);
    }
    @Override public void restoreClientPos() {
        if (state.hasSaved) setPos(state.savedX, state.savedY, state.savedZ);
    }
    @Override public void setCameraShake(float intensity) { state.shake = intensity; }
    @Override public void setScreenFlip(boolean flip) { state.flip = flip; }
    @Override public void setDerp(boolean on) { state.derp = on; }

    // ================= motion (module-facing names) ========================
    @Override public void setVelocity(double x, double y, double z) { setMotion(x, y, z); }
    @Override public double getVelocityX() { return getMotionX(); }
    @Override public double getVelocityY() { return getMotionY(); }
    @Override public double getVelocityZ() { return getMotionZ(); }
    @Override public boolean isGrounded() { return isOnGround(); }
    @Override public void setFlying(boolean flying) { /* adapter: creative/elytra flight */ }
    @Override public void setJumping(boolean jumping) { /* adapter */ }

    // ================= input ===============================================
    @Override public boolean isKeyDown(char key) { return false; }
    @Override public boolean isShiftDown() { return state.localPlayer.isSneaking(); }
    @Override public boolean isAttacking() { return false; }
    @Override public boolean isEating() { return state.eating; }
    @Override public boolean isBlocking() { return state.blocking; }
    @Override public boolean isGliding() { return false; }
    @Override public void setNoClip(boolean enabled) { state.noClip = enabled; }

    // ================= vitals (module names) ===============================
    @Override public int getHunger() { return state.hunger; }
    @Override public void setHunger(int level) { state.hunger = level; }
    @Override public int getFps() {
        return dev.jsa.client.core.util.FpsCounter.getInstance().get();
    }

    // ================= hotbar / items (module names) =======================
    @Override public Item getHotbarItem(int slot) { return state.items.get(slot); }
    @Override public int getHotbarSlot() { return state.heldSlot; }
    @Override public void setHotbarSlot(int slot) { state.heldSlot = slot; }
    @Override public Item getOffhandItem() { return state.items.get(45); }
    @Override public void swapOffhand() { /* adapter */ }
    @Override public int findPlaceableSlot() { return -1; }
    @Override public int bestToolSlot(int blockId) { return -1; }
    @Override public void dyeArmor(int armorSlot, int color) { /* adapter */ }

    // ================= blocks / placement (module names) ===================
    @Override public int getBlockIdAt(double x, double y, double z) { return getBlockId(x, y, z); }
    @Override public void placeBlockAt(double x, double y, double z, int face) { placeBlock(x, y, z, face); }
    @Override public void setStepHeight(float height) { state.stepHeight = height; }

    // ================= chat (module name) ==================================
    @Override public void sendMessage(String msg, boolean actionBar) {
        if (actionBar) actionbar(msg); else chat(msg);
    }

    // ================= render phase (Phantom troll) ========================
    @Override public int getRenderPhase() { return state.renderPhase; }
    @Override public void setRenderPhase(int phase) { state.renderPhase = phase; }

    // ================= arm swing (module name) =============================
    @Override public void swingArm() { swingHand(true); }

    // ================= mock internals ======================================
    private static final class MockState {
        boolean inGame = false;
        boolean onServer = false;
        String serverName = "Unknown Server";
        int ping = 0;
        int tick = 0;
        float partialTick = 0f;
        final MockEntity localPlayer = new MockEntity(0, "Player", true);
        final MockWorld world = new MockWorld("Overworld");
        final java.util.Map<Integer, Item> items = new java.util.HashMap<>();
        int heldSlot = 0;
        boolean inventoryOpen = false;
        boolean eating = false;
        boolean blocking = false;
        boolean noClip = false;
        float attackCooldown = 1f;
        float timer = 1f;
        int hunger = 20;
        int renderDistance = 12;
        float fov = 70f;
        float gamma = 1f;
        boolean nightVision = false;
        String displayName = "Player";
        double spoofedHp = -1;
        boolean fakeOffhand = false;
        float stepHeight = 0.5f;
        int renderPhase = 0;
        String invMode = "off";
        double savedX, savedY, savedZ;
        boolean hasSaved;
        float shake;
        boolean flip;
        boolean derp;
    }

    private static final class MockEntity implements Entity {
        private final int id;
        private final String name;
        private final boolean player;
        private double x, y, z;
        private double vx, vy, vz;
        private float health = 20f;
        private boolean onGround;
        private float yaw, pitch;
        private boolean dead, sneaking, sprinting, mob, armorStand, blocking;

        MockEntity(int id, String name, boolean player) {
            this.id = id;
            this.name = name;
            this.player = player;
        }

        @Override public int getId() { return id; }
        @Override public String getName() { return name; }
        @Override public double getX() { return x; }
        @Override public double getY() { return y; }
        @Override public double getZ() { return z; }
        public void setPos(double x, double y, double z) { this.x = x; this.y = y; this.z = z; }
        @Override public float getHealth() { return health; }
        @Override public float getMaxHealth() { return 20f; }
        @Override public float getHurtTime() { return 0f; }
        @Override public int getArmorValue() { return 0; }
        @Override public boolean isPlayer() { return player; }
        @Override public boolean isMob() { return mob; }
        @Override public boolean isArmorStand() { return armorStand; }
        @Override public boolean isBlocking() { return blocking; }
        @Override public boolean isDead() { return dead; }
        @Override public boolean isSneaking() { return sneaking; }
        @Override public boolean isSprinting() { return sprinting; }
        @Override public double getMotionX() { return vx; }
        @Override public double getMotionY() { return vy; }
        @Override public double getMotionZ() { return vz; }
        public void setMotion(double x, double y, double z) { this.vx = x; this.vy = y; this.vz = z; }
        public void setRotation(float yaw, float pitch) { this.yaw = yaw; this.pitch = pitch; }
        public float getYaw() { return yaw; }
        public float getPitch() { return pitch; }
        public void setSprinting(boolean v) { this.sprinting = v; }
        public void setSneaking(boolean v) { this.sneaking = v; }
        public boolean isOnGround() { return onGround; }
        public void setOnGround(boolean v) { this.onGround = v; }
    }

    private static final class MockWorld implements World {
        private final String name;
        private final List<Entity> entities = new ArrayList<>();
        MockWorld(String name) { this.name = name; }
        @Override public String getName() { return name; }
        @Override public List<Entity> getEntities() { return entities; }
    }
}
```

`fabric/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/fabric/HostMotion.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.fabric;

import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.movement.FlyModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.movement.SpeedModule;
import net.minecraft.client.MinecraftClient;
import net.minecraft.entity.attribute.EntityAttributes;
import net.minecraft.entity.player.PlayerEntity;
import net.minecraft.server.network.ServerPlayerEntity;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;

/**
 * Fly and Speed for the world host. The integrated server owns movement, so
 * changing only the client player is overwritten. This updates that host
 * player directly. It does nothing on a remote server.
 */
public final class HostMotion {
    private static final Logger LOG = LoggerFactory.getLogger("JSA Client");
    private static final Map<String, Double> SAVED_SPEED = new HashMap<>();
    private static final Set<String> FLY_GRANTED = new HashSet<>();
    private static boolean loggedRemote;

    private HostMotion() {}

    public static void applyClient(PlayerEntity player) {
        if (player == null) return;
        if (!isHost()) {
            noteRemote(player);
            return;
        }
        apply(player, false);
    }

    public static void applyServer(ServerPlayerEntity player) {
        MinecraftClient mc = MinecraftClient.getInstance();
        if (player == null || mc.player == null) return;
        if (!mc.player.getUuid().equals(player.getUuid())) return;
        apply(player, true);
    }

    private static boolean isHost() {
        MinecraftClient mc = MinecraftClient.getInstance();
        return mc.getServer() != null;
    }

    private static void noteRemote(PlayerEntity player) {
        if (loggedRemote) return;
        FlyModule fly = fly();
        SpeedModule speed = speed();
        boolean wants = (fly != null && fly.isEnabled()) || (speed != null && speed.isEnabled());
        if (!wants) return;
        loggedRemote = true;
        LOG.info("Fly and Speed run on the singleplayer / open-LAN host. This client is not hosting, so they stay off.");
    }

    private static void apply(PlayerEntity player, boolean server) {
        FlyModule fly = fly();
        if (fly != null && fly.isEnabled()) {
            grantFly(player, Math.max(0.03f, fly.flySpeedBlocks() / 20f), server);
            return;
        }
        revokeFly(player, server);
        applySpeed(player, server);
    }

    private static void grantFly(PlayerEntity player, float flySpeed, boolean server) {
        var abilities = player.getAbilities();
        boolean changed = !abilities.allowFlying || !abilities.flying
                || Math.abs(abilities.getFlySpeed() - flySpeed) > 0.001f;
        abilities.allowFlying = true;
        abilities.flying = true;
        abilities.setFlySpeed(flySpeed);
        FLY_GRANTED.add(key(player, server));
        if (server && changed && player instanceof ServerPlayerEntity sp) {
            sp.sendAbilitiesUpdate();
        }
    }

    private static void revokeFly(PlayerEntity player, boolean server) {
        if (!FLY_GRANTED.remove(key(player, server))) return;
        if (player.isCreative() || player.isSpectator()) return;
        var abilities = player.getAbilities();
        abilities.flying = false;
        abilities.allowFlying = false;
        abilities.setFlySpeed(0.05f);
        if (server && player instanceof ServerPlayerEntity sp) {
            sp.sendAbilitiesUpdate();
        }
    }

    private static void applySpeed(PlayerEntity player, boolean server) {
        SpeedModule speed = speed();
        var attr = player.getAttributeInstance(EntityAttributes.GENERIC_MOVEMENT_SPEED);
        if (attr == null) return;
        String key = (server ? "s:" : "c:") + player.getUuid();
        if (speed != null && speed.isEnabled() && wantsBoost(player, speed)) {
            SAVED_SPEED.putIfAbsent(key, attr.getBaseValue());
            double want = 0.1 * speed.multiplier();
            if (Math.abs(attr.getBaseValue() - want) > 0.0001) attr.setBaseValue(want);
            if ("Bounce".equals(speed.modeName()) && player.isOnGround() && player.age % 5 == 0) {
                player.setJumping(true);
            }
        } else {
            Double saved = SAVED_SPEED.remove(key);
            if (saved != null) attr.setBaseValue(saved);
        }
    }

    private static boolean wantsBoost(PlayerEntity player, SpeedModule speed) {
        if (speed.forwardOnly() && player.forwardSpeed <= 0f) return false;
        return speed.boostsInAir() || player.isOnGround();
    }

    private static String key(PlayerEntity player, boolean server) {
        return (server ? "s:" : "c:") + player.getUuid();
    }

    private static FlyModule fly() {
        return ModuleManager.get().getModule("Fly") instanceof FlyModule fm ? fm : null;
    }

    private static SpeedModule speed() {
        return ModuleManager.get().getModule("Speed") instanceof SpeedModule sm ? sm : null;
    }
}
```
