`fabric/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/fabric/JsaClickGuiScreen.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.fabric;

import dev.jsa.client.core.event.RenderEvent;
import dev.jsa.client.core.gui.ClickGui;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Entity;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.DrawContext;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.screen.Screen;
import net.minecraft.text.Text;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW;

/**
 * Real client screen so the cursor stays free and clicks hit modules.
 * Does not pause the world (SP / open LAN keep ticking).
 */
public class JsaClickGuiScreen extends Screen {

    public JsaClickGuiScreen() {
        super(Text.literal("JSA"));
    }

    @Override
    protected void init() {
        ClickGui.get().attachScreen();
    }

    @Override
    public void removed() {
        ClickGui.get().onScreenRemoved();
    }

    @Override
    public void tick() {
        // Close after the fade, from the tick, so render is not torn down mid-frame.
        if (ClickGui.get().finishedClosing()) close();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean shouldPause() {
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    public void render(DrawContext context, int mouseX, int mouseY, float delta) {
        ClickGui.get().setPointer(mouseX, mouseY);
        RenderEvent ev = RenderEvent.get();
        ev.setSink(new Sink(context));
        ClickGui.get().render(ev);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mouseClicked(double mouseX, double mouseY, int button) {
        ClickGui.get().click((int) mouseX, (int) mouseY, button);
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mouseDragged(double mouseX, double mouseY, int button, double deltaX, double deltaY) {
        if (button == 0) ClickGui.get().drag((int) mouseX, (int) mouseY);
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mouseReleased(double mouseX, double mouseY, int button) {
        ClickGui.get().release();
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean keyPressed(int keyCode, int scanCode, int modifiers) {
        // ESC fades the GUI out. Insert / Right Shift / Right Ctrl are toggled
        // by the client tick so they don't open and close in the same press.
        if (keyCode == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE) {
            if (ClickGui.get().isClosing()) close();
            else ClickGui.get().requestClose();
            return true;
        }
        return super.keyPressed(keyCode, scanCode, modifiers);
    }

    private final class Sink implements RenderEvent.RenderSink {
        private final DrawContext ctx;

        Sink(DrawContext ctx) { this.ctx = ctx; }

        @Override public void box(Entity e, int argb) {}
        @Override public void healthBar(Entity e, float fraction) {}
        @Override public void line(Entity e, int argb) {}

        @Override public void text(int x, int y, String s, int argb) {
            ctx.drawText(client.textRenderer, s, x, y, argb, true);
        }

        @Override public void fill(int x, int y, int w, int h, int argb) {
            ctx.fill(x, y, x + w, y + h, argb);
        }

        @Override public void outline(int x, int y, int w, int h, int argb) {
            ctx.fill(x, y, x + w, y + 1, argb);
            ctx.fill(x, y + h - 1, x + w, y + h, argb);
            ctx.fill(x, y, x + 1, y + h, argb);
            ctx.fill(x + w - 1, y, x + w, y + h, argb);
        }

        @Override public int width() { return client.getWindow().getScaledWidth(); }
        @Override public int height() { return client.getWindow().getScaledHeight(); }
    }
}
```

`fabric/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/fabric/JsaClientFabric.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.fabric;

import dev.jsa.client.core.JsaClientLoader;
import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.event.RenderEvent;
import dev.jsa.client.core.gui.ClickGui;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Entity;
import dev.jsa.client.fabric.meteor.MeteorAddonHost;
import net.fabricmc.api.ClientModInitializer;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.event.lifecycle.v1.ClientTickEvents;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.keybinding.v1.KeyBindingHelper;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.rendering.v1.HudRenderCallback;
import net.minecraft.client.MinecraftClient;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.DrawContext;
import net.minecraft.client.option.KeyBinding;
import net.minecraft.client.util.InputUtil;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;

/**
 * Fabric 1.20.1 entry — live SP / open-LAN toolkit with ClickGUI.
 * Default open key: <b>Insert</b> (also Right Shift). Rebind under Controls → JSA Client.
 */
public class JsaClientFabric implements ClientModInitializer {

    public static final String MOD_ID = "jsaclient";
    public static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger("JSA Client");

    public static LiveFabricPlatform ADAPTER;
    public static KeyBinding openGuiKey;
    private static boolean rshiftWas;
    private static boolean rctrlWas;

    @Override
    public void onInitializeClient() {
        ADAPTER = new LiveFabricPlatform();
        MC.set(ADAPTER);
        JsaClientLoader.boot();

        openGuiKey = KeyBindingHelper.registerKeyBinding(new KeyBinding(
                "key.jsaclient.opengui",
                InputUtil.Type.KEYSYM,
                GLFW.GLFW_KEY_INSERT,
                "category.jsaclient"
        ));

        WorldEspRenderer.register();

        ClientTickEvents.END_CLIENT_TICK.register(client -> {
            if (client.player == null) return;

            boolean insert = openGuiKey.wasPressed();
            boolean rshift = InputUtil.isKeyPressed(client.getWindow().getHandle(), GLFW.GLFW_KEY_RIGHT_SHIFT);
            boolean rctrl = InputUtil.isKeyPressed(client.getWindow().getHandle(), GLFW.GLFW_KEY_RIGHT_CONTROL);
            boolean fallbackEdge = (rshift && !rshiftWas) || (rctrl && !rctrlWas);
            rshiftWas = rshift;
            rctrlWas = rctrl;

            if (insert || fallbackEdge) {
                if (client.currentScreen instanceof JsaClickGuiScreen) {
                    // Fade closed. Pressing the key again while it fades reopens.
                    if (ClickGui.get().isClosing()) ClickGui.get().beginOpen();
                    else ClickGui.get().requestClose();
                } else if (client.currentScreen == null) {
                    client.setScreen(new JsaClickGuiScreen());
                }
            }

            ADAPTER.pulse();
            MeteorAddonHost.tick();
            ModuleManager.get().tick();
        });

        HudRenderCallback.EVENT.register((DrawContext ctx, float tickDelta) -> {
            MinecraftClient client = MinecraftClient.getInstance();
            if (client.player == null) return;

            RenderEvent ev = RenderEvent.get();
            ev.setWorldPass(false);
            ev.setSink(new DrawContextSink(ctx, client));
            ModuleManager.get().onRender(ev);
            WorldEspRenderer.drawTags(ctx);
        });

        LOGGER.info("JSA Client ready — {} modules. Press INSERT (or Right Shift) for ClickGUI.",
                ModuleManager.get().getAllModules().size());
    }

    private static final class DrawContextSink implements RenderEvent.RenderSink {
        private final DrawContext ctx;
        private final MinecraftClient client;

        DrawContextSink(DrawContext ctx, MinecraftClient client) {
            this.ctx = ctx;
            this.client = client;
        }

        @Override public void box(Entity e, int argb) {}

        @Override public void healthBar(Entity e, float fraction) {}

        @Override public void line(Entity e, int argb) {}

        @Override public void text(int x, int y, String s, int argb) {
            ctx.drawText(client.textRenderer, s, x, y, argb, true);
        }

        @Override public void fill(int x, int y, int w, int h, int argb) {
            ctx.fill(x, y, x + w, y + h, argb);
        }

        @Override public void outline(int x, int y, int w, int h, int argb) {
            ctx.fill(x, y, x + w, y + 1, argb);
            ctx.fill(x, y + h - 1, x + w, y + h, argb);
            ctx.fill(x, y, x + 1, y + h, argb);
            ctx.fill(x + w - 1, y, x + w, y + h, argb);
        }

        @Override public int width() { return client.getWindow().getScaledWidth(); }
        @Override public int height() { return client.getWindow().getScaledHeight(); }
    }
}
```

`fabric/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/fabric/LiveFabricPlatform.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.fabric;

import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Entity;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Item;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Packet;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Platform;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.World;
import net.minecraft.client.MinecraftClient;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.screen.ChatScreen;
import net.minecraft.client.network.ClientPlayerEntity;
import net.minecraft.client.option.Perspective;
import net.minecraft.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.entity.effect.StatusEffectInstance;
import net.minecraft.entity.effect.StatusEffects;
import net.minecraft.entity.decoration.ArmorStandEntity;
import net.minecraft.entity.mob.MobEntity;
import net.minecraft.entity.player.PlayerEntity;
import net.minecraft.item.BlockItem;
import net.minecraft.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.item.MiningToolItem;
import net.minecraft.item.SwordItem;
import net.minecraft.text.Text;
import net.minecraft.util.Hand;
import net.minecraft.util.hit.BlockHitResult;
import net.minecraft.util.math.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.util.math.Direction;
import net.minecraft.util.math.MathHelper;
import net.minecraft.util.math.Vec3d;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Live Fabric 1.20.1 {@link Platform} bound to {@link MinecraftClient}.
 * Powers SP / open-LAN modules and the ClickGUI.
 */
public class LiveFabricPlatform implements Platform {

    private final MinecraftClient mc = MinecraftClient.getInstance();

    private float timer = 1f;
    private float spoofFov = -1;
    private float gammaOverride = -1;
    private boolean nightVision;
    private boolean nightVisionApplied;
    private float stepHeight = 0.6f;
    private double entityReach = 3.0;
    private double blockReach = 4.5;
    private int aoLevel = 1;
    private int noRenderMask;
    private boolean noClip;
    private boolean flying;
    private String displayName;
    private double spoofHp = -1;
    private boolean fakeOffhand;
    private String invMode = "off";
    private float shake;
    private boolean flip;
    private boolean derp;
    private int renderPhase;
    private double savedX, savedY, savedZ;
    private boolean hasSaved;
    private boolean extraGuiKeyDown;
    private ClientPlayerEntity player() { return mc.player; }

    @Override public String getMinecraftVersion() { return "1.20.1"; }
    @Override public String getAdapterId() { return "fabric"; }

    @Override public boolean inGame() { return mc.player != null && mc.world != null; }
    @Override public boolean onServer() {
        return mc.getCurrentServerEntry() != null || (mc.isIntegratedServerRunning() && mc.getServer() != null
                && mc.getServer().isRemote());
    }
    @Override public String getServerName() {
        if (mc.getCurrentServerEntry() != null) return mc.getCurrentServerEntry().name;
        if (mc.isIntegratedServerRunning()) return "Open to LAN / Singleplayer";
        return "Singleplayer";
    }
    @Override public int getPing() {
        if (mc.getNetworkHandler() == null || player() == null) return 0;
        var entry = mc.getNetworkHandler().getPlayerListEntry(player().getUuid());
        return entry == null ? 0 : entry.getLatency();
    }

    @Override public Entity getLocalPlayer() {
        return player() == null ? null : wrap(player());
    }

    @Override public World getWorld() {
        return new World() {
            @Override public String getName() {
                return mc.world == null ? "?" : mc.world.getRegistryKey().getValue().toString();
            }
            @Override public List<Entity> getEntities() {
                List<Entity> out = new ArrayList<>();
                if (mc.world == null) return out;
                for (var e : mc.world.getEntities()) out.add(wrap(e));
                return out;
            }
        };
    }

    @Override public int getTick() { return player() == null ? 0 : player().age; }
    @Override public long nanoTime() { return System.nanoTime(); }
    @Override public float getPartialTick() { return mc.getTickDelta(); }

    @Override public double getX() { return player() == null ? 0 : player().getX(); }
    @Override public double getY() { return player() == null ? 0 : player().getY(); }
    @Override public double getZ() { return player() == null ? 0 : player().getZ(); }
    @Override public void setPos(double x, double y, double z) {
        if (player() != null) player().setPosition(x, y, z);
    }

    @Override public double getMotionX() { return player() == null ? 0 : player().getVelocity().x; }
    @Override public double getMotionY() { return player() == null ? 0 : player().getVelocity().y; }
    @Override public double getMotionZ() { return player() == null ? 0 : player().getVelocity().z; }
    @Override public void setMotion(double x, double y, double z) {
        if (player() != null) player().setVelocity(x, y, z);
    }
    @Override public void addMotion(double x, double y, double z) {
        if (player() != null) player().addVelocity(x, y, z);
    }
    @Override public boolean isOnGround() { return player() != null && player().isOnGround(); }
    @Override public void setOnGround(boolean onGround) {
        if (player() != null) player().setOnGround(onGround);
    }

    @Override public float getYaw() { return player() == null ? 0 : player().getYaw(); }
    @Override public float getPitch() { return player() == null ? 0 : player().getPitch(); }
    @Override public void setRotation(float yaw, float pitch) {
        if (player() == null) return;
        player().setYaw(yaw);
        player().setPitch(MathHelper.clamp(pitch, -90f, 90f));
    }

    @Override public boolean isSprinting() { return player() != null && player().isSprinting(); }
    @Override public void setSprinting(boolean sprinting) {
        if (player() != null) player().setSprinting(sprinting);
    }
    @Override public boolean isSneaking() { return player() != null && player().isSneaking(); }
    @Override public void setSneaking(boolean sneaking) {
        if (player() != null) player().setSneaking(sneaking);
    }
    @Override public boolean isUsingItem() { return player() != null && player().isUsingItem(); }
    @Override public boolean isMoving() {
        return player() != null && (player().input.movementForward != 0 || player().input.movementSideways != 0);
    }
    @Override public boolean isOnLadder() { return player() != null && player().isClimbing(); }
    @Override public boolean isInWater() { return player() != null && player().isTouchingWater(); }
    @Override public boolean isInLava() { return player() != null && player().isInLava(); }

    @Override public void swingHand(boolean mainHand) {
        if (player() != null) player().swingHand(mainHand ? Hand.MAIN_HAND : Hand.OFF_HAND);
    }
    @Override public void openInventory() { /* vanilla inventory key */ }
    @Override public void closeInventory() {
        if (mc.currentScreen != null) mc.currentScreen.close();
    }

    @Override public int getHeldSlot() { return player() == null ? 0 : player().getInventory().selectedSlot; }
    @Override public void setHeldSlot(int slot) {
        if (player() != null) player().getInventory().selectedSlot = MathHelper.clamp(slot, 0, 8);
    }
    @Override public int getOffhandSlot() { return 40; }
    @Override public Item getItem(int slot) {
        if (player() == null) return null;
        ItemStack stack = player().getInventory().getStack(slot);
        return wrapItem(stack);
    }
    @Override public void setItem(int slot, Item item) { /* creative only — skip */ }
    @Override public Item getHeldItem() {
        return player() == null ? null : wrapItem(player().getMainHandStack());
    }
    @Override public int findItemSlot(int from, int to, Item item) { return -1; }
    @Override public int findFoodSlot(int from, int to) {
        if (player() == null) return -1;
        for (int i = from; i <= to; i++) {
            ItemStack s = player().getInventory().getStack(i);
            if (s.isFood()) return i;
        }
        return -1;
    }
    @Override public int findWeaponSlot(int from, int to) {
        if (player() == null) return -1;
        for (int i = from; i <= to; i++) {
            ItemStack s = player().getInventory().getStack(i);
            if (s.getItem() instanceof SwordItem || s.getItem() instanceof MiningToolItem) return i;
        }
        return -1;
    }
    @Override public int findArmorSlot(int armorSlot, Item item) { return -1; }
    @Override public int findBlockSlot(int from, int to, int blockId) { return findPlaceableSlot(); }

    @Override public List<Entity> getEntities() { return getWorld().getEntities(); }
    @Override public List<Entity> getEntitiesInRadius(double radius) {
        List<Entity> out = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Entity e : getEntities()) {
            if (distanceTo(e) <= radius) out.add(e);
        }
        return out;
    }
    @Override public boolean canSee(Entity e) { return true; }
    @Override public boolean canSeePos(double x, double y, double z) { return true; }

    @Override public int getBlockId(double x, double y, double z) {
        if (mc.world == null) return 0;
        return net.minecraft.registry.Registries.BLOCK.getRawId(
                mc.world.getBlockState(BlockPos.ofFloored(x, y, z)).getBlock());
    }
    @Override public boolean isAir(double x, double y, double z) {
        return mc.world != null && mc.world.getBlockState(BlockPos.ofFloored(x, y, z)).isAir();
    }
    @Override public boolean canPlace(double x, double y, double z) { return isAir(x, y, z); }
    @Override public void placeBlock(double x, double y, double z, int face) {
        if (mc.interactionManager == null || player() == null) return;
        BlockPos pos = BlockPos.ofFloored(x, y, z);
        Direction dir = Direction.byId(face);
        BlockHitResult hit = new BlockHitResult(new Vec3d(x, y, z), dir, pos, false);
        mc.interactionManager.interactBlock(player(), Hand.MAIN_HAND, hit);
    }

    @Override public void playSound(String name, double x, double y, double z, float volume, float pitch) {}
    @Override public void playSoundAtPlayer(String name, float volume, float pitch) {}
    @Override public void spawnParticle(int particleId, double x, double y, double z, double vx, double vy, double vz) {}

    @Override public void chat(String msg) {
        if (player() != null) player().sendMessage(Text.literal(msg), false);
    }
    @Override public void serverMessage(String msg) { chat(msg); }
    @Override public void actionbar(String msg) {
        if (player() != null) player().sendMessage(Text.literal(msg), true);
    }

    @Override public float getHealth() { return player() == null ? 0 : player().getHealth(); }
    @Override public int getFoodLevel() { return player() == null ? 0 : player().getHungerManager().getFoodLevel(); }
    @Override public boolean isDead() { return player() != null && player().isDead(); }
    @Override public String getLocalName() {
        if (displayName != null) return displayName;
        return player() == null ? "?" : player().getName().getString();
    }

    @Override public void sendPacket(Packet p) {}
    @Override public boolean isPacketPositionC2S(Packet p) { return false; }
    @Override public boolean isPacketLookC2S(Packet p) { return false; }
    @Override public boolean isPacketVelocityS2C(Packet p) { return false; }
    @Override public boolean isPacketSprintC2S(Packet p) { return false; }
    @Override public boolean isPacketRespawnS2C(Packet p) { return false; }
    @Override public void setPacketPositionY(Packet p, double y) {}
    @Override public void setPacketOnGround(Packet p, boolean onGround) {}
    @Override public void setPacketVelocity(Packet p, double x, double y, double z) {}

    @Override public void sendPositionPacket(boolean onGround) {
        if (player() != null) player().networkHandler.sendPacket(
                new net.minecraft.network.packet.c2s.play.PlayerMoveC2SPacket.OnGroundOnly(onGround));
    }
    @Override public void sendPositionPacket(double x, double y, double z, boolean onGround, boolean hasX, boolean hasY) {
        if (player() != null) player().networkHandler.sendPacket(
                new net.minecraft.network.packet.c2s.play.PlayerMoveC2SPacket.PositionAndOnGround(x, y, z, onGround));
    }
    @Override public void sendLookPacket(float yaw, float pitch) {
        if (player() != null) player().networkHandler.sendPacket(
                new net.minecraft.network.packet.c2s.play.PlayerMoveC2SPacket.LookAndOnGround(yaw, pitch, player().isOnGround()));
    }
    @Override public void sendSneak(boolean sneaking) { setSneaking(sneaking); }
    @Override public void sendSprint(boolean sprinting) { setSprinting(sprinting); }
    @Override public void spoofPing(int ping) {}
    @Override public void queuePacket(Packet p, int delayTicks) {}

    @Override public void attack(Entity target) {
        if (mc.interactionManager == null || player() == null || mc.world == null) return;
        var raw = mc.world.getEntityById(target.getId());
        if (raw != null) mc.interactionManager.attackEntity(player(), raw);
    }
    @Override public float getAttackCooldown() {
        return player() == null ? 1f : player().getAttackCooldownProgress(0.5f);
    }
    @Override public void setAttackCooldown(float v) { /* client indicator only */ }
    @Override public double distanceTo(Entity e) {
        return e == null ? 0 : e.distanceTo(getX(), getY(), getZ());
    }
    @Override public boolean isCreative() {
        return player() != null && player().getAbilities().creativeMode;
    }

    @Override public float getTimer() { return timer; }
    @Override public void setTimer(float multiplier) { this.timer = multiplier; }
    @Override public void setUseItemSpeed(float speed) {}
    @Override public void setEating(boolean eating) {}
    @Override public boolean isJumping() {
        return player() != null && player().input.jumping;
    }

    @Override public Item getArmorItem(int armorSlot) {
        if (player() == null) return null;
        return wrapItem(player().getInventory().getArmorStack(armorSlot));
    }
    @Override public void equipFromHotbar(int hotbarSlot, int armorSlot) {}

    @Override public void respawn() {
        if (player() != null) player().requestRespawn();
    }
    @Override public String getGameMode() {
        if (player() == null) return "?";
        if (player().isSpectator()) return "Spectator";
        if (player().getAbilities().creativeMode) return "Creative";
        if (player().getAbilities().invulnerable) return "Adventure";
        return "Survival";
    }
    @Override public void setRenderDistance(int blocks) {
        mc.options.getViewDistance().setValue(MathHelper.clamp(blocks / 16, 2, 32));
    }
    @Override public int getRenderDistance() {
        return mc.options.getViewDistance().getValue() * 16;
    }
    @Override public float getFov() {
        return spoofFov > 0 ? spoofFov : mc.options.getFov().getValue().floatValue();
    }
    @Override public void setFov(float fov) { this.spoofFov = fov; }
    @Override public void setFullbright(float gamma) { this.gammaOverride = gamma; }
    @Override public void setNightVision(boolean on) { this.nightVision = on; }
    @Override public boolean isInventoryOpen() {
        return mc.currentScreen != null && !(mc.currentScreen instanceof ChatScreen);
    }

    @Override public int getContainerSize() { return 0; }
    @Override public void shiftClickFromContainer(int slot) {}

    @Override public List<int[]> getBlockIdsInRadius(double radius) { return List.of(); }
    @Override public void breakBlock(double x, double y, double z) {
        if (mc.interactionManager == null) return;
        mc.interactionManager.attackBlock(BlockPos.ofFloored(x, y, z), Direction.UP);
    }
    @Override public int getBlockIdAtCrosshair() {
        if (!(mc.crosshairTarget instanceof BlockHitResult hit)) return 0;
        return getBlockId(hit.getBlockPos().getX(), hit.getBlockPos().getY(), hit.getBlockPos().getZ());
    }

    @Override public void setSoundCategoryVolume(String category, float volume) {}
    @Override public void setDisplayName(String name) { this.displayName = name; }
    @Override public void setSpoofedHealth(double hp, double max) { this.spoofHp = hp; }
    @Override public void setFakeOffhand(boolean on) { this.fakeOffhand = on; }

    @Override public void setVelocity(double x, double y, double z) { setMotion(x, y, z); }
    @Override public double getVelocityX() { return getMotionX(); }
    @Override public double getVelocityY() { return getMotionY(); }
    @Override public double getVelocityZ() { return getMotionZ(); }
    @Override public boolean isGrounded() { return isOnGround(); }
    @Override public void setFlying(boolean flying) {
        this.flying = flying;
        if (player() == null || mc.getServer() == null) return;
        player().getAbilities().allowFlying = flying || player().isCreative();
        player().getAbilities().flying = flying;
        player().sendAbilitiesUpdate();
    }
    @Override public void setJumping(boolean jumping) {
        if (player() != null) player().jump();
    }

    @Override public boolean isKeyDown(char key) {
        long win = mc.getWindow().getHandle();
        return switch (Character.toLowerCase(key)) {
            case 'w' -> GLFW.glfwGetKey(win, GLFW.GLFW_KEY_W) == GLFW.GLFW_PRESS;
            case 'a' -> GLFW.glfwGetKey(win, GLFW.GLFW_KEY_A) == GLFW.GLFW_PRESS;
            case 's' -> GLFW.glfwGetKey(win, GLFW.GLFW_KEY_S) == GLFW.GLFW_PRESS;
            case 'd' -> GLFW.glfwGetKey(win, GLFW.GLFW_KEY_D) == GLFW.GLFW_PRESS;
            case ' ' -> GLFW.glfwGetKey(win, GLFW.GLFW_KEY_SPACE) == GLFW.GLFW_PRESS;
            case 'c' -> GLFW.glfwGetKey(win, GLFW.GLFW_KEY_C) == GLFW.GLFW_PRESS;
            case 'r' -> GLFW.glfwGetKey(win, GLFW.GLFW_KEY_R) == GLFW.GLFW_PRESS;
            default -> false;
        };
    }
    @Override public boolean isShiftDown() {
        return GLFW.glfwGetKey(mc.getWindow().getHandle(), GLFW.GLFW_KEY_LEFT_SHIFT) == GLFW.GLFW_PRESS
                || GLFW.glfwGetKey(mc.getWindow().getHandle(), GLFW.GLFW_KEY_RIGHT_SHIFT) == GLFW.GLFW_PRESS;
    }
    @Override public boolean isAttacking() {
        return mc.options.attackKey.isPressed();
    }
    @Override public boolean isEating() { return isUsingItem(); }
    @Override public boolean isBlocking() { return player() != null && player().isBlocking(); }
    @Override public boolean isGliding() { return player() != null && player().isFallFlying(); }
    @Override public void setNoClip(boolean enabled) { this.noClip = enabled; }

    @Override public int getHunger() { return getFoodLevel(); }
    @Override public void setHunger(int level) {
        if (player() != null) player().getHungerManager().setFoodLevel(level);
    }
    @Override public int getFps() {
        try {
            int mcFps = mc.getCurrentFps();
            if (mcFps > 0) return mcFps;
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        return dev.jsa.client.core.util.FpsCounter.getInstance().get();
    }

    @Override public Item getHotbarItem(int slot) { return getItem(slot); }
    @Override public int getHotbarSlot() { return getHeldSlot(); }
    @Override public void setHotbarSlot(int slot) { setHeldSlot(slot); }
    @Override public Item getOffhandItem() {
        return player() == null ? null : wrapItem(player().getOffHandStack());
    }
    @Override public void swapOffhand() {}
    @Override public int findPlaceableSlot() {
        if (player() == null) return -1;
        for (int i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
            ItemStack s = player().getInventory().getStack(i);
            if (s.getItem() instanceof BlockItem) return i;
        }
        return -1;
    }
    @Override public int bestToolSlot(int blockId) { return findWeaponSlot(0, 8); }
    @Override public void dyeArmor(int armorSlot, int color) {}

    @Override public int getBlockIdAt(double x, double y, double z) { return getBlockId(x, y, z); }
    @Override public void placeBlockAt(double x, double y, double z, int face) { placeBlock(x, y, z, face); }
    @Override public void setStepHeight(float height) {
        this.stepHeight = height;
        applyStepHeight();
    }

    private void applyStepHeight() {
        if (player() == null) return;
        try {
            var f = net.minecraft.entity.Entity.class.getDeclaredField("stepHeight");
            f.setAccessible(true);
            f.setFloat(player(), stepHeight);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            try {
                var m = player().getClass().getMethod("setStepHeight", float.class);
                m.invoke(player(), stepHeight);
            } catch (Throwable ignored2) {}
        }
    }

    @Override public void sendMessage(String msg, boolean actionBar) {
        if (actionBar) actionbar(msg); else chat(msg);
    }

    @Override public int getRenderPhase() { return renderPhase; }
    @Override public void setRenderPhase(int phase) { this.renderPhase = phase; }
    @Override public void swingArm() { swingHand(true); }

    @Override public void showOverlay(String title, String subtitle, int ticks) {
        actionbar(title + (subtitle == null || subtitle.isEmpty() ? "" : " — " + subtitle));
    }
    @Override public void pushFakeChat(String line) { chat(line); }
    @Override public void setSpoofedInventoryMode(String mode) { this.invMode = mode; }
    @Override public void saveClientPos() {
        savedX = getX(); savedY = getY(); savedZ = getZ(); hasSaved = true;
    }
    @Override public void blinkClientPos(double x, double y, double z) {
        if (!hasSaved) saveClientPos();
        setPos(x, y, z);
    }
    @Override public void restoreClientPos() {
        if (hasSaved) setPos(savedX, savedY, savedZ);
    }
    @Override public void setCameraShake(float intensity) { this.shake = intensity; }
    @Override public void setScreenFlip(boolean flip) { this.flip = flip; }
    @Override public void setDerp(boolean on) { this.derp = on; }

    /** Set each tick from the Fabric entry (Right Shift / Right Ctrl fallback). */
    public void setExtraGuiKeyDown(boolean down) { this.extraGuiKeyDown = down; }

    @Override public boolean isGuiToggleKeyDown() {
        // Insert is handled via Fabric KeyBinding.wasPressed() to avoid double-toggle.
        return extraGuiKeyDown;
    }
    @Override public int getMouseX() {
        return (int) (mc.mouse.getX() * mc.getWindow().getScaledWidth() / Math.max(1, mc.getWindow().getWidth()));
    }
    @Override public int getMouseY() {
        return (int) (mc.mouse.getY() * mc.getWindow().getScaledHeight() / Math.max(1, mc.getWindow().getHeight()));
    }
    @Override public boolean isMouseButtonDown(int button) {
        int glfw = button == 1 ? GLFW.GLFW_MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT
                : button == 2 ? GLFW.GLFW_MOUSE_BUTTON_MIDDLE
                : GLFW.GLFW_MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT;
        return GLFW.glfwGetMouseButton(mc.getWindow().getHandle(), glfw) == GLFW.GLFW_PRESS;
    }
    @Override public int getScreenWidth() { return mc.getWindow().getScaledWidth(); }
    @Override public int getScreenHeight() { return mc.getWindow().getScaledHeight(); }

    @Override public void setEntityReach(double blocks) { this.entityReach = blocks; }
    @Override public void setBlockReach(double blocks) { this.blockReach = blocks; }
    @Override public double getEntityReach() { return entityReach; }
    @Override public double getBlockReach() { return blockReach; }
    @Override public void setAmbientOcclusion(int level) { this.aoLevel = level; }
    @Override public void setNoRenderMask(int mask) { this.noRenderMask = mask; }

    public float gammaOverride() { return gammaOverride; }
    public boolean nightVision() { return nightVision; }
    public float stepHeight() { return stepHeight; }
    public int noRenderMask() { return noRenderMask; }
    public int aoLevel() { return aoLevel; }
    public boolean noClip() { return noClip; }
    public float shake() { return shake; }
    public boolean flip() { return flip; }
    public boolean derp() { return derp; }
    public float spoofFov() { return spoofFov; }

    /** Apply per-tick side effects that need the live client. */
    public void pulse() {
        if (player() == null) return;
        if (flying && mc.getServer() != null) {
            player().getAbilities().flying = true;
        }
        if (stepHeight > 0.6f) applyStepHeight();
        if (noClip) {
            try {
                var f = net.minecraft.entity.Entity.class.getDeclaredField("noClip");
                f.setAccessible(true);
                f.setBoolean(player(), true);
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        }
        if (nightVision) {
            player().addStatusEffect(new StatusEffectInstance(
                    StatusEffects.NIGHT_VISION, 220, 0, false, false, true));
            nightVisionApplied = true;
        } else if (nightVisionApplied) {
            player().removeStatusEffect(StatusEffects.NIGHT_VISION);
            nightVisionApplied = false;
        }
        if (derp) {
            player().setYaw(player().getYaw() + 25f);
            player().setPitch((float) (Math.sin(player().age * 0.4) * 40));
        }
        if (flip) {
            mc.options.setPerspective(Perspective.THIRD_PERSON_FRONT);
        }
    }

    private Entity wrap(net.minecraft.entity.Entity e) {
        return new Entity() {
            @Override public int getId() { return e.getId(); }
            @Override public String getName() { return e.getName().getString(); }
            @Override public double getX() { return e.getX(); }
            @Override public double getY() { return e.getY(); }
            @Override public double getZ() { return e.getZ(); }
            @Override public float getHealth() {
                return e instanceof LivingEntity le ? le.getHealth() : 0;
            }
            @Override public float getMaxHealth() {
                return e instanceof LivingEntity le ? le.getMaxHealth() : 1;
            }
            @Override public float getHurtTime() {
                return e instanceof LivingEntity le ? le.hurtTime : 0;
            }
            @Override public int getArmorValue() {
                return e instanceof LivingEntity le ? le.getArmor() : 0;
            }
            @Override public boolean isPlayer() { return e instanceof PlayerEntity; }
            @Override public boolean isMob() { return e instanceof MobEntity; }
            @Override public boolean isArmorStand() { return e instanceof ArmorStandEntity; }
            @Override public boolean isBlocking() {
                return e instanceof LivingEntity le && le.isBlocking();
            }
            @Override public boolean isDead() { return !e.isAlive(); }
            @Override public boolean isSneaking() { return e.isSneaking(); }
            @Override public boolean isSprinting() { return e.isSprinting(); }
            @Override public double getMotionX() { return e.getVelocity().x; }
            @Override public double getMotionY() { return e.getVelocity().y; }
            @Override public double getMotionZ() { return e.getVelocity().z; }
            @Override public double distanceTo(double x, double y, double z) {
                return e.getPos().distanceTo(new Vec3d(x, y, z));
            }
        };
    }

    private Item wrapItem(ItemStack stack) {
        if (stack == null || stack.isEmpty()) return null;
        final int id = net.minecraft.registry.Registries.ITEM.getRawId(stack.getItem());
        final String name = stack.getName().getString();
        final boolean food = stack.isFood();
        final boolean sword = stack.getItem() instanceof SwordItem;
        final boolean axe = stack.getItem() instanceof net.minecraft.item.AxeItem;
        final boolean weapon = sword || axe || stack.getItem() instanceof MiningToolItem;
        final int foodVal = food && stack.getItem().getFoodComponent() != null
                ? stack.getItem().getFoodComponent().getHunger() : 0;
        final int maxDmg = stack.getMaxDamage();
        final int dmgLeft = maxDmg <= 0 ? 0 : maxDmg - stack.getDamage();
        return new Item() {
            @Override public int getId() { return id; }
            @Override public String getName() { return name; }
            @Override public boolean isFood() { return food; }
            @Override public int getFoodValue() { return foodVal; }
            @Override public boolean isCookedFood() { return food; }
            @Override public float getAttackDamage() { return sword ? 7f : 1f; }
            @Override public float getAttackSpeed() { return 1f; }
            @Override public int getDurability() { return dmgLeft; }
            @Override public int getMaxDurability() { return maxDmg; }
            @Override public int getArmorSlot() { return -1; }
            @Override public boolean isEnderPearl() {
                return stack.isOf(net.minecraft.item.Items.ENDER_PEARL);
            }
            @Override public boolean isTotem() {
                return stack.isOf(net.minecraft.item.Items.TOTEM_OF_UNDYING);
            }
            @Override public boolean isShulkerBox() { return false; }
            @Override public boolean isAxe() { return axe; }
            @Override public boolean isSword() { return sword; }
            @Override public boolean isWeapon() { return weapon; }
        };
    }
}

```

`fabric/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/fabric/WorldEspRenderer.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.fabric;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import dev.jsa.client.core.event.RenderEvent;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Entity;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.rendering.v1.WorldRenderContext;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.rendering.v1.WorldRenderEvents;
import net.minecraft.client.MinecraftClient;
import net.minecraft.client.font.TextRenderer;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.DrawContext;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import net.minecraft.client.render.BufferBuilder;
import net.minecraft.client.render.GameRenderer;
import net.minecraft.client.render.Tessellator;
import net.minecraft.client.render.VertexFormat;
import net.minecraft.client.render.VertexFormats;
import net.minecraft.client.util.math.MatrixStack;
import net.minecraft.util.math.Box;
import net.minecraft.util.math.Vec3d;
import org.joml.Matrix4f;
import org.joml.Vector4f;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean;

/**
 * Draws ESP boxes and tracer lines in the world, through walls.
 * Original renderer — uses the camera and the entity's interpolated box.
 * Hook: Fabric {@code WorldRenderEvents.LAST}.
 */
public final class WorldEspRenderer {
    private static final Logger LOG = LoggerFactory.getLogger("JSA Client");
    private static final AtomicBoolean REGISTERED = new AtomicBoolean();
    private static final List<Cuboid> BOXES = new ArrayList<>();
    private static final List<Seg> LINES = new ArrayList<>();
    private static final List<Tag> TAGS = new ArrayList<>();
    private static final Map<Integer, Integer> TAG_ROWS = new HashMap<>();
    private static final RenderEvent.RenderSink SINK = new WorldSink();

    private static MinecraftClient client;
    private static WorldRenderContext context;
    private static Matrix4f view;
    private static float tick;
    private static boolean logged;

    private WorldEspRenderer() {}

    /** Idempotent. Safe from both the Fabric mod and the shaded loader jar. */
    public static void register() {
        if (!REGISTERED.compareAndSet(false, true)) return;
        // LAST is after clouds. Fabulous graphics composites before that, so that
        // mode draws on AFTER_TRANSLUCENT while the world buffer is still bound.
        WorldRenderEvents.LAST.register(ctx -> {
            if (!ctx.advancedTranslucency()) onWorld(ctx);
        });
        WorldRenderEvents.AFTER_TRANSLUCENT.register(ctx -> {
            if (ctx.advancedTranslucency()) onWorld(ctx);
        });
        LOG.info("World ESP hook: WorldRenderEvents.LAST");
    }

    /** Screen-space tags projected during the world pass. Call from the HUD pass. */
    public static void drawTags(DrawContext ctx) {
        if (ctx == null || TAGS.isEmpty()) return;
        MinecraftClient mc = MinecraftClient.getInstance();
        TextRenderer font = mc.textRenderer;
        if (font == null) return;
        for (Tag tag : TAGS) {
            int w = font.getWidth(tag.text);
            ctx.drawText(font, tag.text, tag.x - w / 2, tag.y, tag.argb, true);
        }
        TAGS.clear();
        TAG_ROWS.clear();
    }

    private static void onWorld(WorldRenderContext ctx) {
        MinecraftClient mc = MinecraftClient.getInstance();
        if (mc.player == null || mc.world == null || ctx.matrixStack() == null || ctx.camera() == null) {
            TAGS.clear();
            TAG_ROWS.clear();
            return;
        }

        BOXES.clear();
        LINES.clear();
        TAGS.clear();
        TAG_ROWS.clear();

        client = mc;
        context = ctx;
        tick = ctx.tickDelta();
        view = cameraView(ctx);

        RenderEvent ev = RenderEvent.get();
        try {
            ev.setWorldPass(true);
            ev.setSink(SINK);
            ModuleManager.get().onRender(ev);
            flush(ctx);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            if (!logged) {
                logged = true;
                LOG.warn("World ESP render failed", t);
            }
        } finally {
            ev.setWorldPass(false);
            ev.setSink(null);
            client = null;
            context = null;
            view = null;
        }
    }

    /**
     * The world matrix at this point has the camera rotation only.
     * Shift by the camera position so box vertices can stay in world space.
     */
    private static Matrix4f cameraView(WorldRenderContext ctx) {
        Matrix4f base = new Matrix4f(ctx.matrixStack().peek().getPositionMatrix());
        Vec3d cam = ctx.camera().getPos();
        base.translate((float) -cam.x, (float) -cam.y, (float) -cam.z);
        return base;
    }

    private static void flush(WorldRenderContext ctx) {
        if (BOXES.isEmpty() && LINES.isEmpty()) return;

        RenderSystem.enableBlend();
        RenderSystem.defaultBlendFunc();
        RenderSystem.disableCull();
        RenderSystem.disableDepthTest();
        RenderSystem.depthMask(false);
        RenderSystem.setShaderColor(1f, 1f, 1f, 1f);
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionColorProgram);
        RenderSystem.lineWidth(2f);

        MatrixStack modelView = RenderSystem.getModelViewStack();
        modelView.push();
        modelView.loadIdentity();
        modelView.multiplyPositionMatrix(view != null ? view : cameraView(ctx));
        RenderSystem.applyModelViewMatrix();

        Tessellator tess = Tessellator.getInstance();
        BufferBuilder buffer = tess.getBuffer();
        try {
            buffer.begin(VertexFormat.DrawMode.DEBUG_LINES, VertexFormats.POSITION_COLOR);
            for (Cuboid box : BOXES) {
                float[] c = rgba(box.argb);
                boxEdges(buffer, box, c[0], c[1], c[2], c[3]);
            }
            for (Seg seg : LINES) {
                float[] c = rgba(seg.argb);
                buffer.vertex(seg.x1, seg.y1, seg.z1).color(c[0], c[1], c[2], c[3]);
                buffer.vertex(seg.x2, seg.y2, seg.z2).color(c[0], c[1], c[2], c[3]);
            }
            tess.draw();
        } finally {
            modelView.pop();
            RenderSystem.applyModelViewMatrix();
            RenderSystem.lineWidth(1f);
            RenderSystem.setShaderColor(1f, 1f, 1f, 1f);
            RenderSystem.depthMask(true);
            RenderSystem.enableDepthTest();
            RenderSystem.enableCull();
            RenderSystem.disableBlend();
        }
    }

    /** Twelve edges. Positions stay in world space; the camera matrix is the model-view. */
    private static void boxEdges(BufferBuilder buffer, Cuboid box, float r, float g, float b, float a) {
        float x1 = (float) box.x1, y1 = (float) box.y1, z1 = (float) box.z1;
        float x2 = (float) box.x2, y2 = (float) box.y2, z2 = (float) box.z2;
        edge(buffer, x1, y1, z1, x2, y1, z1, r, g, b, a);
        edge(buffer, x2, y1, z1, x2, y1, z2, r, g, b, a);
        edge(buffer, x2, y1, z2, x1, y1, z2, r, g, b, a);
        edge(buffer, x1, y1, z2, x1, y1, z1, r, g, b, a);
        edge(buffer, x1, y2, z1, x2, y2, z1, r, g, b, a);
        edge(buffer, x2, y2, z1, x2, y2, z2, r, g, b, a);
        edge(buffer, x2, y2, z2, x1, y2, z2, r, g, b, a);
        edge(buffer, x1, y2, z2, x1, y2, z1, r, g, b, a);
        edge(buffer, x1, y1, z1, x1, y2, z1, r, g, b, a);
        edge(buffer, x2, y1, z1, x2, y2, z1, r, g, b, a);
        edge(buffer, x2, y1, z2, x2, y2, z2, r, g, b, a);
        edge(buffer, x1, y1, z2, x1, y2, z2, r, g, b, a);
    }

    private static void edge(BufferBuilder buffer,
                             float x1, float y1, float z1, float x2, float y2, float z2,
                             float r, float g, float b, float a) {
        buffer.vertex(x1, y1, z1).color(r, g, b, a);
        buffer.vertex(x2, y2, z2).color(r, g, b, a);
    }

    private static Box bounds(Entity e) {
        net.minecraft.entity.Entity mc = client.world.getEntityById(e.getId());
        if (mc == null) {
            double x = e.getX();
            double y = e.getY();
            double z = e.getZ();
            double half = e.isPlayer() ? 0.3 : 0.25;
            double height = e.isPlayer() ? 1.8 : 1.0;
            return new Box(x - half, y, z - half, x + half, y + height, z + half);
        }
        Vec3d pos = mc.getLerpedPos(tick);
        return mc.getBoundingBox()
                .offset(pos.x - mc.getX(), pos.y - mc.getY(), pos.z - mc.getZ())
                .expand(0.05);
    }

    private static int[] project(double x, double y, double z) {
        if (view == null || context == null || context.projectionMatrix() == null) return null;
        Vector4f v = new Vector4f((float) x, (float) y, (float) z, 1f);
        view.transform(v);
        context.projectionMatrix().transform(v);
        if (v.w <= 0.05f) return null;
        float nx = v.x / v.w;
        float ny = v.y / v.w;
        int sw = client.getWindow().getScaledWidth();
        int sh = client.getWindow().getScaledHeight();
        int sx = Math.round((nx * 0.5f + 0.5f) * sw);
        int sy = Math.round((1f - (ny * 0.5f + 0.5f)) * sh);
        if (sx < -48 || sy < -48 || sx > sw + 48 || sy > sh + 48) return null;
        return new int[]{sx, sy};
    }

    private static float[] rgba(int argb) {
        return new float[]{
                ((argb >>> 16) & 0xFF) / 255f,
                ((argb >>> 8) & 0xFF) / 255f,
                (argb & 0xFF) / 255f,
                ((argb >>> 24) & 0xFF) / 255f
        };
    }

    private static final class WorldSink implements RenderEvent.RenderSink {
        @Override public void box(Entity e, int argb) {
            if (e == null || client == null) return;
            Box b = bounds(e);
            BOXES.add(new Cuboid(b.minX, b.minY, b.minZ, b.maxX, b.maxY, b.maxZ, argb));
        }

        @Override public void healthBar(Entity e, float fraction) {
            if (e == null || client == null) return;
            Box b = bounds(e);
            float t = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, fraction));
            double x = b.maxX + 0.06;
            double z = (b.minZ + b.maxZ) * 0.5;
            LINES.add(new Seg((float) x, (float) b.minY, (float) z, (float) x, (float) b.maxY, (float) z, 0x66000000));
            float top = (float) (b.minY + (b.maxY - b.minY) * t);
            int g = (int) (t * 220);
            int r = (int) ((1f - t) * 220);
            LINES.add(new Seg((float) x, (float) b.minY, (float) z, (float) x, top, (float) z, 0xFF000000 | (r << 16) | (g << 8)));
        }

        @Override public void line(Entity e, int argb) {
            if (e == null || client == null || context == null || context.camera() == null) return;
            Box b = bounds(e);
            Vec3d cam = context.camera().getPos();
            Vec3d look = Vec3d.fromPolar(context.camera().getPitch(), context.camera().getYaw());
            LINES.add(new Seg(
                    (float) (cam.x + look.x * 0.45),
                    (float) (cam.y + look.y * 0.45),
                    (float) (cam.z + look.z * 0.45),
                    (float) ((b.minX + b.maxX) * 0.5),
                    (float) ((b.minY + b.maxY) * 0.5),
                    (float) ((b.minZ + b.maxZ) * 0.5),
                    argb));
        }

        @Override public void label(Entity e, String text, int argb) {
            if (e == null || text == null || text.isEmpty() || client == null) return;
            Box b = bounds(e);
            int[] at = project((b.minX + b.maxX) * 0.5, b.maxY + 0.18, (b.minZ + b.maxZ) * 0.5);
            if (at == null) return;
            int row = TAG_ROWS.merge(e.getId(), 1, Integer::sum) - 1;
            TAGS.add(new Tag(at[0], at[1] - row * 10, argb, text));
        }

        @Override public void text(int x, int y, String s, int argb) {}
        @Override public void fill(int x, int y, int w, int h, int argb) {}
        @Override public void outline(int x, int y, int w, int h, int argb) {}

        @Override public int width() {
            return client == null ? 1 : client.getWindow().getScaledWidth();
        }

        @Override public int height() {
            return client == null ? 1 : client.getWindow().getScaledHeight();
        }
    }

    private static final class Cuboid {
        final double x1, y1, z1, x2, y2, z2;
        final int argb;

        Cuboid(double x1, double y1, double z1, double x2, double y2, double z2, int argb) {
            this.x1 = x1;
            this.y1 = y1;
            this.z1 = z1;
            this.x2 = x2;
            this.y2 = y2;
            this.z2 = z2;
            this.argb = argb;
        }
    }

    private static final class Seg {
        final float x1, y1, z1, x2, y2, z2;
        final int argb;

        Seg(float x1, float y1, float z1, float x2, float y2, float z2, int argb) {
            this.x1 = x1;
            this.y1 = y1;
            this.z1 = z1;
            this.x2 = x2;
            this.y2 = y2;
            this.z2 = z2;
            this.argb = argb;
        }
    }

    private static final class Tag {
        final int x, y, argb;
        final String text;

        Tag(int x, int y, int argb, String text) {
            this.x = x;
            this.y = y;
            this.argb = argb;
            this.text = text;
        }
    }
}

```

`fabric/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/fabric/meteor/JsaAddonManager.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.fabric.meteor;

import net.fabricmc.loader.api.FabricLoader;
import net.fabricmc.loader.api.entrypoint.EntrypointContainer;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Discovers Fabric entrypoint {@code meteor} the same way an addon loader does:
 * {@code FabricLoader.getEntrypointContainers}, then {@code getPackage}.
 * Meteor Client itself calls {@code onInitialize} during its boot, so this
 * loader does not call it again.
 */
public final class JsaAddonManager {
    private static final List<Found> FOUND = new ArrayList<>();
    private static boolean done;

    private JsaAddonManager() {}

    public static List<Found> found() {
        return Collections.unmodifiableList(FOUND);
    }

    public static boolean meteorPresent() {
        return FabricLoader.getInstance().isModLoaded("meteor-client");
    }

    /** Safe to call every tick until it finishes. */
    public static void discover() {
        if (done) return;
        if (!meteorPresent()) {
            System.out.println("[JSA] Meteor Client is not installed. Entrypoint \"meteor\" was not loaded. "
                    + "Install Meteor Client, then put addon jars in mods/. "
                    + "Their modules show under ClickGUI → Meteor Addons.");
            done = true;
            return;
        }
        try {
            Class<?> addonType = Class.forName("meteordevelopment.meteorclient.addons.MeteorAddon");
            List<EntrypointContainer<?>> containers = entrypoints(addonType);
            FOUND.clear();
            for (EntrypointContainer<?> container : containers) {
                String mod = container.getProvider().getMetadata().getId();
                String name = container.getProvider().getMetadata().getName();
                try {
                    Object addon = container.getEntrypoint();
                    String pkg = String.valueOf(addon.getClass().getMethod("getPackage").invoke(addon));
                    FOUND.add(new Found(mod, name, pkg));
                    System.out.println("[JSA] Meteor entrypoint \"" + name + "\" (" + mod + ") package " + pkg);
                } catch (Throwable t) {
                    System.out.println("[JSA] Meteor entrypoint \"" + name + "\" failed: "
                            + t.getClass().getSimpleName() + " " + t.getMessage());
                }
            }
            System.out.println("[JSA] Discovered " + FOUND.size()
                    + " meteor entrypoint(s). Meteor Client already ran onInitialize; JSA lists their modules.");
            done = true;
        } catch (ClassNotFoundException e) {
            System.out.println("[JSA] Meteor Client is loaded but MeteorAddon is missing. "
                    + "Entrypoint \"meteor\" was not loaded.");
            done = true;
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            System.out.println("[JSA] Meteor addon discovery waiting: "
                    + t.getClass().getSimpleName() + " " + t.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @SuppressWarnings({"unchecked", "rawtypes"})
    private static List<EntrypointContainer<?>> entrypoints(Class<?> addonType) {
        return FabricLoader.getInstance().getEntrypointContainers("meteor", (Class) addonType);
    }

    public static final class Found {
        public final String modId;
        public final String name;
        public final String packageName;

        Found(String modId, String name, String packageName) {
            this.modId = modId;
            this.name = name;
            this.packageName = packageName;
        }
    }
}

```

`fabric/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/fabric/meteor/MeteorAddonHost.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.fabric.meteor;

import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ModuleManager;

import java.util.Collection;

/**
 * Lists every Meteor module — Meteor Client's own modules and addon modules —
 * in the JSA ClickGUI. They still execute through Meteor Client; JSA only
 * lists and toggles them.
 */
public final class MeteorAddonHost {
    private static boolean finished;

    private MeteorAddonHost() {}

    /** Call once per client tick until it returns. Safe if Meteor is absent. */
    public static void tick() {
        if (finished) return;
        JsaAddonManager.discover();
        try {
            if (!JsaAddonManager.meteorPresent()) {
                finished = true;
                return;
            }
            Class<?> modulesCls = Class.forName("meteordevelopment.meteorclient.systems.modules.Modules");
            Object modules = modulesCls.getMethod("get").invoke(null);
            if (modules == null) return; // Meteor not finished booting yet
            Object all = modules.getClass().getMethod("getAll").invoke(modules);
            if (!(all instanceof Collection<?> list)) {
                finished = true;
                return;
            }
            int imported = 0;
            for (Object meteorModule : list) {
                if (meteorModule == null) continue;
                String pkg = meteorModule.getClass().getPackageName();
                boolean builtin = pkg.startsWith("meteordevelopment.meteorclient");
                String title = readName(meteorModule);
                if (title == null || title.isBlank()) continue;
                if (ModuleManager.get().getModule(title) != null) {
                    // Exact name clash with a JSA module (or a module already
                    // listed). Meteor's own modules stay under a "Meteor "
                    // prefix; addon modules with that exact name are skipped.
                    if (!builtin) continue;
                    title = "Meteor " + title;
                    if (ModuleManager.get().getModule(title) != null) continue;
                }
                ModuleManager.get().register(new MeteorProxyModule(title, meteorModule));
                imported++;
            }
            System.out.println("[JSA] Meteor: imported " + imported + " module(s) into ClickGUI → Meteor Addons.");
            finished = true;
        } catch (ClassNotFoundException e) {
            warnMissingMeteor();
            finished = true;
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            System.out.println("[JSA] Meteor addon import waiting: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName() + " " + t.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private static String readName(Object module) {
        try {
            Object title = module.getClass().getField("title").get(module);
            if (title != null && !title.toString().isBlank()) return title.toString();
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        try {
            Object name = module.getClass().getField("name").get(module);
            if (name != null) return name.toString();
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        return module.getClass().getSimpleName();
    }

    private static void warnMissingMeteor() {
        System.out.println("[JSA] Meteor support ready. Install Meteor Client for 1.20.1. Its modules and addon modules show under ClickGUI → Meteor Addons.");
    }
}

```

`fabric/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/fabric/meteor/MeteorProxyModule.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.fabric.meteor;

import dev.jsa.client.core.module.Category;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ToggledModule;

/**
 * ClickGUI row that toggles a Meteor module. The module still runs
 * inside Meteor; this only mirrors on/off.
 */
public final class MeteorProxyModule extends ToggledModule {
    private final Object meteorModule;

    public MeteorProxyModule(String name, Object meteorModule) {
        super(name, Category.METEOR);
        this.meteorModule = meteorModule;
    }

    @Override
    protected void onEnable() {
        setMeteorActive(true);
    }

    @Override
    protected void onDisable() {
        setMeteorActive(false);
    }

    private void setMeteorActive(boolean want) {
        try {
            boolean active = (boolean) meteorModule.getClass().getMethod("isActive").invoke(meteorModule);
            if (active != want) {
                meteorModule.getClass().getMethod("toggle").invoke(meteorModule);
            }
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            System.out.println("[JSA] Meteor toggle failed for " + getName() + ": " + t.getMessage());
        }
    }
}
```

`fabric/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/fabric/mixin/JsaLightmapMixin.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.fabric.mixin;

import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.render.FullbrightModule;
import net.minecraft.client.render.LightmapTextureManager;
import net.minecraft.client.texture.NativeImage;
import net.minecraft.client.texture.NativeImageBackedTexture;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Final;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Shadow;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;

/**
 * Gamma mode paints the lightmap white. The options slider clamps at 1,
 * which is not fullbright.
 */
@Mixin(LightmapTextureManager.class)
public class JsaLightmapMixin {
    @Shadow @Final private NativeImage image;
    @Shadow @Final private NativeImageBackedTexture texture;
    @Shadow private boolean dirty;

    @Inject(method = "update", at = @At("HEAD"))
    private void jsa$keepDirty(float delta, CallbackInfo ci) {
        if (wash()) dirty = true;
    }

    @Inject(method = "update", at = @At("TAIL"))
    private void jsa$wash(float delta, CallbackInfo ci) {
        if (!wash()) return;
        for (int y = 0; y < 16; y++) {
            for (int x = 0; x < 16; x++) {
                image.setColor(x, y, 0xFFFFFFFF);
            }
        }
        texture.upload();
    }

    private static boolean wash() {
        return ModuleManager.get().getModule("Fullbright") instanceof FullbrightModule fb && fb.washesLightmap();
    }
}

```

`fabric/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/fabric/mixin/JsaPlayerMixin.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.fabric.mixin;

import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.movement.NoFallModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.player.NoClipModule;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.render.VanillaStepModule;
import dev.jsa.client.fabric.HostMotion;
import net.minecraft.client.network.ClientPlayerEntity;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;

/**
 * Applies movement modules inside player movement so vanilla does not
 * overwrite them. Same kind of hook Meteor uses — original code, not a copy.
 */
@Mixin(ClientPlayerEntity.class)
public class JsaPlayerMixin {

    @Inject(method = "tickMovement", at = @At("HEAD"))
    private void jsa$beforeMove(CallbackInfo ci) {
        ClientPlayerEntity player = (ClientPlayerEntity) (Object) this;
        ModuleManager modules = ModuleManager.get();
        HostMotion.applyClient(player);

        if (modules.getModule("NoClip") instanceof NoClipModule nc && nc.isEnabled()) {
            player.noClip = true;
            player.getAbilities().allowFlying = true;
            player.getAbilities().flying = true;
            player.getAbilities().setFlySpeed(Math.max(0.03f, nc.flySpeedBlocks() / 20f));
        }

        if (modules.getModule("NoFall") instanceof NoFallModule nf && nf.isEnabled()) {
            player.fallDistance = 0f;
        }

        if (modules.getModule("VanillaStep") instanceof VanillaStepModule step && step.isEnabled()) {
            ((JsaEntityAccessor) player).jsa$setStepHeight(step.stepHeight());
        }
    }
}
```

`fabric/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/fabric/mixin/JsaServerPlayerMixin.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.fabric.mixin;

import dev.jsa.client.fabric.HostMotion;
import net.minecraft.server.network.ServerPlayerEntity;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;

/**
 * Host-side Fly and Speed. Runs only for the local player on the integrated
 * server (singleplayer or open LAN). Other players are left alone.
 */
@Mixin(ServerPlayerEntity.class)
public class JsaServerPlayerMixin {

    @Inject(method = "tick", at = @At("HEAD"))
    private void jsa$hostMotion(CallbackInfo ci) {
        HostMotion.applyServer((ServerPlayerEntity) (Object) this);
    }
}
```

`gradlew`:

```
#!/bin/sh

#
# Copyright © 2015-2021 the original authors.
#
# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
# You may obtain a copy of the License at
#
#      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
#
# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
# distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
# limitations under the License.
#
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
#

##############################################################################
#
#   Gradle start up script for POSIX generated by Gradle.
#
#   Important for running:
#
#   (1) You need a POSIX-compliant shell to run this script. If your /bin/sh is
#       noncompliant, but you have some other compliant shell such as ksh or
#       bash, then to run this script, type that shell name before the whole
#       command line, like:
#
#           ksh Gradle
#
#       Busybox and similar reduced shells will NOT work, because this script
#       requires all of these POSIX shell features:
#         * functions;
#         * expansions «$var», «${var}», «${var:-default}», «${var+SET}»,
#           «${var#prefix}», «${var%suffix}», and «$( cmd )»;
#         * compound commands having a testable exit status, especially «case»;
#         * various built-in commands including «command», «set», and «ulimit».
#
#   Important for patching:
#
#   (2) This script targets any POSIX shell, so it avoids extensions provided
#       by Bash, Ksh, etc; in particular arrays are avoided.
#
#       The "traditional" practice of packing multiple parameters into a
#       space-separated string is a well documented source of bugs and security
#       problems, so this is (mostly) avoided, by progressively accumulating
#       options in "$@", and eventually passing that to Java.
#
#       Where the inherited environment variables (DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS, JAVA_OPTS,
#       and GRADLE_OPTS) rely on word-splitting, this is performed explicitly;
#       see the in-line comments for details.
#
#       There are tweaks for specific operating systems such as AIX, CygWin,
#       Darwin, MinGW, and NonStop.
#
#   (3) This script is generated from the Groovy template
#       https://github.com/gradle/gradle/blob/HEAD/platforms/jvm/plugins-application/src/main/resources/org/gradle/api/internal/plugins/unixStartScript.txt
#       within the Gradle project.
#
#       You can find Gradle at https://github.com/gradle/gradle/.
#
##############################################################################

# Attempt to set APP_HOME

# Resolve links: $0 may be a link
app_path=$0

# Need this for daisy-chained symlinks.
while
    APP_HOME=${app_path%"${app_path##*/}"}  # leaves a trailing /; empty if no leading path
    [ -h "$app_path" ]
do
    ls=$( ls -ld "$app_path" )
    link=${ls#*' -> '}
    case $link in             #(
      /*)   app_path=$link ;; #(
      *)    app_path=$APP_HOME$link ;;
    esac
done

# This is normally unused
# shellcheck disable=SC2034
APP_BASE_NAME=${0##*/}
# Discard cd standard output in case $CDPATH is set (https://github.com/gradle/gradle/issues/25036)
APP_HOME=$( cd -P "${APP_HOME:-./}" > /dev/null && printf '%s
' "$PWD" ) || exit

# Use the maximum available, or set MAX_FD != -1 to use that value.
MAX_FD=maximum

warn () {
    echo "$*"
} >&2

die () {
    echo
    echo "$*"
    echo
    exit 1
} >&2

# OS specific support (must be 'true' or 'false').
cygwin=false
msys=false
darwin=false
nonstop=false
case "$( uname )" in                #(
  CYGWIN* )         cygwin=true  ;; #(
  Darwin* )         darwin=true  ;; #(
  MSYS* | MINGW* )  msys=true    ;; #(
  NONSTOP* )        nonstop=true ;;
esac

CLASSPATH=$APP_HOME/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.jar


# Determine the Java command to use to start the JVM.
if [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
    if [ -x "$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java" ] ; then
        # IBM's JDK on AIX uses strange locations for the executables
        JAVACMD=$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java
    else
        JAVACMD=$JAVA_HOME/bin/java
    fi
    if [ ! -x "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
        die "ERROR: JAVA_HOME is set to an invalid directory: $JAVA_HOME

Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the
location of your Java installation."
    fi
else
    JAVACMD=java
    if ! command -v java >/dev/null 2>&1
    then
        die "ERROR: JAVA_HOME is not set and no 'java' command could be found in your PATH.

Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the
location of your Java installation."
    fi
fi

# Increase the maximum file descriptors if we can.
if ! "$cygwin" && ! "$darwin" && ! "$nonstop" ; then
    case $MAX_FD in #(
      max*)
        # In POSIX sh, ulimit -H is undefined. That's why the result is checked to see if it worked.
        # shellcheck disable=SC2039,SC3045
        MAX_FD=$( ulimit -H -n ) ||
            warn "Could not query maximum file descriptor limit"
    esac
    case $MAX_FD in  #(
      '' | soft) :;; #(
      *)
        # In POSIX sh, ulimit -n is undefined. That's why the result is checked to see if it worked.
        # shellcheck disable=SC2039,SC3045
        ulimit -n "$MAX_FD" ||
            warn "Could not set maximum file descriptor limit to $MAX_FD"
    esac
fi

# Collect all arguments for the java command, stacking in reverse order:
#   * args from the command line
#   * the main class name
#   * -classpath
#   * -D...appname settings
#   * --module-path (only if needed)
#   * DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS, JAVA_OPTS, and GRADLE_OPTS environment variables.

# For Cygwin or MSYS, switch paths to Windows format before running java
if "$cygwin" || "$msys" ; then
    APP_HOME=$( cygpath --path --mixed "$APP_HOME" )
    CLASSPATH=$( cygpath --path --mixed "$CLASSPATH" )

    JAVACMD=$( cygpath --unix "$JAVACMD" )

    # Now convert the arguments - kludge to limit ourselves to /bin/sh
    for arg do
        if
            case $arg in                                #(
              -*)   false ;;                            # don't mess with options #(
              /?*)  t=${arg#/} t=/${t%%/*}              # looks like a POSIX filepath
                    [ -e "$t" ] ;;                      #(
              *)    false ;;
            esac
        then
            arg=$( cygpath --path --ignore --mixed "$arg" )
        fi
        # Roll the args list around exactly as many times as the number of
        # args, so each arg winds up back in the position where it started, but
        # possibly modified.
        #
        # NB: a `for` loop captures its iteration list before it begins, so
        # changing the positional parameters here affects neither the number of
        # iterations, nor the values presented in `arg`.
        shift                   # remove old arg
        set -- "$@" "$arg"      # push replacement arg
    done
fi


# Add default JVM options here. You can also use JAVA_OPTS and GRADLE_OPTS to pass JVM options to this script.
DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS='"-Xmx64m" "-Xms64m"'

# Collect all arguments for the java command:
#   * DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS, JAVA_OPTS, JAVA_OPTS, and optsEnvironmentVar are not allowed to contain shell fragments,
#     and any embedded shellness will be escaped.
#   * For example: A user cannot expect ${Hostname} to be expanded, as it is an environment variable and will be
#     treated as '${Hostname}' itself on the command line.

set -- \
        "-Dorg.gradle.appname=$APP_BASE_NAME" \
        -classpath "$CLASSPATH" \
        org.gradle.wrapper.GradleWrapperMain \
        "$@"

# Stop when "xargs" is not available.
if ! command -v xargs >/dev/null 2>&1
then
    die "xargs is not available"
fi

# Use "xargs" to parse quoted args.
#
# With -n1 it outputs one arg per line, with the quotes and backslashes removed.
#
# In Bash we could simply go:
#
#   readarray ARGS < <( xargs -n1 <<<"$var" ) &&
#   set -- "${ARGS[@]}" "$@"
#
# but POSIX shell has neither arrays nor command substitution, so instead we
# post-process each arg (as a line of input to sed) to backslash-escape any
# character that might be a shell metacharacter, then use eval to reverse
# that process (while maintaining the separation between arguments), and wrap
# the whole thing up as a single "set" statement.
#
# This will of course break if any of these variables contains a newline or
# an unmatched quote.
#

eval "set -- $(
        printf '%s\n' "$DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS $JAVA_OPTS $GRADLE_OPTS" |
        xargs -n1 |
        sed ' s~[^-[:alnum:]+,./:=@_]~\\&~g; ' |
        tr '\n' ' '
    )" '"$@"'

exec "$JAVACMD" "$@"

```

`loader/build.gradle`:

```gradle
// JSA Client `loader` — multi-version reflective adapter + Fabric-recognizable mod jar.
plugins {
    id 'java'
}

java {
    toolchain {
        languageVersion = JavaLanguageVersion.of(17)
    }
    withSourcesJar()
}

tasks.withType(JavaCompile).configureEach {
    options.encoding = 'UTF-8'
    options.release = 17
}

repositories {
    mavenCentral()
    maven { url 'https://maven.fabricmc.net/' }
}

dependencies {
    implementation project(':core')
    compileOnly 'com.google.code.gson:gson:2.10.1'
    // Enough for ClientModInitializer; Fabric API is optional at runtime.
    compileOnly "net.fabricmc:fabric-loader:${project.loader_version}"
}

processResources {
    inputs.property 'version', project.version
    filesMatching('fabric.mod.json') {
        expand 'version': project.version
    }
}

tasks.named('jar', Jar) {
    dependsOn(':core:jar', ':fabric:remapJar')
    // Real ClickGUI screen (intermediary) so the loader jar can open it on Fabric 1.20.1.
    def fabricRemap = project(':fabric').tasks.named('remapJar')
    dependsOn(fabricRemap)
    from(zipTree(fabricRemap.map { it.outputs.files.singleFile })) {
        include 'dev/jsa/client/fabric/JsaClickGuiScreen.class'
        include 'dev/jsa/client/fabric/JsaClickGuiScreen$*.class'
        include 'dev/jsa/client/fabric/WorldEspRenderer.class'
        include 'dev/jsa/client/fabric/WorldEspRenderer$*.class'
        include 'dev/jsa/client/fabric/HostMotion.class'
        include 'dev/jsa/client/fabric/LiveFabricPlatform.class'
        include 'dev/jsa/client/fabric/LiveFabricPlatform$*.class'
        include 'dev/jsa/client/fabric/mixin/*.class'
        include 'dev/jsa/client/fabric/meteor/*.class'
        include 'jsaclient.mixins.json'
        rename 'jsaclient\\.mixins\\.json', 'jsaclient-loader.mixins.json'
    }
    manifest {
        attributes(
            'Main-Class': 'dev.jsa.client.loader.LoaderMain',
            'Implementation-Title': 'JSA Client Loader',
            'Implementation-Version': project.version
        )
    }
    // Bundle core so one loader jar boots the full SP/LAN toolkit.
    from({
        zipTree(project(':core').tasks.named('jar', Jar).get().archiveFile.get().asFile)
    })
    duplicatesStrategy = DuplicatesStrategy.EXCLUDE
    archiveBaseName.set('jsa-loader')
    archiveVersion.set(project.version)
}
```
