`loader/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/loader/FabricLoaderEntrypoint.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.loader;

import dev.jsa.client.core.JsaClientLoader;
import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.gui.ClickGui;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Platform;
import net.fabricmc.api.ClientModInitializer;
import net.fabricmc.loader.api.FabricLoader;

/**
 * Fabric client entry so Modrinth recognizes the loader jar as a mod.
 * Boots the reflective adapter and hooks Fabric API tick/HUD when present.
 */
public final class FabricLoaderEntrypoint implements ClientModInitializer {

    @Override
    public void onInitializeClient() {
        if (FabricLoader.getInstance().isModLoaded("jsaclient")) {
            System.out.println("[JSA Loader] jsaclient is already loaded — skipping second client boot.");
            return;
        }
        System.out.println("[JSA Loader] Fabric entry — booting SP/LAN toolkit…");
        bootPlatform();
        hookFabricApi();
        hookWorldRender();
        System.out.println("[JSA Loader] Modules: " + ModuleManager.get().getAllModules().size()
                + " — Press INSERT (or Right Shift) for ClickGUI.");
    }

    private static Object livePlatform;

    /** Prefer the real Fabric player bridge (remapped). Reflection is only a fallback. */
    private static void bootPlatform() {
        try {
            Class<?> type = Class.forName("dev.jsa.client.fabric.LiveFabricPlatform");
            livePlatform = type.getDeclaredConstructor().newInstance();
            MC.set((Platform) livePlatform);
            JsaClientLoader.boot();
            System.out.println("[JSA Loader] Live Fabric platform — modules talk to the real player.");
            return;
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            System.out.println("[JSA Loader] Live platform missing (" + t + "). Reflective fallback.");
        }
        LoaderMain.boot();
    }

    private static void tickMeteorAddons() {
        try {
            Class.forName("dev.jsa.client.fabric.meteor.MeteorAddonHost")
                    .getMethod("tick").invoke(null);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    private static void pulseLive() {
        if (livePlatform == null) return;
        try {
            livePlatform.getClass().getMethod("pulse").invoke(livePlatform);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    /** World boxes/lines live in the shaded Fabric renderer, not the HUD bridge. */
    private static void hookWorldRender() {
        try {
            Class.forName("dev.jsa.client.fabric.WorldEspRenderer")
                    .getMethod("register")
                    .invoke(null);
            System.out.println("[JSA Loader] World ESP hook registered.");
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            System.out.println("[JSA Loader] World ESP hook failed: " + t);
        }
    }

    private static void hookFabricApi() {
        try {
            Class<?> events = Class.forName(
                    "net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.event.lifecycle.v1.ClientTickEvents");
            Object endTick = events.getField("END_CLIENT_TICK").get(null);
            Class<?> callback = Class.forName(
                    "net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.event.lifecycle.v1.ClientTickEvents$EndTick");

            Object listener = java.lang.reflect.Proxy.newProxyInstance(
                    callback.getClassLoader(),
                    new Class<?>[]{callback},
                    (proxy, method, args) -> {
                        String name = method.getName();
                        if ("equals".equals(name)) return proxy == args[0];
                        if ("hashCode".equals(name)) return System.identityHashCode(proxy);
                        if ("toString".equals(name)) return "JsaLoaderEndTick";
                        // Always tick while in-game; don't rely on remapped player field names.
                        if (args != null && args.length > 0 && isInGame(args[0])) {
                            // Insert / RShift / RCtrl — open a real Screen (no cursor warp)
                            if (guiKeyPressed(args[0])) {
                                if (!guiWasDown) toggleScreen(args[0]);
                                guiWasDown = true;
                            } else {
                                guiWasDown = false;
                            }
                            pulseLive();
                            tickMeteorAddons();
                            ModuleManager.get().tick();
                        }
                        return null;
                    });

            endTick.getClass().getMethod("register", callback).invoke(endTick, listener);
            System.out.println("[JSA Loader] Fabric API tick hook registered.");
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            System.out.println("[JSA Loader] Tick hook failed: " + t);
        }

        try {
            Class<?> hud = Class.forName(
                    "net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.rendering.v1.HudRenderCallback");
            Object event = hud.getField("EVENT").get(null);
            Class<?> cb = Class.forName(
                    "net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.rendering.v1.HudRenderCallback");
            Object listener = java.lang.reflect.Proxy.newProxyInstance(
                    cb.getClassLoader(),
                    new Class<?>[]{cb},
                    (proxy, method, args) -> {
                        String name = method.getName();
                        if ("equals".equals(name)) return proxy == args[0];
                        if ("hashCode".equals(name)) return System.identityHashCode(proxy);
                        if ("toString".equals(name)) return "JsaLoaderHud";
                        if (args != null && args.length > 0) {
                            LoaderHudBridge.render(args[0], args.length > 1 ? args[1] : null);
                        }
                        return null;
                    });
            event.getClass().getMethod("register", cb).invoke(event, listener);
            System.out.println("[JSA Loader] Fabric API HUD hook registered.");
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            System.out.println("[JSA Loader] HUD hook failed: " + t);
        }
    }

    private static boolean guiWasDown;

    private static boolean isInGame(Object client) {
        try {
            for (var f : client.getClass().getFields()) {
                if (f.getType().getName().contains("ClientPlayer")
                        || f.getName().equals("player")
                        || f.getName().contains("player")) {
                    Object v = f.get(client);
                    if (v != null && v.getClass().getName().contains("Player")) return true;
                }
            }
            // Fallback: world field non-null
            for (var f : client.getClass().getFields()) {
                if (f.getName().contains("world") || f.getName().contains("level")) {
                    if (f.get(client) != null) return true;
                }
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        return true; // tick anyway; modules no-op if not ready
    }

    private static boolean guiKeyPressed(Object client) {
        try {
            Object win = client.getClass().getMethod("getWindow").invoke(client);
            long handle = (long) win.getClass().getMethod("getHandle").invoke(win);
            Class<?> glfw = Class.forName("org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW");
            var getKey = glfw.getMethod("glfwGetKey", long.class, int.class);
            int insert = (int) getKey.invoke(null, handle, 260);      // GLFW_KEY_INSERT
            int rShift = (int) getKey.invoke(null, handle, 344);      // GLFW_KEY_RIGHT_SHIFT
            int rCtrl  = (int) getKey.invoke(null, handle, 345);      // GLFW_KEY_RIGHT_CONTROL
            return insert == 1 || rShift == 1 || rCtrl == 1;
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    /** Open or close {@code JsaClickGuiScreen} without yarn names (works on intermediary). */
    private static void toggleScreen(Object client) {
        try {
            Class<?> screenCls = Class.forName("dev.jsa.client.fabric.JsaClickGuiScreen");
            Object current = currentScreen(client);
            if (current != null && screenCls.isInstance(current)) {
                // Fade closed. A second press while it fades reopens.
                if (ClickGui.get().isClosing()) ClickGui.get().beginOpen();
                else ClickGui.get().requestClose();
                return;
            }
            if (current == null) {
                setScreen(client, screenCls.getDeclaredConstructor().newInstance(), screenCls);
            }
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            System.out.println("[JSA Loader] ClickGUI screen failed: " + t);
            ClickGui.get().toggle();
        }
    }

    private static Object currentScreen(Object client) {
        Class<?> c = client.getClass();
        while (c != null) {
            for (var f : c.getDeclaredFields()) {
                String n = f.getName();
                if (n.equals("currentScreen") || n.equals("field_1755")) {
                    try {
                        f.setAccessible(true);
                        return f.get(client);
                    } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
                }
            }
            c = c.getSuperclass();
        }
        return null;
    }

    private static void setScreen(Object client, Object screen, Class<?> screenCls) throws Exception {
        Class<?> param = screen == null ? screenCls.getSuperclass() : screen.getClass().getSuperclass();
        for (String name : new String[]{"setScreen", "method_1507"}) {
            try {
                client.getClass().getMethod(name, param).invoke(client, screen);
                return;
            } catch (NoSuchMethodException ignored) {}
        }
        // Last resort: any single-arg method that accepts the screen type.
        for (var m : client.getClass().getMethods()) {
            if (m.getParameterCount() == 1 && m.getParameterTypes()[0].isAssignableFrom(screenCls)) {
                m.invoke(client, screen);
                return;
            }
        }
        throw new NoSuchMethodException("setScreen");
    }
}

```

`loader/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/loader/LoaderHudBridge.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.loader;

import dev.jsa.client.core.event.RenderEvent;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Entity;

import java.lang.reflect.Method;

/**
 * Reflective HUD sink for Fabric DrawContext — keeps the loader free of a
 * Minecraft compile dependency while still painting ClickGUI / module HUD.
 */
final class LoaderHudBridge {
    private LoaderHudBridge() {}

    static void render(Object drawContext, Object tickDelta) {
        try {
            Object client = Class.forName("net.minecraft.client.MinecraftClient")
                    .getMethod("getInstance").invoke(null);
            if (client == null) return;
            Object player;
            try {
                player = client.getClass().getField("player").get(client);
            } catch (NoSuchFieldException e) {
                player = client.getClass().getMethod("getPlayer").invoke(client);
            }
            if (player == null) return;

            Object textRenderer = client.getClass().getField("textRenderer").get(client);
            Object window = client.getClass().getMethod("getWindow").invoke(client);
            int sw = (int) window.getClass().getMethod("getScaledWidth").invoke(window);
            int sh = (int) window.getClass().getMethod("getScaledHeight").invoke(window);

            Method drawText = findDrawText(drawContext.getClass());
            Method fill = findFill(drawContext.getClass());

            RenderEvent ev = RenderEvent.get();
            ev.setSink(new RenderEvent.RenderSink() {
                @Override public void box(Entity e, int argb) {}
                @Override public void healthBar(Entity e, float fraction) {}
                @Override public void line(Entity e, int argb) {}
                @Override public void text(int x, int y, String s, int argb) {
                    try {
                        if (drawText != null) {
                            drawText.invoke(drawContext, textRenderer, s, x, y, argb, true);
                        }
                    } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
                }
                @Override public void fill(int x, int y, int w, int h, int argb) {
                    try {
                        if (fill != null) fill.invoke(drawContext, x, y, x + w, y + h, argb);
                    } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
                }
                @Override public void outline(int x, int y, int w, int h, int argb) {
                    fill(x, y, w, 1, argb);
                    fill(x, y + h - 1, w, 1, argb);
                    fill(x, y, 1, h, argb);
                    fill(x + w - 1, y, 1, h, argb);
                }
                @Override public int width() { return sw; }
                @Override public int height() { return sh; }
            });
            ev.setWorldPass(false);
            ModuleManager.get().onRender(ev);
            drawWorldTags(drawContext);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            // Best-effort; modules still tick without HUD.
        }
    }

    /** Name tags projected in the world pass. Method name is ours, so it survives remapping. */
    private static void drawWorldTags(Object drawContext) {
        try {
            Class<?> type = Class.forName("dev.jsa.client.fabric.WorldEspRenderer");
            for (Method m : type.getMethods()) {
                if (m.getName().equals("drawTags") && m.getParameterCount() == 1) {
                    m.invoke(null, drawContext);
                    return;
                }
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    private static Method findDrawText(Class<?> ctx) {
        for (Method m : ctx.getMethods()) {
            if (!m.getName().equals("drawText") && !m.getName().equals("method_51433")) continue;
            Class<?>[] p = m.getParameterTypes();
            if (p.length == 6) return m;
        }
        return null;
    }

    private static Method findFill(Class<?> ctx) {
        for (Method m : ctx.getMethods()) {
            if (!m.getName().equals("fill") && !m.getName().equals("method_25294")) continue;
            Class<?>[] p = m.getParameterTypes();
            if (p.length == 5 && p[4] == int.class) return m;
        }
        return null;
    }
}

```

`loader/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/loader/LoaderMain.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.loader;

import dev.jsa.client.core.JsaClientLoader;
import dev.jsa.client.core.MC;
import dev.jsa.client.core.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.jsa.client.core.version.VersionInfo;
import dev.jsa.client.core.version.VersionMatrix;
import dev.jsa.client.core.version.VersionProbe;
import dev.jsa.client.core.version.Versions;
import dev.jsa.client.loader.platform.ReflectivePlatformAdapter;

/**
 * Multi-version entry for Minecraft <b>1.6.4 – 26.3</b>.
 *
 * <pre>
 *   java -Djsa.mc.version=1.12.2 -cp jsa-loader.jar;minecraft.jar;... ^
 *        dev.jsa.client.loader.LoaderMain
 *
 *   java -cp jsa-loader.jar dev.jsa.client.loader.LoaderMain --matrix
 * </pre>
 */
public final class LoaderMain {
    private LoaderMain() {}

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        if (args != null) {
            for (String a : args) {
                if ("--matrix".equals(a) || "--versions".equals(a)) {
                    printMatrix();
                    return;
                }
                if ("--help".equals(a) || "-h".equals(a)) {
                    printHelp();
                    return;
                }
            }
        }

        System.out.println("[JSA Loader] SP/LAN toolkit — versions 1.6.4 .. 26.3");
        boot();
        var v = Versions.get();
        System.out.println("[JSA Loader] Version: " + v);
        System.out.println("[JSA Loader] Era:     " + v.era().label() + " — " + v.era().description());
        System.out.println("[JSA Loader] In range: " + v.inSupportedRange());
        System.out.println("[JSA Loader] Modules: " + ModuleManager.get().getAllModules().size());
        System.out.println("[JSA Loader] Caps:    " + Versions.caps().summary());
        if (!v.inSupportedRange() && v.isKnown()) {
            System.out.println("[JSA Loader] WARNING: version outside 1.6.4–26.3; binding best-effort.");
        }
    }

    public static void boot() {
        VersionInfo info = VersionProbe.probe("loader");
        Versions.install(info);

        ReflectivePlatformAdapter adapter = new ReflectivePlatformAdapter(info);
        MC.set(adapter);
        JsaClientLoader.boot();

        System.out.println("[JSA Loader] Bound adapter=" + adapter.getAdapterId()
                + " mc=" + adapter.getMinecraftVersion()
                + " era=" + info.era().label());
    }

    private static void printMatrix() {
        for (String line : VersionMatrix.smokeReport()) {
            System.out.println(line);
        }
    }

    private static void printHelp() {
        System.out.println("""
            JSA Loader — multi-version SP/open-LAN client (1.6.4 .. 26.3)

            Boot (attach Minecraft jars on the classpath):
              java -Djsa.mc.version=1.20.1 ^
                   -cp jsa-loader.jar;<minecraft jars>;<libraries> ^
                   dev.jsa.client.loader.LoaderMain

            Force version when probe cannot read version.json:
              -Djsa.mc.version=1.6.4
              -Djsa.mc.version=26.3

            Flags:
              --matrix     Print era capability smoke table
              --versions   Same as --matrix
              --help       This text

            Fabric users: install the fabric mod jar for a pinned version
            (default build = 1.20.1). Change gradle.properties to retarget.
            """);
    }
}

```

`loader/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/loader/McClassIndex.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.loader;

import dev.jsa.client.core.version.MinecraftEra;
import dev.jsa.client.core.version.VersionInfo;

/**
 * Era-aware class name index for reflective binding across 1.6.4 – 26.3.
 * Tries names in era-preferred order, then falls back across all known aliases.
 */
public final class McClassIndex {
    private final VersionInfo version;
    private final MinecraftEra era;

    public McClassIndex(VersionInfo version) {
        this.version = version == null ? VersionInfo.unknown("loader") : version;
        this.era = this.version.era();
    }

    public VersionInfo version() { return version; }
    public MinecraftEra era() { return era; }

    public Class<?> minecraftClient() {
        return switch (era) {
            case YARN_1_14, NETHER_1_16, CAVES_1_17, TALL_1_18, WILD_1_19,
                 TRAILS_1_20, TRIALS_1_21, CLASSIC_1_26, CALENDAR_26 -> first(
                    "net.minecraft.client.MinecraftClient",
                    "net.minecraft.client.Minecraft"
            );
            case LEGACY_1_6, RELEASE_1_7, RELEASE_1_8, COMBAT_1_9, FLATTENING_1_13 -> first(
                    "net.minecraft.client.Minecraft",
                    "net.minecraft.client.MinecraftClient"
            );
            default -> first(
                    "net.minecraft.client.MinecraftClient",
                    "net.minecraft.client.Minecraft"
            );
        };
    }

    public Class<?> playerEntity() {
        return switch (era) {
            case LEGACY_1_6 -> first(
                    "net.minecraft.client.entity.EntityClientPlayerMP",
                    "net.minecraft.client.entity.EntityPlayerSP",
                    "net.minecraft.entity.player.EntityPlayer"
            );
            case RELEASE_1_7, RELEASE_1_8, COMBAT_1_9, FLATTENING_1_13 -> first(
                    "net.minecraft.client.entity.EntityPlayerSP",
                    "net.minecraft.client.entity.player.ClientPlayerEntity",
                    "net.minecraft.client.network.ClientPlayerEntity"
            );
            default -> first(
                    "net.minecraft.client.network.ClientPlayerEntity",
                    "net.minecraft.client.entity.player.ClientPlayerEntity",
                    "net.minecraft.client.entity.EntityPlayerSP",
                    "net.minecraft.entity.player.PlayerEntity",
                    "net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player"
            );
        };
    }

    public Class<?> world() {
        return switch (era) {
            case CAVES_1_17, TALL_1_18, WILD_1_19, TRAILS_1_20, TRIALS_1_21,
                 CLASSIC_1_26, CALENDAR_26 -> first(
                    "net.minecraft.client.multiplayer.ClientLevel",
                    "net.minecraft.client.world.ClientWorld",
                    "net.minecraft.world.level.Level"
            );
            case YARN_1_14, NETHER_1_16 -> first(
                    "net.minecraft.client.world.ClientWorld",
                    "net.minecraft.client.multiplayer.ClientWorld",
                    "net.minecraft.world.World"
            );
            default -> first(
                    "net.minecraft.client.multiplayer.WorldClient",
                    "net.minecraft.client.world.ClientWorld",
                    "net.minecraft.world.World",
                    "net.minecraft.world.level.Level"
            );
        };
    }

    public Class<?> sharedConstants() {
        return first("net.minecraft.SharedConstants");
    }

    public String[] playerGetXNames() {
        if (era.ordinal() >= MinecraftEra.YARN_1_14.ordinal() && era != MinecraftEra.UNKNOWN) {
            return new String[]{"getX", "getPosX", "posX"};
        }
        return new String[]{"posX", "getX", "field_70165_t"};
    }

    public String[] playerGetYNames() {
        if (era.ordinal() >= MinecraftEra.YARN_1_14.ordinal() && era != MinecraftEra.UNKNOWN) {
            return new String[]{"getY", "getPosY", "posY"};
        }
        return new String[]{"posY", "getY", "field_70163_u"};
    }

    public String[] playerGetZNames() {
        if (era.ordinal() >= MinecraftEra.YARN_1_14.ordinal() && era != MinecraftEra.UNKNOWN) {
            return new String[]{"getZ", "getPosZ", "posZ"};
        }
        return new String[]{"posZ", "getZ", "field_70161_v"};
    }

    public String[] instanceMethodNames() {
        return new String[]{"getInstance", "getMinecraft", "func_71410_x"};
    }

    public String[] playerFieldNames() {
        return switch (era) {
            case LEGACY_1_6, RELEASE_1_7, RELEASE_1_8, COMBAT_1_9, FLATTENING_1_13 ->
                    new String[]{"thePlayer", "player", "field_71439_g"};
            default ->
                    new String[]{"player", "thePlayer", "field_71439_g"};
        };
    }

    public String[] worldFieldNames() {
        return switch (era) {
            case LEGACY_1_6, RELEASE_1_7, RELEASE_1_8, COMBAT_1_9, FLATTENING_1_13 ->
                    new String[]{"theWorld", "world", "field_71441_e"};
            default ->
                    new String[]{"world", "level", "theWorld", "field_71441_e"};
        };
    }

    public boolean modernMappings() {
        return version.atLeast(1, 14, 0) || classExists("net.minecraft.client.MinecraftClient");
    }

    public boolean usesWorldLevelPackages() {
        return version.atLeast(1, 17, 0) || classExists("net.minecraft.world.level.block.Block");
    }

    private static Class<?> first(String... names) {
        for (String n : names) {
            try {
                return Class.forName(n, false, McClassIndex.class.getClassLoader());
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        }
        return null;
    }

    private static boolean classExists(String name) {
        try {
            Class.forName(name, false, McClassIndex.class.getClassLoader());
            return true;
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return false;
        }
    }
}

```

`loader/src/main/java/dev/jsa/client/loader/platform/ReflectivePlatformAdapter.java`:

```java
package dev.jsa.client.loader.platform;

import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Entity;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Item;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Packet;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.Platform;
import dev.jsa.client.core.platform.World;
import dev.jsa.client.core.version.VersionInfo;
import dev.jsa.client.loader.McClassIndex;

import java.lang.reflect.Method;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Version-agnostic {@link Platform} that talks to Minecraft through reflection.
 * Works without compiling against a specific MC jar — covers 1.6.4 through 26.3
 * via {@link McClassIndex} era maps when the client is on the classpath.
 *
 * <p>When live MC classes are missing (unit tests / headless), falls back to an
 * internal mock so the SP/LAN module bundle still boots.</p>
 */
public class ReflectivePlatformAdapter implements Platform {

    private final VersionInfo version;
    private final McClassIndex index;
    private final FallbackState fallback = new FallbackState();

    private Object mcInstance;
    private boolean resolved;

    public ReflectivePlatformAdapter(VersionInfo version) {
        this.version = version == null ? VersionInfo.unknown("loader") : version;
        this.index = new McClassIndex(this.version);
        resolveMinecraft();
    }

    private void resolveMinecraft() {
        try {
            Class<?> mc = index.minecraftClient();
            if (mc == null) return;
            for (String name : index.instanceMethodNames()) {
                try {
                    Method m = mc.getMethod(name);
                    mcInstance = m.invoke(null);
                    if (mcInstance != null) {
                        resolved = true;
                        return;
                    }
                } catch (ReflectiveOperationException ignored) {}
            }
            for (String field : new String[]{"instance", "theMinecraft", "field_71432_P"}) {
                try {
                    var f = mc.getDeclaredField(field);
                    f.setAccessible(true);
                    mcInstance = f.get(null);
                    if (mcInstance != null) {
                        resolved = true;
                        return;
                    }
                } catch (ReflectiveOperationException ignored) {}
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
    }

    public McClassIndex classIndex() { return index; }

    @Override public String getMinecraftVersion() { return version.raw(); }
    @Override public String getAdapterId() { return "loader"; }

    @Override public boolean inGame() {
        if (!resolved) return fallback.inGame;
        Object player = invoke(mcInstance, index.playerFieldNames());
        return player != null;
    }

    @Override public boolean onServer() {
        if (!resolved) return fallback.onServer;
        Object world = invoke(mcInstance, index.worldFieldNames());
        return world != null && !isIntegrated(world);
    }

    @Override public String getServerName() {
        if (!resolved) return fallback.serverName;
        return onServer() ? "Multiplayer / LAN" : "Singleplayer";
    }

    @Override public int getPing() { return fallback.ping; }

    @Override public Entity getLocalPlayer() {
        return resolved ? new ReflectiveEntity(invoke(mcInstance, index.playerFieldNames())) : fallback.player;
    }

    @Override public World getWorld() {
        return resolved ? new ReflectiveWorld(invoke(mcInstance, index.worldFieldNames())) : fallback.world;
    }

    @Override public int getTick() { return fallback.tick++; }
    @Override public long nanoTime() { return System.nanoTime(); }
    @Override public float getPartialTick() { return 0f; }

    @Override public double getX() { return num(getLocalPlayer(), "getX", "posX"); }
    @Override public double getY() { return num(getLocalPlayer(), "getY", "posY"); }
    @Override public double getZ() { return num(getLocalPlayer(), "getZ", "posZ"); }

    @Override public void setPos(double x, double y, double z) {
        Entity e = getLocalPlayer();
        if (e instanceof ReflectiveEntity re) re.setPos(x, y, z);
        else if (e instanceof FallbackEntity fe) fe.setPos(x, y, z);
    }

    @Override public double getMotionX() { return num(getLocalPlayer(), "getVelocityX", "motionX"); }
    @Override public double getMotionY() { return num(getLocalPlayer(), "getVelocityY", "motionY"); }
    @Override public double getMotionZ() { return num(getLocalPlayer(), "getVelocityZ", "motionZ"); }

    @Override public void setMotion(double x, double y, double z) { setVelocity(x, y, z); }
    @Override public void addMotion(double x, double y, double z) {
        setVelocity(getMotionX() + x, getMotionY() + y, getMotionZ() + z);
    }

    @Override public boolean isOnGround() { return bool(getLocalPlayer(), "isOnGround", "onGround"); }
    @Override public void setOnGround(boolean onGround) { /* best-effort */ }

    @Override public float getYaw() { return (float) num(getLocalPlayer(), "getYaw", "rotationYaw"); }
    @Override public float getPitch() { return (float) num(getLocalPlayer(), "getPitch", "rotationPitch"); }
    @Override public void setRotation(float yaw, float pitch) {
        Entity e = getLocalPlayer();
        if (e instanceof ReflectiveEntity re) re.setRotation(yaw, pitch);
        else if (e instanceof FallbackEntity fe) { fe.yaw = yaw; fe.pitch = pitch; }
    }

    @Override public boolean isSprinting() { return bool(getLocalPlayer(), "isSprinting"); }

    @Override public void setSneaking(boolean sneaking) { /* adapter */ }

    @Override public boolean isOnLadder() { return false; }

    }
    @Override public List<Entity> getEntitiesInRadius(double radius) {

    @Override public float getHealth() {

    @Override public boolean isKeyDown(char key) {
        Integer code = switch (Character.toLowerCase(key)) {
            case 'w' -> 87; case 'a' -> 65; case 's' -> 83; case 'd' -> 68;
            case ' ' -> 32; case 'c' -> 67; case 'r' -> 82;
            default -> null;
        };
        return code != null && glfwKeyDown(code);
    }
    @Override public boolean isShiftDown() {
        return glfwKeyDown(340) || glfwKeyDown(344) || isSneaking(); // L/R shift
    }
    @Override public boolean isGuiToggleKeyDown() {
        return glfwKeyDown(260) || glfwKeyDown(344) || glfwKeyDown(345); // Insert / RShift / RCtrl
    }
    @Override public int getMouseX() {
        try {
            Object mc = mcInstance != null ? mcInstance : Class.forName("net.minecraft.client.MinecraftClient")
                    .getMethod("getInstance").invoke(null);
            if (mc == null) return 0;
            Object mouse = mc.getClass().getField("mouse").get(mc);
            Object win = mc.getClass().getMethod("getWindow").invoke(mc);
            double mx = ((Number) mouse.getClass().getMethod("getX").invoke(mouse)).doubleValue();
            int ww = (int) win.getClass().getMethod("getWidth").invoke(win);
            int sw = (int) win.getClass().getMethod("getScaledWidth").invoke(win);
            return (int) (mx * sw / Math.max(1, ww));
        } catch (Throwable t) { return 0; }
    }
    @Override public int getMouseY() {
        try {
            Object mc = mcInstance != null ? mcInstance : Class.forName("net.minecraft.client.MinecraftClient")
                    .getMethod("getInstance").invoke(null);
            if (mc == null) return 0;
            Object mouse = mc.getClass().getField("mouse").get(mc);
            Object win = mc.getClass().getMethod("getWindow").invoke(mc);
            double my = ((Number) mouse.getClass().getMethod("getY").invoke(mouse)).doubleValue();
            int wh = (int) win.getClass().getMethod("getHeight").invoke(win);
            int sh = (int) win.getClass().getMethod("getScaledHeight").invoke(win);
            return (int) (my * sh / Math.max(1, wh));
        } catch (Throwable t) { return 0; }
    }
    @Override public boolean isMouseButtonDown(int button) {
        try {
            Object mc = mcInstance != null ? mcInstance : Class.forName("net.minecraft.client.MinecraftClient")
                    .getMethod("getInstance").invoke(null);
            Object win = mc.getClass().getMethod("getWindow").invoke(mc);
            long handle = (long) win.getClass().getMethod("getHandle").invoke(win);
            Class<?> glfw = Class.forName("org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW");
            int glfwBtn = button == 1 ? 1 : button == 2 ? 2 : 0;
            int state = (int) glfw.getMethod("glfwGetMouseButton", long.class, int.class)
                    .invoke(null, handle, glfwBtn);
            return state == 1;
        } catch (Throwable t) { return false; }
    }
    @Override public boolean isAttacking() { return false; }

    private boolean glfwKeyDown(int keyCode) {
        try {
            Object mc = mcInstance != null ? mcInstance : Class.forName("net.minecraft.client.MinecraftClient")
                    .getMethod("getInstance").invoke(null);
            if (mc == null) return false;
            Object win = mc.getClass().getMethod("getWindow").invoke(mc);
            long handle = (long) win.getClass().getMethod("getHandle").invoke(win);
            Class<?> glfw = Class.forName("org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW");
            int state = (int) glfw.getMethod("glfwGetKey", long.class, int.class)
                    .invoke(null, handle, keyCode);
            return state == 1;
        } catch (Throwable t) { return false; }
    }
    @Override public boolean isEating() { return fallback.eating; }
    @Override public boolean isBlocking() { return fallback.blocking; }
    @Override public boolean isGliding() { return false; }
    @Override public void setNoClip(boolean enabled) { fallback.noClip = enabled; }

    @Override public int getHunger() { return fallback.hunger; }
    @Override public void setHunger(int level) { fallback.hunger = level; }
    @Override public int getFps() {
        // Prefer live Minecraft counter; fall back to our render sampler.
        try {
            Object mc = mcInstance != null ? mcInstance
                    : Class.forName("net.minecraft.client.MinecraftClient").getMethod("getInstance").invoke(null);
            if (mc != null) {
                for (String name : new String[]{"getCurrentFps", "getFps"}) {
                    try {
                        Object v = mc.getClass().getMethod(name).invoke(mc);
                        if (v instanceof Number n) return n.intValue();
                    } catch (ReflectiveOperationException ignored) {}
                }
                for (String name : new String[]{"currentFps", "fps", "fpsCounter", "field_1738"}) {
                    try {
                        var f = mc.getClass().getDeclaredField(name);
                        f.setAccessible(true);
                        Object v = f.get(null); // often static on MinecraftClient
                        if (v instanceof Number n) return n.intValue();
                    } catch (ReflectiveOperationException ignored) {}
                    try {
                        var f = mc.getClass().getDeclaredField(name);
                        f.setAccessible(true);
                        Object v = f.get(mc);
                        if (v instanceof Number n) return n.intValue();
                    } catch (ReflectiveOperationException ignored) {}
                }
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        return dev.jsa.client.core.util.FpsCounter.getInstance().get();
    }

    @Override public Item getHotbarItem(int slot) { return getItem(slot); }

    // -- prank defaults with fallback state ---------------------------------
    @Override public void showOverlay(String title, String subtitle, int ticks) {
        actionbar(title + (subtitle == null ? "" : " | " + subtitle));
    }
    @Override public void pushFakeChat(String line) { chat("[fake] " + line); }
    @Override public void setSpoofedInventoryMode(String mode) { fallback.invMode = mode; }
    @Override public void saveClientPos() {

        fallback.hasSaved = true;
    }
    @Override public void blinkClientPos(double x, double y, double z) {
        if (!fallback.hasSaved) saveClientPos();
        setPos(x, y, z);
    }
    @Override public void restoreClientPos() {
        if (fallback.hasSaved) setPos(fallback.savedX, fallback.savedY, fallback.savedZ);
    }
    @Override public void setCameraShake(float intensity) { fallback.shake = intensity; }
    @Override public void setScreenFlip(boolean flip) { fallback.flip = flip; }
    @Override public void setDerp(boolean on) { fallback.derp = on; }

    // -- reflection helpers -------------------------------------------------
    private static Object invoke(Object target, String... names) {
        if (target == null) return null;
        Class<?> c = target.getClass();
        for (String name : names) {
            try {
                Method m = c.getMethod(name);
                return m.invoke(target);
            } catch (ReflectiveOperationException ignored) {}
            try {
                var f = c.getField(name);
                return f.get(target);
            } catch (ReflectiveOperationException ignored) {}
            try {
                var f = c.getDeclaredField(name);
                f.setAccessible(true);
                return f.get(target);
            } catch (ReflectiveOperationException ignored) {}
        }
        return null;
    }

    private static double num(Entity e, String... names) {
        if (e instanceof ReflectiveEntity re) return re.num(names);
        if (e instanceof FallbackEntity fe) {
            if (names[0].contains("X") && !names[0].contains("Velocity") && !names[0].contains("motion")) return fe.x;
            if (names[0].contains("Y") && !names[0].contains("Velocity") && !names[0].contains("motion")) return fe.y;
            if (names[0].contains("Z") && !names[0].contains("Velocity") && !names[0].contains("motion")) return fe.z;
            if (names[0].toLowerCase().contains("yaw")) return fe.yaw;
            if (names[0].toLowerCase().contains("pitch")) return fe.pitch;
            return 0;
        }
        return 0;
    }

    private static boolean bool(Entity e, String... names) {
        if (e instanceof ReflectiveEntity re) return re.bool(names);
        if (e instanceof FallbackEntity fe) return fe.onGround;
        return false;
    }

    private static boolean isIntegrated(Object world) {
        try {
            Method m = world.getClass().getMethod("isClient");
            Object v = m.invoke(world);
            return v instanceof Boolean && (Boolean) v;
        } catch (ReflectiveOperationException e) {
            return true;
        }
    }

    // -- nested types -------------------------------------------------------
    private static final class ReflectiveEntity implements Entity {
        private final Object handle;
        ReflectiveEntity(Object handle) { this.handle = handle; }

        @Override public int getId() { return (int) Math.round(num("getId", "entityId")); }
        @Override public String getName() {
            Object n = ReflectivePlatformAdapter.invoke(handle, "getName", "getDisplayName");
            return n == null ? "Entity" : n.toString();
        }
        @Override public double getX() { return num("getX", "posX"); }
        @Override public double getY() { return num("getY", "posY"); }
        @Override public double getZ() { return num("getZ", "posZ"); }
        @Override public float getHealth() { return (float) num("getHealth"); }
        @Override public float getMaxHealth() { return 20f; }
        @Override public float getHurtTime() { return 0; }
        @Override public int getArmorValue() { return 0; }
        @Override public boolean isPlayer() { return true; }
        @Override public boolean isMob() { return false; }
        @Override public boolean isArmorStand() { return false; }
        @Override public boolean isBlocking() { return false; }
        @Override public boolean isDead() { return bool("isDead", "removed"); }
        @Override public boolean isSneaking() { return bool("isSneaking"); }
        @Override public boolean isSprinting() { return bool("isSprinting"); }
        @Override public double getMotionX() { return num("getVelocityX", "motionX"); }
        @Override public double getMotionY() { return num("getVelocityY", "motionY"); }
        @Override public double getMotionZ() { return num("getVelocityZ", "motionZ"); }

        void setPos(double x, double y, double z) {
            tryInvoke("setPosition", new Class[]{double.class, double.class, double.class}, x, y, z);
            tryInvoke("setPos", new Class[]{double.class, double.class, double.class}, x, y, z);
        }
        void setVelocity(double x, double y, double z) {
            tryInvoke("setVelocity", new Class[]{double.class, double.class, double.class}, x, y, z);
        }
        void setRotation(float yaw, float pitch) {
            tryInvoke("setYaw", new Class[]{float.class}, yaw);
            tryInvoke("setPitch", new Class[]{float.class}, pitch);
        }

        double num(String... names) {
            Object v = ReflectivePlatformAdapter.invoke(handle, names);
            return v instanceof Number n ? n.doubleValue() : 0;
        }
        boolean bool(String... names) {
            Object v = ReflectivePlatformAdapter.invoke(handle, names);
            return v instanceof Boolean b && b;
        }
        private void tryInvoke(String name, Class<?>[] types, Object... args) {
            if (handle == null) return;
            try {
                Method m = handle.getClass().getMethod(name, types);
                m.invoke(handle, args);
            } catch (ReflectiveOperationException ignored) {}
        }
    }

    private static final class ReflectiveWorld implements World {
        private final Object handle;
        ReflectiveWorld(Object handle) { this.handle = handle; }
        @Override public String getName() { return "world"; }
        @Override public List<Entity> getEntities() { return List.of(); }
    }

    private static final class FallbackState {
        boolean inGame = true;
        boolean onServer = false;
        String serverName = "Loader Fallback";
        int ping = 0;
        int tick = 0;
        final FallbackEntity player = new FallbackEntity();
        final FallbackWorld world = new FallbackWorld();
        final Map<Integer, Item> items = new HashMap<>();
        int heldSlot = 0;
        boolean inventoryOpen;
        boolean eating;
        boolean blocking;
        boolean noClip;
        float attackCooldown = 1f;
        float timer = 1f;
        int hunger = 20;
        int renderDistance = 12;
        float fov = 70f;
        float gamma = 1f;
        boolean nightVision;
        String displayName = "Player";
        double spoofedHp = -1;
        boolean fakeOffhand;
        float stepHeight = 0.6f;
        int renderPhase;
        String invMode = "off";
        double savedX, savedY, savedZ;
        boolean hasSaved;
        float shake;
        boolean flip;
        boolean derp;
    }

    private static final class FallbackEntity implements Entity {
        double x, y = 64, z;
        double vx, vy, vz;
        float yaw, pitch;
        boolean onGround = true;
        void setPos(double x, double y, double z) { this.x = x; this.y = y; this.z = z; }
        void setMotion(double x, double y, double z) { this.vx = x; this.vy = y; this.vz = z; }
        @Override public int getId() { return 0; }
        @Override public String getName() { return "Player"; }
        @Override public double getX() { return x; }
        @Override public double getY() { return y; }
        @Override public double getZ() { return z; }
        @Override public float getHealth() { return 20; }
        @Override public float getMaxHealth() { return 20; }
        @Override public float getHurtTime() { return 0; }
        @Override public int getArmorValue() { return 0; }
        @Override public boolean isPlayer() { return true; }
        @Override public boolean isMob() { return false; }
        @Override public boolean isArmorStand() { return false; }
        @Override public boolean isBlocking() { return false; }
        @Override public boolean isDead() { return false; }
        @Override public boolean isSneaking() { return false; }
        @Override public boolean isSprinting() { return false; }
        @Override public double getMotionX() { return vx; }
        @Override public double getMotionY() { return vy; }
        @Override public double getMotionZ() { return vz; }
    }

    private static final class FallbackWorld implements World {
        @Override public String getName() { return "Overworld"; }
        @Override public List<Entity> getEntities() { return List.of(); }
    }
}
```
